# Astera × RTM Parity Tracker

> Single-page master tracker for "what RTM has" × "what Astera has built" ×
> "which Wave delivers the rest" × "priority". Updated per wave; living doc.

**Baseline**: Wave 5a (Combat depth) land — SkillCast hollow gap 解消 + Armor + DamageRule pipeline + rich damage events + ADR-0030 Bukkit hijack 方針コミット。
**RTM reference**: [ADR-0029 RTM Empirical Catalog](adr/0029-rtm-empirical-catalog.md) +
[architecture/rtm-divergence.md](architecture/rtm-divergence.md).
**Roadmap structure**: [ADR-0028 Roadmap Realignment](adr/0028-roadmap-realignment-paper-as-edge.md).
**Implementation order (master)**: [IMPLEMENTATION-ROADMAP.md](IMPLEMENTATION-ROADMAP.md) —
12 tier leverage-ordered, phase-by-phase, with cumulative parity milestones
and parallelization windows.

> **How to read**: this tracker says **what** is left; IMPLEMENTATION-ROADMAP
> says **in what order**. Use the master roadmap to decide which wave to
> trigger next; use this tracker to verify "did the wave land everything in
> its column".

## Wave 5a Phase A prep — Tier 0 foundational primitives

The next session (Wave 5a Combat depth) folds **Tier 0 foundational primitives**
into its Phase A (me solo, ~40min before workers spawn):

- `Vec3` / `MutableVec3` math operators: `normalize` / `dot` / `cross` /
  `length` / `distance` (knockback formula needs these)
- `IMcEvent` 6 new variants: `PlayerJoined`, `PlayerQuit`, `EntityDamaged`,
  `EntityDied`, `BlockPlaced`, `BlockBroken` (consumed by Wave 5a damage
  attribution + Wave 5c game-mode listeners + Wave 5d block tracking)

## Wave 5f Phase A prep — CEL library dependency

Wave 5f introduces CEL (Common Expression Language) for skill extensibility.
Phase A decides between:

1. **JCEL** (org.projectnessie:cel-jackson + cel-tools) — mature, JVM-friendly
2. **proxima JCEL** — alternate
3. **Self-built mini-parser** — domain-fit DSL, no upstream dep risk

Decision recorded in ADR-0035. Library dep added to `gradle/libs.versions.toml`
in Wave 5f Phase A.

## Wave 6 Phase A prep — Lettuce + HTTP client

Wave 6b extends `IBroadcaster` to real Redis + Discord webhook impls. Phase A
adds to `gradle/libs.versions.toml`:

- **Lettuce** (Redis client) — for pub/sub fan-out
- **okhttp3** or `java.net.http.HttpClient` (built-in) — for Discord webhook POST

## Legend

- **状態**:
  - ✅ **full** — domain + application + persistence + (if applicable) Paper impl all done
  - 🟨 **partial** — some layer done, some missing
  - 🔌 **port-only** — interface declared, in-memory or Fake impl exists, **no Paper impl yet**
  - ❌ **nothing** — not in the codebase
- **優先度**:
  - **P0** — critical for Phase 1 PoC (1 weapon fires end-to-end)
  - **P1** — required for Phase 3 Siege Warfare gameplay
  - **P2** — Phase 4-5 economic / social enrichment
  - **P3** — Phase 6-7 long-tail (Gen AI, certification, etc.)
- **Wave 割当**:
  - **(済)** = already landed (Wave 1-4.5)
  - **5a** = Combat depth (skill dispatch + Armor + DamageRule depth)
  - **5b** = HUD ports (BossBar / Scoreboard / TabList / ActionBar / Title)
  - **5c** = Game mode framework + 6 game modes + Star Generator + Anomaly
  - **5d** = Stage geometry + Block tracking + Barrier + Schematic
  - **5e** = Session + Social + Tutorial + EnderChest + Reward bridge
  - **5f** = Marketplace + JP currency compliance + CEL expression
  - **5v** = Match recording capture (event log + key frame; playback Phase 4)
  - **6** = Paper Sweep (all Bukkit impls at once)
  - **Phase 4+** = post-Siege roadmap

**Wave 5k summary**: 4 new ports (3 mutation gateways + resource pool service)
+ 1 new scheduler port + 4 new domain types + 12 new domain events. All Konsist
green; 2 new rules added (baseline 30 → 32). Parity gauge **same ✅ count**
(catalog +9 entries, including 5 ✅ and 1 partial under this new category).

## Category 8c: Anti-cheat + Anti-AFK (Wave 5q, ADR-0083)

Lightweight in-process validators + external anti-cheat plugin bridge +
per-player AFK detection. Wave 5q dropped the original anti-grief rollback
scope (covered by Wave 5n region permissions + Wave 5w restic backups) and
added anti-AFK detection so match plumbing has a tick-event source for the
leaver-penalty path.

| RTM 機能 | Astera 現状 | Wave 割当 | 優先度 | 根拠 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `IMovementValidator` + `MovementValidation` sealed (`Ok / Suspicious / Reject`) + `MovementSuspicion` sealed (`HighSpeed / VerticalAnomaly / WallClipping`) + `MovementRejection` sealed (`Teleport / NoClip`) | ✅ Wave 5q (port + `DefaultMovementValidator` O(1) hard-cap impl) | (済 Wave 5q) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/anticheat/MovementValidation.kt,domain/anticheat/MovementSuspicion.kt,domain/anticheat/MovementRejection.kt,application/port/outbound/anticheat/IMovementValidator.kt,application/service/anticheat/DefaultMovementValidator.kt}`; ADR-0083 §α |
| `IDamageRateValidator` + `DamageRateValidation` sealed (`Ok / TooFast`) | ✅ Wave 5q (port + `SlidingWindowDamageRateValidator`); Wave 5a damage pipeline calls `record()` before `validate()` on next hit | (済 Wave 5q) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../{application/port/outbound/anticheat/IDamageRateValidator.kt,application/service/anticheat/SlidingWindowDamageRateValidator.kt}`; ADR-0083 §α |
| `IInteractValidator` + `InteractValidation` sealed (`Ok / RateExceeded`) + `InteractAction` sealed (`ContainerOpen / BlockClick / ItemUse`) | ✅ Wave 5q (port + `SlidingWindowInteractValidator` per `(playerId, actionKind)` bucket) | (済 Wave 5q) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/anticheat/InteractAction.kt,domain/anticheat/InteractValidation.kt,application/port/outbound/anticheat/IInteractValidator.kt,application/service/anticheat/SlidingWindowInteractValidator.kt}`; ADR-0083 §α |
| `IExternalAnticheatBridge` (Astera ↔ external plugin) + `AnticheatSuspicion` sealed (`Movement / DamageRate / Interact / CustomFlag`) + `AnticheatAction` sealed (`Kick / Ban / Notify / Quiet`) | ✅ Wave 5q (port + `NoopExternalAnticheatBridge` default; Spartan / Negativity / Vulcan adapter at **Wave 6b** once **Wave 8f** picks the plugin) | **Wave 5q** (port + Noop) + **Wave 6b** (Paper adapter) + **Wave 8f** (plugin choice ADR) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/anticheat/AnticheatSuspicion.kt,domain/anticheat/AnticheatAction.kt,application/port/outbound/anticheat/IExternalAnticheatBridge.kt,application/service/anticheat/NoopExternalAnticheatBridge.kt}`; ADR-0083 §β |
| `AnticheatOrchestrator` (validators → bridge → permission check → events) + `AnticheatActionPolicy` fun interface | ✅ Wave 5q (orchestrator + `DefaultAnticheatActionPolicy` severity-based decision tree); admins / GMs with `astera.anticheat.bypass` skip both flag and action | (済 Wave 5q) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../application/service/anticheat/AnticheatOrchestrator.kt`; ADR-0083 §β + Wave 5t (ADR-0086) bypass permission |
| `IAfkDetector` + `ActivityKind` sealed (`Movement / Click / Chat / SkillCast`) + `AfkStatus` sealed (`Active / Warning(secondsBeforeKick) / Afk(sinceTick)`) + `AfkRecord` | ✅ Wave 5q (port + `InMemoryAfkDetector` warning=30s / kick=2min defaults; tick-agnostic via `tickProvider`) | (済 Wave 5q) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/afk/ActivityKind.kt,domain/afk/AfkStatus.kt,domain/afk/AfkRecord.kt,application/port/outbound/afk/IAfkDetector.kt,application/service/afk/InMemoryAfkDetector.kt}`; ADR-0083 §γ |
| `AfkSweepScheduler` (periodic sweeper, edge-triggered events, 1s sweep default) | ✅ Wave 5q (`IScheduler.runRepeatingTicks` + per-player de-dup bucket) | (済 Wave 5q) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../application/service/afk/AfkSweepScheduler.kt`; ADR-0083 §γ |
| 4 domain events: `AnticheatFlagRaised` / `AnticheatActionTaken` / `PlayerAfkWarned` / `PlayerAfkKicked` | ✅ Wave 5q | (済 Wave 5q) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../domain/event/{AnticheatFlagRaised,AnticheatActionTaken,PlayerAfkWarned,PlayerAfkKicked}.kt`; ADR-0083 |
| Match queue / leaver-penalty hookup on `PlayerAfkKicked` | ❌ deferred to **Wave 5w** (`MatchFoundUseCase` already plans `IPlayerAfkKicked` subscriber for queue-cooldown) | **Wave 5w** | P1 | RTM gap; ADR-0083 §γ Decision |
| Paper-side activity listeners (PlayerMoveEvent / Click / AsyncChat / SkillCastRequested) feeding `IAfkDetector.recordActivity` | ❌ deferred to **Wave 6b** (Paper adapter layer) | **Wave 6b** | P1 | ADR-0083 §γ Consequences |
| Paper-side hookup `IMovementValidator` → `PlayerMoveEvent` cancel + `IDamageRateValidator` → Wave 5a `ApplyDamage` pipeline + `IInteractValidator` → click listener | 🟨 `IMovementValidator` is hooked via packet bridge in **Wave 5y** (`MovementValidatorPacketBridge` → `PacketKind.MovementServerbound`), supersedes `PlayerMoveEvent` cancel path; damage-rate + interact listeners remain **Wave 6b** | **Wave 5y** (movement) / **Wave 6b** (damage / interact) | P1 | ADR-0083 §β Decision (movement clause superseded by ADR-0090) |

**Wave 5q summary**: 3 new ports (validator triad) + 1 bridge port + 1
orchestrator + 1 AFK port + 1 AFK sweeper + 4 new domain events. 2 new
Konsist rules (Anti-cheat domain Bukkit isolation + `IAfkDetector`
application-layer Bukkit isolation). Catalog **+11 entries**, ✅ +9
(orchestrator wires through `IPermissionService` for Wave 5t-style
`astera.anticheat.bypass` escape hatch). Anti-grief rollback ❌
*intentionally not delivered*: covered by Wave 5n region permissions
(ADR-0081) + Wave 5w restic backups (ADR-0089) instead.

## Category 8d: Packet system + per-viewer visibility (Wave 5y, ADR-0090)

Type-erased packet interception port (PacketEvents soft-dep behind the
Wave 6b adapter) + per-viewer entity / player visibility gateway + Wave 5q
anti-cheat integration (movement validator now sees the raw inbound packet
before Paper applies the position). Closes the "PacketEvents adoption
deferred to Phase 3+" clause that ADR-0015 had reserved for a future ADR.

| RTM 機能 | Astera 現状 | Wave 割当 | 優先度 | 根拠 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `IPacketInterceptor` outbound port + `PacketKind` sealed (`MovementClientbound / MovementServerbound / DamageAnimation / ChatClientbound / EntitySpawn / EntityDestroy`) + `PacketEnvelope(payloadView: Map<String, Any>)` + `PacketDecision` sealed (`Pass / Cancel / Replace`) | ✅ Wave 5y (port + `InMemoryPacketInterceptor` registration-order + Cancel short-circuit + Replace payload merge) | (済 Wave 5y) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/packet/{PacketKind,PacketEnvelope,PacketDecision,InterceptorSubscription}.kt,application/port/outbound/packet/IPacketInterceptor.kt,application/service/packet/InMemoryPacketInterceptor.kt}`; ADR-0090 §Decision-2 |
| `IPlayerVisibilityGateway` outbound port + `VisibilityError` sealed (`UnknownViewer / UnknownTarget / SameSelfHide / BackendError(PacketBackendError)`) + `PacketBackendError` sealed (`PacketEventsUnavailable / SerializationFailure`) | ✅ Wave 5y (port + `InMemoryPlayerVisibilityGateway` per-viewer hide index + `EntityHiddenFromViewer` / `EntityShownToViewer` events on first hide / last show) | (済 Wave 5y) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../{domain/visibility/{VisibilityError,PacketBackendError}.kt,application/port/outbound/visibility/IPlayerVisibilityGateway.kt,application/service/visibility/InMemoryPlayerVisibilityGateway.kt}`; ADR-0090 §Decision-2,5 |
| `MovementValidatorPacketBridge` (Wave 5q `IMovementValidator` → `PacketKind.MovementServerbound` listener, returns `PacketDecision.Cancel` on `MovementValidation.Reject`, reports flag via existing `AnticheatOrchestrator`) | ✅ Wave 5y (bridge + last-known-position cache + rollback-on-reject + bypass-aware via orchestrator); seed packet does not validate (insufficient context) | (済 Wave 5y) | P0 | `plugin/core/.../application/service/anticheat/MovementValidatorPacketBridge.kt`; ADR-0090 §Decision-6 |
| 2 new domain events: `EntityHiddenFromViewer` / `EntityShownToViewer` (per-viewer visibility transitions) | ✅ Wave 5y (published only on the first hide and the last show; idempotent re-hide / no-op show do not emit) | (済 Wave 5y) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../domain/event/{EntityHiddenFromViewer,EntityShownToViewer}.kt`; ADR-0090 |
| Wave 6b PacketEvents-backed `IPacketInterceptor` + `IPlayerVisibilityGateway` adapters (Netty-side dispatch + `Player.hidePlayer` / spawn-suppression fallback when PE absent) | ❌ deferred to **Wave 6b** (Paper adapter layer); InMemory defaults are the production fallback when PE is missing | **Wave 6b** | P0 | ADR-0090 §Decision-3,5 + vanilla-Paper degradation path |
| Strongly-typed `PacketEnvelope` sub-types per `PacketKind` (replace `payloadView: Map<String, Any>`) | ❌ deferred to **Phase 5+** (after PacketEvents shape stabilises across 2 MC version bumps); revision is a new ADR | **Phase 5+** | P3 | ADR-0090 §Negative + §Alternatives Considered |
| Test fixtures: `FakePacketInterceptor` + `FakePlayerVisibilityGateway` (cross-plugin, downstream waves consume) | ✅ Wave 5y (mirror `InMemory*` semantics; recorded `calls` / `registrations` lists for assertion; configurable failure modes) | (済 Wave 5y) | P1 | `plugin/test-fixtures/.../{FakePacketInterceptor,FakePlayerVisibilityGateway}.kt`; ADR-0090 §Mitigations |

**Wave 5y summary**: 2 new ports (`IPacketInterceptor` +
`IPlayerVisibilityGateway`) + 4 new domain types (`PacketKind` +
`PacketEnvelope` + `PacketDecision` + `InterceptorSubscription`) + 2 new
sealed error surfaces (`VisibilityError` + `PacketBackendError`) + 2 new
in-memory default implementations + 1 anti-cheat bridge
(`MovementValidatorPacketBridge`) + 2 new domain events
(`EntityHiddenFromViewer` / `EntityShownToViewer`) + 2 new test fixtures.
2 new Konsist rules (`core.domain.*` must not import
`com.github.retrooper.packetevents.*`; `IPacketInterceptor` /
`IPlayerVisibilityGateway` application-layer impls must not import Bukkit
Paper). Catalog **+7 entries**, ✅ +5. ADR-0015 PacketEvents clause
superseded by ADR-0090. `./gradlew check` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar`
全 green.

## Category 8d-ext: Per-viewer state extension (Wave 5y-ext, ADR-0112)

Foundation layer that extends Wave 5y's per-viewer **visibility** with
per-viewer **state divergence** — same viewer can now see a *different*
NPC skin / name / equipment / scale, a *different* custom block at a given
position, and a *different* GUI slot / title — without the underlying
entity / world / GUI changing for anyone else. Closes the gap catalogued
in `.claude/plans/wave-5g-core-hardening-giggly-sunset.md` §Round 6 so
Wave 5pve mob skin pool / Wave 5flex glow-per-viewer / Wave 5h spy ability
/ Wave 5p tutorial highlight all gain a Core port they can call instead
of waiting for Wave 6.

| RTM 機能 | Astera 現状 | Wave 割当 | 優先度 | 根拠 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `IPerViewerNpcStateGateway` outbound port + 5 method families (`overrideSkin/resetSkin`, `overrideName/resetName`, `overrideEquipment/resetEquipment`, `overrideAttribute`, `clearAllOverrides` + `clearAllOverridesForViewer` + `currentOverrides` read-back) + `NpcAttributeKind` sealed 4-variant (`Scale(value≤16.0) / Glowing / Invisible / NameVisible`) + `NpcViewerOverride` snapshot data class | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (port + `InMemoryPerViewerNpcStateGateway` two-tier index w/ equality-detection no-op suppression + `trackViewer/trackEntity/untrackViewer/untrackEntity` lifecycle hooks; `untrackEntity` cascades to per-viewer `PerViewerOverridesCleared(TargetDespawned)`) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P0 | ADR-0112; `plugin/core/.../{domain/perviewer/{NpcAttributeKind,NpcViewerOverride}.kt,application/port/outbound/perviewer/npc/IPerViewerNpcStateGateway.kt,application/service/perviewer/InMemoryPerViewerNpcStateGateway.kt}` |
| `IPerViewerBlockStateGateway` outbound port + 4 axis methods (`overrideBlockType / overrideTexture / hideBlock / showBlock`) + `clearOverride / clearAllOverridesForViewer` + `(WorldId, Vec3)` keying (sub-block positions allowed for Display entities) | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (port + `InMemoryPerViewerBlockStateGateway` exclusive-axis tuple per entry per `BlockOverrideApplied` invariant; `hideBlock` preserves underlying type/texture so `showBlock` restores in one step) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P0 | ADR-0112; `plugin/core/.../{application/port/outbound/perviewer/block/IPerViewerBlockStateGateway.kt,application/service/perviewer/InMemoryPerViewerBlockStateGateway.kt}` |
| `IPerViewerGuiStateGateway` outbound port + `overrideSlotContent / overrideTitle / clearOverridesForViewer(handle) / clearAllOverridesForViewer(viewer)` + `GuiHandle(guiId, viewer)` value pair (handle-owner check on every mutator) | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (port + `InMemoryPerViewerGuiStateGateway` viewer→handle→state index w/ idempotent no-op suppression + dedicated `sweepOnQuit(viewer)` entry-point so PlayerQuit cleared events carry the correct `ClearReason`) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P0 | ADR-0112; `plugin/core/.../{domain/perviewer/GuiHandle.kt,application/port/outbound/perviewer/gui/IPerViewerGuiStateGateway.kt,application/service/perviewer/InMemoryPerViewerGuiStateGateway.kt}` |
| `PerViewerOverrideError` sealed (`UnknownViewer / UnknownTarget / InvalidOverride(reason) / BackendError(reason)`) + `ClearReason` sealed (`PlayerQuit / Explicit / TargetDespawned`) | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (distinct from `VisibilityError` because override has different failure cells than visibility toggle) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../domain/perviewer/{PerViewerOverrideError,ClearReason}.kt`; ADR-0112 |
| 4 new domain events: `NpcOverrideApplied(viewer, npc, snapshot)` + `BlockOverrideApplied(viewer, world, position, overrideType?, overrideTexture?, hidden)` w/ init-block exclusivity (exactly one axis non-default) + `GuiOverrideApplied(handle, slotIndex?, slotOverride?, titleOverride?)` w/ init-block `slotOverride.xor(titleOverride)` + `PerViewerOverridesCleared(viewer, gatewayFamily, reason, clearedCount)` w/ string-discriminator family ∈ `{npc, block, gui}` | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (no-op equality-detection suppresses re-apply events; cleared event emitted only when at least one slot was actually cleared) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P1 | `plugin/core/.../domain/event/{NpcOverrideApplied,BlockOverrideApplied,GuiOverrideApplied,PerViewerOverridesCleared}.kt`; ADR-0112 |
| `PlayerQuitServer` auto-clear subscriber wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` (fans out to NPC `untrackViewer` + Block `untrackViewer` + GUI `sweepOnQuit`) | ✅ Wave 5y-ext (subscription handle retained on the plugin so `onDisable` can cancel cleanly before the bus tears down) | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P0 | ADR-0112 §"Wiring"; `plugin/core/.../platform/AsteraCorePlugin.kt` |
| Test fixtures: `FakePerViewerNpcStateGateway` + `FakePerViewerBlockStateGateway` + `FakePerViewerGuiStateGateway` (cross-plugin; recorded `calls` list + configurable failure modes via `rejectViewer / rejectNpc / failBackend`) | ✅ Wave 5y-ext | (済 Wave 5y-ext) | P1 | `plugin/test-fixtures/.../{FakePerViewerNpcStateGateway,FakePerViewerBlockStateGateway,FakePerViewerGuiStateGateway}.kt` |
| Wave 6 PacketEvents-backed `IPerViewerNpcStateGateway` (PlayerInfoUpdate + EntityMetadata + EntityEquipment per viewer) + `IPerViewerBlockStateGateway` (BlockChange-per-viewer) + `IPerViewerGuiStateGateway` (Paper Inventory mutation routed through `IGuiAdapter` per-viewer) at priority 100 | ❌ deferred to **Wave 6** (Paper adapter layer); InMemory defaults are the production fallback when PE is missing | **Wave 6** | P0 | ADR-0112 §"Consequences" → "Wave 6 PacketEvents sweep stays wrap-only" |

**Wave 5y-ext summary**: 3 new ports
(`IPerViewerNpcStateGateway` + `IPerViewerBlockStateGateway` +
`IPerViewerGuiStateGateway`) + 5 new domain types (`NpcAttributeKind`
sealed + `NpcViewerOverride` snapshot + `PerViewerOverrideError` sealed
+ `GuiHandle` value + `ClearReason` sealed) + 3 new in-memory default
implementations + 4 new domain events (`NpcOverrideApplied` +
`BlockOverrideApplied` w/ exclusivity invariant + `GuiOverrideApplied`
w/ `xor` invariant + `PerViewerOverridesCleared` w/ family
discriminator) + 1 PlayerQuit auto-clear subscriber + 3 new
cross-plugin test fixtures. 1 new Konsist rule
(`per-viewer state domain stays pure (ADR-0112)`, total 49). Catalog
**+8 entries**, ✅ +7. ADR-0112 → Accepted. `./gradlew check` +
`:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green.

## Aggregate: what Wave 5a〜5f + Wave 6 will deliver

### Wave 5a (Combat depth, Phase A→B 6 worker→C, ~5-6h)
- SkillCast 6 archetype handler (PROJECTILE/MELEE/AOE/BUFF/TELEPORT/SUMMON)
- Armor domain (ArmorSpec / ArmorSlot / set bonus)
- DamageRule depth (armor reduction + crit chance/multiplier + knockback + friendly-fire)
- Skill resource cost (mana/energy)
- Charged skill / hold-to-cast variant
- Death attribution → respawn flow
- **Unblocks P0 "skill dispatch hollow" gap**

### Wave 5b (HUD ports, Phase A→B 4-5 worker→C, ~3-4h)
- `IBossBar` / `IBossBarManager` port + Fake impl
- `IScoreboard` / `ITeamScoreboard` port + Fake impl
- `ITabList` port + Fake impl
- `IMcPlayer.sendActionBar()` / `sendTitle()` API extension
- (Paper impls land in Wave 6)

### Wave 5c (Game modes + Star Generator + Anomaly, Phase A→B 5-6 worker→C, ~5-6h)
- Sealed `GameMode` framework + per-mode listener pattern
- 6 game mode domain implementations (Battle Loyal / Break Core / Carry TNT / Carry Mini TNT / Capture Wool / Steal Jewelry)
- Star Generator domain + scheduler config
- Anomaly type sealed + AnomalyScheduler
- Sudden death special mechanics

### Wave 5d (Stage geometry + Block tracking + Barrier, Phase A→B 6-7 worker→C, ~6-7h)
- Block tracking domain (BlockPlaced / BlockBroken events PROMOTED from Wave 5c stub, per-match state via IBlockStateGateway + InMemory impl)
- Block durability domain (max 100 HP + tool×block matrix YAML + pseudo-recovery scheduler @ 10s / +5 HP)
- Barrier system domain (custom enclosure + status scheduler, DisplayEntity visual is Wave 6)
- Custom machine domain (Redstone dispenser + skill core + trap types; P2 minimal — no use case)
- Capture zone / KOTH region type (domain + tick use case + scheduler)
- Schematic spec (ISchematicLoader port + InMemory Fake; FAWE adapter is Wave 6)
- Per-match world isolation (MatchWorld domain + repository + Create/Destroy use cases; Paper world creation is Wave 6)
- Block restoration after match (BlockChangeJournal + RestoreBlocksOnMatchEnded listener subscribing to MatchEnded)

### Wave 5e (Session + Social + Tutorial + EnderChest + Reward bridge + Inventory snapshot, Phase A→B 6 worker→C, ~6h)
- Player session state (InMatch / Lobby / Spectator / Dead / **Loading** — RTM #5 action-lock)
- Spectator mode domain
- Friend list / Party domain
- Team chat channel domain
- Achievement / Title / Badge domain
- Tutorial system domain
- EnderChest / Personal Vault (`IPersonalVaultService` + Postgres)
- Personal settings DB
- Reward 2-server flow (game-server side; Redis-bridge spec)
- Audit log domain + table
- **PlayerInventorySnapshot domain + V10 + Postgres + Save/Restore use case + 2 listener** (RTM #5)

### Wave 5f (Marketplace + JP currency + CEL expression, Phase A→B 4-5 worker→C, ~4-5h)
- Player marketplace + creator revenue share
- JP regulatory currency compliance (`CurrencyPolicy` domain with expiry / cap / non-redeemable flags)
- CEL expression language for skill extensibility
- Particle definition system
- Reactive GUI binding (`ResolvedReactiveSlot` extension for numeric / display override)

### Wave 5v (Match recording capture, Phase A→B 3 territory→C, ~4h)
- `IMatchRecorder` inbound port + `IMatchReplayRepository` outbound port + `IRecorderStateProvider` outbound port
- `ReplayCaptureEngine`: IDomainEventBus subscriber (5 event types: DamageDealt / PlayerKilled / SkillCast / ChatMessageSent / PhaseChanged), key-frame ticker (IScheduler), 12 MB hard cap
- 9-variant `ReplayEvent` sealed (PlayerMoved / DamageDealt / PlayerKilled / PlayerRespawned / ItemUsed / SkillCast / ChatSent / MatchPhaseChanged / PillarCaptured) — all flat data classes over primitives + value classes (Konsist-enforced)
- 2-variant `ReplayFormat` sealed (`EventLogKeyFrame` default + `FullPerTick` forensic)
- V20 `match_replays` Flyway migration (Postgres JSONB for events / key-frames / format + columnar start_tick / end_tick / size_bytes)
- `PostgresMatchReplayRepository` + `InMemoryMatchReplayRepository` + `NoopRecorderStateProvider` (Wave 6a `PaperRecorderStateProvider` overlay later)
- `/astera-match replay <start|stop|export> <matchId>` admin commands + 3 new permissions
- `.replay.json` export envelope (`ReplayExportEnvelope` + sealed-by-key `ReplayEventJson`) — Phase 4 web UI consumer wire shape
- 2 new Konsist rules + ADR-0088
- 2 new domain events (`MatchRecordingStarted` / `MatchRecordingStopped`) on `core.domain.event`

### Wave 6 (Paper Sweep, Phase A→B 8+ worker→C, 1-2 sessions)
- PaperDamageGateway
- PaperStatusEffectContainer + tick loop
- PaperProjectileService (all 5 motion variants)
- PaperWorldGateway (placeBlock / removeBlock / spawnParticle / playSound / full teleport)
- PaperBossBar / PaperScoreboard / PaperTabList
- PaperSchematicLoader (FAWE adapter)
- PaperSpectator + Bukkit GameMode mapping
- BukkitEventAdapter extensions (EntityDamage / EntityDeath / PlayerJoin / PlayerQuit / BlockPlace / BlockBreak / InventoryClick already done)
- IBroadcaster Redis + Discord real impls
- Vec3 math (normalize / dot / cross / length)
- **PaperInventorySnapshotSerializer (Bukkit ↔ JSON via BukkitObjectOutputStream)** + auto save/restore wire (RTM #5)
- **InventoryActionLockEnforcer** (BukkitEventAdapter で Loading state 中 InventoryClick/PickupItem/Interact cancel)
- All Paper-side wire-up

## Living doc — update protocol

Each Wave 5x / Wave 6 / Phase 4+ session updates this tracker as part of
**Phase C (me 統合)** alongside the wave's commit:

1. Mark delivered rows: `❌ → ✅` (full) or `❌ → 🔌` (port-only when Paper-impl
   待ち)
2. Update `Summary metrics` table
3. Add new rows discovered during the wave (RTM issue / Writerside doc not yet
   tracked)
4. Update aggregate "Wave X delivers" section if scope shifted

The tracker is **single source of truth** for "what's left". When in doubt,
consult this doc before re-reading RTM upstream — and only re-fetch RTM if a
specific issue / file needs verification.

ADR-0085 → Accepted. **Phase 1 PoC critical path 完了 marker** — Wave 5pve = critical path 最後の domain wave、Wave 6 Paper sweep に進める。
- Wave _infra-5 (2026-05-19): weapon→item→model chain closed + Studio frontend `/weapons` → `/items` migration (ADR-0070 Accepted). Kotlin: `ItemYamlConfig.model: String?` + `ItemSpec.modelSlug: String?` (opaque slug — no `.model.json` suffix, null defers to vendor/archetype fallback). Studio backend `/api/items/load/:slug`: priority resolution content dir → samples dir → archetype map, exposes `modelSource: 'content'|'samples'|'archetype'` + `modelPathRelative`. New endpoint `GET /api/items/model/by-slug/:slug`. Studio frontend: `WeaponsView.vue`/`WeaponPreview.vue` deleted, `ItemsView.vue`/`ItemPreview.vue` created consuming `/api/items` + new `LoadedItem` (incl. `modelSource`). `src/api.ts` rewritten — `WeaponListEntry`/`WeaponYamlConfig`/`LoadedWeapon`/`api.weapons`/`api.loadWeapon`/`api.weaponModel` removed; `ItemListEntry`/`ItemYamlConfig` (incl. `model?: string`)/`ItemSkillBindingYaml`/`ItemAttributesYaml`/`LoadedItem`/`api.items`/`api.loadItem`/`api.itemModelBySlug`/`api.itemModelByArchetype` added. Router `/weapons` → `/items`, nav tab `ウェポン` → `アイテム`. FALLBACK chip narrowed to `modelSource === 'archetype' && modelFallback`. Sample content: `content/items/example-pistol.yaml` declares `model: example-pistol`, `content/models/example-pistol.model.json` seeded from `tools/model-gen/samples/sword.model.json` (placeholder — visual fidelity is ADR-0061 Mismatch A, separate `build-model` track). Konsist 22 rules green; `./gradlew check` green; Studio `bun run typecheck && bun test` green. ADR-0070 → Accepted (references ADR-0061 + ADR-0073, supersedes neither). No RTM rows flipped (structural plumbing). ADR-0061 Mismatch C resolved; Mismatch A remains open.
- Wave 5pve-boss (2026-05-23): PvE boss + encounter + loot foundation — extends Wave 5pve (ADR-0085) with boss layer (ADR-0098 Accepted). Domain (`core.domain.mob.{boss,encounter,loot,raid,runtime}.*`, 13 new files): boss subdomain (`BossId` + `BossSpec` composing `MobTypeId` via `baseMobType` + `BossPhase` with i18n nameKey + immunityTicks + transitionCast + periodic/onHit skill lists + sealed `BossPhaseTransition.HpThreshold` + `BossError` sealed 6-variant); encounter subdomain (`EncounterZoneId` + `EncounterZone` with weighted `EncounterMobEntry` mobPool + maxConcurrent + sealed `DifficultyCurve.{Flat,Polynomial}`); loot subdomain (`LootDrop` chance + count range + `LootRollContext` carrying boss/zone/party metadata + `LootRoll` with seed for deterministic replay); raid subdomain (sealed `RaidLockPolicy.{Open,PartyLock(maxPartySize 1..8)}` + `RaidClaim` first-hitter tracking); runtime subdomain (`MobId` UUID-wrapping value class distinct from template-level `MobTypeId`). 4 new core domain events (`BossSpawned` / `BossPhaseChanged` / `BossDefeated` / `LootDropped`). 4 new outbound ports in `core.application.port.outbound.mob.*`: `IBossLifecycleService` (registerSpec / spawn / instanceOf / onDamage with raid-lock + HpThreshold transition eval / onDeath + `BossInstance` + `BossInstanceHandle` snapshots) + `IEncounterZoneRegistry` (register / get / all / size) + `ILootRollService` (registerTable / roll(context, seed, atTick) — publishes `LootDropped` per call) + `IBossKillAuditWriter` (idempotent record(BossDefeated) on `mobId`). 4 in-core service impls (`InMemoryBossLifecycleService` single-threaded orchestration + `InMemoryEncounterZoneRegistry` LinkedHashMap + `DeterministicLootRollService` with `(seed XOR table XOR mob XOR killer-UUID-bits XOR boss?)` per-call seed mixing + `InMemoryBossKillAuditWriter` Phase 1 default — persistence overlays at priority 100 in Wave 6c). Persistence: V30 `boss_kills` migration (UUID PK + `mob_id` VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE for idempotency + `boss_id` VARCHAR(128) + nullable `killer_id` UUID + nullable `party_id` VARCHAR(64) + `final_phase` SMALLINT + `fight_duration_ticks` BIGINT + `killed_at` TIMESTAMP + `at_tick` BIGINT + 3 indexes: `boss_recent` for leaderboard / speedrun, partial `party` for weekly raid clears, partial `killer` for first-clear history). Konsist +1 rule (35→36): `core domain mob boss encounter loot raid runtime must stay platform-free (ADR-0098)` — all 5 new subpackages MUST NOT import Bukkit family / JDBC / Exposed / `java.util.concurrent.*` (the last guarding the "boss state runs on Paper main thread single-threaded" invariant). Test layer: 8 test classes (53 tests across `BossSpecTest` × 10, `BossPhaseTest` × 5, `BossPhaseTransitionTest` × 4, `RaidLockPolicyTest` × 4, `EncounterZoneTest` × 9, `LootDropTest` × 6, `LootRollContextTest` × 5, `InMemoryBossLifecycleServiceTest` × 10 with stub `IMobSpawner` for entity-handle minting, `DeterministicLootRollServiceTest` × 8 covering guaranteed/zero/range/replay-determinism/event-publish, `InMemoryEncounterZoneRegistryTest` × 4). Wired into `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` (4 new service registrations: `IEncounterZoneRegistry` p=0, `ILootRollService` p=0, `IBossKillAuditWriter` p=0, `IBossLifecycleService` p=0). `./gradlew check` + `shadowJar` 全 green. Net catalog: +1 row (PvE boss + encounter + loot foundation, 🔌 port skeleton). **Total parity 70.5% (105/149) → 70.0% (105/150) — same ✅, +1 🔌, denominator +1 catalog growth (port-only foundation row)**. ADR-0098 → Accepted. Boss content (encounter zones / loot tables / actual bosses) lands in Wave 12b (ADR-0099 Draft) on top of this foundation.
- Wave 5v (2026-05-21): Match recording capture (lightweight, event log + key frame; playback Phase 4) — Domain: `ReplayId` value class + `RecordedReplay` + 9-variant `ReplayEvent` sealed + `KeyFrame` + 2-variant `ReplayFormat` sealed (`EventLogKeyFrame` default keyFramePer=100 / `FullPerTick` forensic) + `ReplayError` sealed + `Vec3Snapshot` Float-triplet + `DamageSourceKind` 8-enum + 2 core domain events (`MatchRecordingStarted` / `MatchRecordingStopped`). Ports: `IMatchRecorder` (inbound) + `IMatchReplayRepository` (outbound) + `IRecorderStateProvider` (outbound). Application: `ReplayCaptureEngine` subscribes 5 event types (DamageDealt / PlayerKilled / SkillCast / ChatMessageSent / PhaseChanged), key-frame ticker via IScheduler every 20 ticks, 12 MB hard cap with FIFO-preserving warn-then-drop + `InMemoryMatchReplayRepository` (priority 0) + `NoopRecorderStateProvider` (priority 0). Persistence: V20 `match_replays` table (UUID `replay_id` + columnar `start_tick` / `end_tick` / `size_bytes` + 3 JSONB columns `format` / `key_frames` / `events`) + `PostgresMatchReplayRepository` (priority 100, upsert on `match_id`) + JSONB DTOs `MatchReplayJson` (sealed-by-key discriminator `type: event-log-keyframe / full-per-tick / damage-dealt / player-killed / ...`) in `:plugin:match.application.replay.json`. Commands: `/astera-match replay <start|stop|export> <matchId>` + 3 permissions + `<dataFolder>/replays/<matchId>.replay.json` writer via `ReplayExportEnvelope` (same wire shape as DB row). Konsist +2 rules (30→32): `Wave 5v ReplayEvent variants must contain only primitive or value-class types` + `Wave 5v IMatchRecorder must not import Bukkit Paper`. RTM +4 rows (Cat 2: Match recording capture ✅ + `/astera-match replay` admin command ✅ + `.replay.json` export envelope ✅ + Match playback UI ❌ Phase 4). +3 unit test classes (`ReplayCaptureEngineTest` + `InMemoryMatchReplayRepositoryTest` + `RecordedReplayInvariantTest`) + 1 Postgres IT (`PostgresMatchReplayRepositoryIT`). ADR-0088 → Accepted. **Total parity 70.3% (102/145) → 70.5% (105/149) — +3 ✅, +1 ❌, denominator +4 catalog growth**.
- Wave 5pve-content (2026-05-24): PvE Content — encounter zones + drop tables + boss mechanics + group raid lock + death penalty rule (ADR-0099 Accepted, **fast-forwarded from originally-planned Wave 12b** to close out the PvE retention loop alongside Wave 5pve-boss). Domain delta: `core.domain.mob.encounter.DeathPenaltyRule` sealed (`SafeDeath` / `ItemDropOnDeath(graceTicks=600 default, 6000 max)`) + `EncounterZone.deathPenalty: DeathPenaltyRule = SafeDeath` additive defaulted field. Ports (`core.application.port.outbound.mob.*`): `IBossSpecRegistry` (parallel to `IEncounterZoneRegistry`) + `IDeathPenaltyPolicy` (read-only resolver, Wave 6b death-handler applies). YAML DTOs (`core.application.config.mob.pve.*`): `LootTableYamlConfig` + `LootDropYaml` (rejects duplicate item_id per table) + `EncounterZoneYamlConfig` + `EncounterMobEntryYaml` + `DifficultyCurveYaml` (discriminator: flat / polynomial) + `DeathPenaltyYaml` (discriminator: safe / item_drop) + `BossYamlConfig` + `BossPhaseYaml` + `BossPhaseTransitionYaml` (discriminator: hp_threshold) + `RaidLockYaml` (discriminator: open / party_lock). Content registries (`core.application.contents.pve.*`): `EncounterZoneContents : YamlDynamicContents<EncounterZoneId, EncounterZone, EncounterZoneYamlConfig>` ALSO implementing `IEncounterZoneRegistry` (replaces the empty in-memory ADR-0098 default at boot) + `BossSpecContents : YamlDynamicContents<BossId, BossSpec, BossYamlConfig>` ALSO implementing `IBossSpecRegistry`. Loader (`core.application.service.mob.pve.*`): `LootTableLoaderService` (stand-alone, loot tables aren't `Contents<K, V>` shape — each table is a list) + `ZoneScopedDeathPenaltyPolicy` (reads `EncounterZone.deathPenalty`, falls back to SAFE on null/unknown zone) + `BossDefeatedLootRoller` (subscribes `BossDefeated`, resolves spec via `IBossSpecRegistry`, calls `ILootRollService.roll` with constructed `LootRollContext`; skips null-killer + `LootTableId.EMPTY` to avoid loot-event noise). Pre-release breaking change per CLAUDE.md §"Pre-release posture": `IEncounterZoneRegistry.all` and `IBossSpecRegistry.all` return `Collection<...>` (was `List<...>` in ADR-0098) so YAML substrate can satisfy without defensive copy. PoC content shipped: 2 encounter zones (`content/pve/encounters/{blazing-pit,whispering-cavern}.yaml` — first party-locked raid w/ item-drop penalty + polynomial difficulty + boss anchor; second open mob grinder w/ safe death + flat difficulty) + 1 boss (`content/pve/bosses/scorched-titan.yaml` — 2 phases molten→enraged@50% HP w/ 60-tick immunity + party_lock max 4) + 4 loot tables (`content/pve/drop-tables/{scorched-titan,ember-imp,flame-jackal,forest-wolf-drops}.yaml` — rare drops range 0.5 → 0.0005, hitting ADR cap) + 3 supporting mob templates (`content/mobs/{ember-imp,flame-jackal,scorched-titan-base}.yaml`). Persistence: V34 `pve_encounter_clears` migration (UUID PK + player_id + zone_id VARCHAR(128) + nullable boss_kill_id UUID FK to V31 boss_kills + nullable party_id + cleared_at + at_tick + 3 indexes: player_recent timeline / zone_first leaderboard / (player, zone, cleared_at DESC) weekly-reset gate). Writer impl lands in Wave 5pve-fix / Wave 6c (schema-only ship, mirroring V31 ADR-0098 pattern). Konsist +1 rule (36→37): `pve content yaml DTOs must stay platform-free (ADR-0099)` — `core.application.config.mob.pve.*` must not import Bukkit family / JDBC / Exposed / `java.util.concurrent.*`. Test layer: 8 new test classes (39 tests — `DeathPenaltyRuleTest` × 6, `LootTableYamlConfigTest` × 5, `EncounterZoneYamlConfigTest` × 7, `BossYamlConfigTest` × 6, `EncounterZoneContentsTest` × 3, `BossSpecContentsTest` × 2, `LootTableLoaderServiceTest` × 3, `ZoneScopedDeathPenaltyPolicyTest` × 4, `BossDefeatedLootRollerTest` × 5). Wired into `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`: replaces `InMemoryEncounterZoneRegistry` with `EncounterZoneContents.bootstrap(content/pve/encounters)` + adds `BossSpecContents.bootstrap(content/pve/bosses)` (forwards YAML-loaded specs into `bossLifecycle.registerSpec()`) + `LootTableLoaderService.bootstrap(content/pve/drop-tables, lootRollService)` + `ZoneScopedDeathPenaltyPolicy` + `BossDefeatedLootRoller.wire(eventBus)` + 3 reloadRegistry registrations (encounters + bosses). `./gradlew check` green; all 7 plugin shadowJars green. RTM row #491 PvE Content flipped ❌→✅ (no denominator change). ADR-0099 → Accepted. **Single commit on branch `wave-5pve-content`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**
- Wave 5refine (2026-05-23): Item Refinement Layer (ADR-0100 Accepted, **fast-forwarded from originally-planned Wave 12c** to unblock retention-axis design earlier in Phase 1 PoC closing). Domain (`items.domain.refinement.*`, 8 files): `ItemInstanceId` value class + `ItemInstance` (per-player container; layers + sub-stat rolls + reroll count → `IAttributeContainer` projection per ADR-0058 §1) + `Refinement` sealed (`Reforge` / `Enchant` / `Gemstone` / `Star` / `Recomb` — HSB+原神+MH+PoE 4軸 closed set) + `RefinementSpec` per-item capability declaration + `SubStatKey` enum (ATTACK / ATTACK_SPEED / CRIT_CHANCE / CRIT_DAMAGE / DEFENSE / MAX_HEALTH / MOVEMENT_SPEED) + `SubStatRoll` 原神聖遺物 record + `RefinementMath` (4-category MULTIPLY coefficient table targeting `item.refined_power_multiplier` + escalating reroll cost ladder 900 / 1000 / 2000 / 2500 / 3000 plateau) + `RefinementError` sealed 6-variant. Application (`items.application.*`): 2 outbound ports (`IItemInstanceRepository` + `IRefinementRandomizer`) + 2 use cases (`ApplyRefinementUseCase` validates layer-against-spec + auto-creates on first apply + publishes `ItemInstanceRefined`; `RerollSubStatsUseCase` charges escalating cost via `Wallet.withdraw(Currency.GOLD)` + rolls candidate + caller-supplied `keepIfBetter` predicate [default `KEEP_IF_HIGHER_SUM`] + always publishes `ItemInstanceRerolled` for analytics — Warframe Riven 反例 教訓: 過去 roll キープが default) + `RefinementProfileRegistry` `(ItemId) -> RefinementSpec` resolver + 2 DTOs (`RefinementYamlConfig` + `RefinementProfileYaml`) + `InMemoryItemInstanceRepository` + `KotlinRandomRefinementRandomizer` + `FakeRefinementRandomizer` (test-fixtures). Core events: `ItemInstanceRefined` + `ItemInstanceRerolled` (in `core.domain.event`, polymorphic-untyped per ADR-0022 — UUID raw + ItemId string discriminator). Persistence: V30 `item_instances` migration (UUID PK / item_id varchar / owner_id UUID / created_at timestamp / `layers` JSONB / `sub_stat_rolls` JSONB / `reroll_count` integer queryable per ADR-0014 + 2 indexes `idx_item_instances_owner` + `idx_item_instances_item_id`) + `PostgresItemInstanceRepository` (priority 100 overlay; polymorphic Refinement via kotlinx-serialization `kind` discriminator + skip-malformed read semantics per `PostgresMarketplaceRepository` pattern) + `ItemInstancesTable` + local `JsonbStringColumnType` (same pattern as `ModerationActionsTable`). Konsist +1 rule (33→34): `items refinement domain stays pure (ADR-0100)` (`scopeFromProject().files` filtered on `items.domain.refinement.*` — Bukkit + kotlinx.serialization + exposed + postgres + sql + lettuce + koin all forbidden). Wiring: `AsteraItemsPlugin` (in-memory repo p=0 + KotlinRandom randomizer p=0 + `RefinementProfileRegistry` + both use cases registered for Wave 6 GUI lookup) + `AsteraPersistencePlugin` (`PostgresItemInstanceRepository` p=100 overlay). Content: `content/refinement/example-sword.yaml` demonstration profile. Tests: 33 unit tests (7 classes — `RefinementSpecTest` × 5 / `RefinementMathTest` × 4 / `ItemInstanceTest` × 5 / `ApplyRefinementUseCaseTest` × 8 / `RerollSubStatsUseCaseTest` × 4 / `RefinementYamlConfigTest` × 3 / `RefinementProfileRegistryTest` × 3 / `ItemInstanceRefinedTest` × 2 [core]) + 5 Postgres IT (`PostgresItemInstanceRepositoryIT`). `./gradlew check` green; all 6 plugin shadowJars green. RTM row #489 flipped ❌→✅ (no denominator change — row already existed). ADR-0100 → Accepted. **Single commit on branch `wave-5refine`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5bet (2026-05-24): 1v1 Betting Match (ADR-0103 Accepted, **fast-forwarded from originally-planned Wave 12f** as 1v1 PvP retention foundation, paving the way for Wave 7b Tournament + Ranked queue). Domain (`economy.domain.betting.*`, 7 files): `BettingMatchId` UUID value class + `BettingMatch` 6-state machine (`PendingAcceptance` → `Active` → `Resolved` または終端 `Declined` / `Expired` / `Cancelled`) + `BetStake` (`(currency, amount)` with **GEM hard-reject** per JP compliance + ADR-0035 alignment) + `BettingPolicy` 8-tunable + `BettingPolicyId` lower-kebab-case + `BettingEscrow` + `BettingEscrowStatus` (Held / Paid / Refunded) + `LossStreakState` (per-player consecutive losses + auto-cooldown open/close) + `BettingError` sealed 15-variant. Application: 5 outbound ports (`IBettingMatchRepository` / `IBettingEscrowService` ledger-transactional rollback-on-opponent-fail / `IBettingHistoryService` daily-stake + cooldown + loss-streak / `IBettingPolicyRegistry` / `IBettingOptInProvider` default-disabled gating) + 4 use cases (`InitiateBettingChallengeUseCase` 8-stage fail-fast validation [self-bet / policy / currency / cap / opt-in / cooldown / loss-streak / daily-cap] / `AcceptBettingChallengeUseCase` `escrow.hold` + matchId binding + 2 `CurrencyChanged(kind=escrow_debit)` publishes / `DeclineBettingChallengeUseCase` opponent-decline-or-rescind / `ExpirePendingBettingChallengesUseCase` per-minute scheduler sweep) + 2 listeners (`ResolveBettingOnMatchEndedListener` MatchEnded → BettingMatchResolved with winner-from-team projection + draw fallback / `DistributeBettingPayoutOnResolvedListener` escrow release-or-refund + loss-streak increment-or-reset + cooldown record + `BettingPayoutDistributed` publish) + `BettingPolicyContents : YamlDynamicContents<BettingPolicyId, BettingPolicy, BettingPolicyYamlConfig>` ALSO implementing `IBettingPolicyRegistry` + `BettingPolicyYamlConfig` DTO (millis-based Duration serialization, case-insensitive currency parsing, GEM rejection). Cross-plugin domain events: `BettingMatchInitiated` + `BettingMatchResolved` + `BettingPayoutDistributed` (all 3 in `core.domain.event`, UUID raw bettingMatchUuid for polymorphic-untyped per ADR-0022). Plus `CurrencyChangeReason.Betting(bettingMatchUuid, kind)` added (kinds: `escrow_debit` / `winner_payout` / `refund`) so analytics splits the 3 series cleanly. Persistence: V38 `betting_matches` (UUID PK + policy_id + challenger / opponent + stake currency / amount + status + 4 lifecycle timestamps + match_id NULL-until-accept VARCHAR(128) [NOT UUID per `MatchId` contract] + winner_id NULL-until-resolve + end_reason_kind + end_reason_detail + CHECK distinct_participants + 4 indexes: status / match_id partial / (challenger, created_at DESC) / (opponent, created_at DESC)) + V39 `bet_escrows` (bettingMatchId PK + FK to V38 ON DELETE RESTRICT + challenger / opponent + currency + per_side_amount + status + held_at + settled_at + CHECK distinct_escrow_participants + CHECK settled_iff_terminal). Postgres overlays (priority 100): `PostgresBettingMatchRepository` (upsert + skip-on-decode-fail per `PostgresMarketplaceRepository` pattern) + `PostgresBettingEscrowService` (transactional debit-credit pair via injected `ICurrencyLedger` — by registration time `PostgresCurrencyLedger` p=100 has won) + `PostgresBettingHistoryService` (SQL aggregate for daily stake + in-process ConcurrentHashMap for loss-streak / last-resolution, same hybrid pattern as `PostgresEngagementSnapshotRepository`). Wiring: `AsteraEconomyPlugin.onEnable` registers in-memory defaults at p=0 (Postgres-less fallback), seeds `BettingPolicyContents` with `BettingPolicy.DEFAULT` then `bootstrap(content/bet-policies/)`, registers 4 use cases + subscribes `MatchEnded` + `BettingMatchResolved` + adds per-minute expiry scheduler; `AsteraPersistencePlugin.onEnable` registers 3 Postgres impls at p=100. Konsist +1 rule (37→38): `betting domain stays pure (ADR-0103)` — `economy.domain.betting.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin (`scopeFromProject().files` based for future plugin rename robustness). i18n: 16 `astera.betting.*` error keys added across en + ja in parity. PoC content: `content/bet-policies/default.yaml` (max 1000 / daily 10000 / cooldown 10min / acceptance 10min / loss-streak 5/24h / STAR+GOLD only / payoutSplit 1.0). Tests: 11 test classes (50+ tests — `BetStakeTest` × 5 / `BettingPolicyTest` × 9 / `BettingPolicyIdTest` × 4 / `BettingMatchTest` × 13 / `BettingStatusTest` × 2 / `LossStreakStateTest` × 5 / `InitiateBettingChallengeUseCaseTest` × 9 / `AcceptBettingChallengeUseCaseTest` × 6 / `DeclineBettingChallengeUseCaseTest` × 4 / `ExpirePendingBettingChallengesUseCaseTest` × 1 / `ResolveBettingOnMatchEndedListenerTest` × 5 / `DistributeBettingPayoutOnResolvedListenerTest` × 5 / `InMemoryBettingEscrowServiceTest` × 9 / `BettingPolicyYamlConfigTest` × 3). `./gradlew check` green; `:plugin:economy:shadowJar` + `:plugin:persistence:shadowJar` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. RTM row #495 1v1 Betting Match flipped ❌→✅ (no denominator change). ADR-0103 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 5bet follow-up**: `/astera-bet` command surface (Phase 2), item-stake variant (Phase 4+ per OSRS Duel Arena 反例), multi-party / spectator betting (Phase 4+). **Single commit on branch `wave-5bet`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5world-events (2026-05-27): World event domain expansion + Inventory mutation primitives (ADR-0111 Accepted, Round 5 of Wave 5g §"Round 5 + Round 6" parallel fire). **Pre-Paper-sweep foundation**: closes the two largest pre-Wave-6 gaps audited 2026-05-27 — (a) Spatial / interaction event surface (player position change / discrete teleport / block interact / chunk load-unload / world weather + time change), (b) Generic inventory mutation surface beyond `IItemDelivery` (single-item) / `IPlayerInventorySnapshotRepository` (snapshot) / `IInventoryShareGuard` (cross-mode policy). **Domain — world reasons** (`core.domain.world.*`, 4 new): `TeleportReason` sealed 6-variant (`Command` / `Respawn` / `Portal(portalId?)` / `Admin` / `MatchTransition(matchId)` / `EnderPearl(itemKind)`) + `BlockInteractAction` sealed 4-variant (`RightClick` / `LeftClick` / `PhysicalContact` / `OffHandUse` — sealed to grow payload without breaking exhaustive callers) + `ChunkUnloadReason` enum 3-variant (`PLAYER_LEFT_RANGE` / `WORLD_UNLOAD` / `FORCED_BY_ADMIN`) + `TimeChangeReason` sealed 4-variant (`NaturalCycle` / `Admin` / `GameRule` / `MatchPhase(matchId)`). **Domain — inventory** (`core.domain.inventory.*`, 8 new files NEW package): `ClearScope` sealed 6-variant (`All` / `Hotbar` / `MainInventory` / `Armor` / `OffHand` / `Slot(slotIndex)` w/ 0..40 init validation) + `InventoryError` sealed 6-variant (`PlayerOffline` / `InventoryFull(playerId, itemId, leftover)` / `InvalidSlotIndex` / `UnknownItem` / `SlotTypeLocked(slotIndex, itemId)` / `InsufficientStock(playerId, itemId, requested, available)`) + `SlotTypeLockRule` fun interface w/ `PERMISSIVE` + `DefaultSlotTypeLockRule` (Minecraft armor-slot keyword semantics: 36 boots / 37 leggings / 38 chestplate / 39 helmet) + `InventoryTransferTarget` sealed 4-variant (`PlayerInventory(playerId)` / `BlockContainer(world, position, containerKind)` / `VirtualContainer(key)` / `Dropped(world, position)`) + `KitId` `JvmInline value class` namespaced lower-kebab (`astera:pvp-starter`) + `LootApplied(lootTableId, deliveredItems, leftoverItems)` + `LootError` sealed 3-variant (`UnknownLootTable` / `PlayerOffline` / `DeliveryFailed(cause)`) + `KitError` sealed 3-variant (`UnknownKit` / `PlayerOffline` / `DeliveryFailed(cause)`). **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 8 new): `PlayerMoved(playerId, from, to, world, atTick)` (region boundary trigger payload) + `PlayerTeleported(playerId, from, to, worldFrom, worldTo, reason, atTick)` (distinct world handles for cross-world short-circuit) + `PlayerInteractedBlock(playerId, world, position, blockId, action, atTick)` (skill-trigger / region permission / custom block / pressure plate single source) + `ChunkLoaded(world, chunkX, chunkZ, atTick)` + `ChunkUnloaded(world, chunkX, chunkZ, reason, atTick)` + `WeatherChanged(world, oldWeather, newWeather, atTick)` (reuses Wave 5n `WeatherState` sealed) + `TimeChanged(world, oldTime, newTime, reason, atTick)` w/ Minecraft 0..23999 daytime range validation + `InventoryTransferred(fromPlayer?, target, items, atTick)` w/ non-empty + positive-count validation (`fromPlayer = null` = system origin). **Outbound ports** (3 new): `IInventoryMutator` (`core.application.port.outbound.inventory.*`) `give / take / clear / swap / setSlot / count` all `Result<T, InventoryError>` w/ canonical Minecraft 0..40 slot layout (0..8 hotbar / 9..35 main / 36..39 armor / 40 off-hand); `take` is **lenient** (returns actual count, callers needing strict semantics compose w/ `count` pre-check) + `IBulkInventoryOps` `applyLootTable(playerId, lootTableId) -> Result<LootApplied, LootError>` + `issueKit(playerId, kitId) -> Result<Unit, KitError>` both firing single `InventoryTransferred` event on success + `IRegionEventFilter` (`core.application.port.outbound.world.*`) `subscribe(regionId, type, handler)` wrapping `IDomainEventBus.subscribe` w/ `(world, position)` extractor + region-membership predicate. **In-memory impls** (`core.application.service.{inventory,world}.*`, 3 new): `InMemoryInventoryMutator` w/ fixed-size `Array<Slot?>` per player (41 slots) + canonical give fill-order (top-up matching stacks in main+hotbar 0..35 → fill empty main+hotbar → leftover surface as `InventoryFull(leftover)`); no max-stack-size enforcement in Phase 1 (Paper adapter enforces) + `InMemoryBulkInventoryOps` composing mutator + injected `lootTableResolver: (LootTableId) -> Map<ItemId, Int>?` + `kitResolver: (KitId) -> Map<ItemId, Int>?` + `tickProvider` lambda (single `InventoryTransferred` event per successful bulk op) + `InMemoryRegionEventFilter` w/ canonical position extractor covering `PlayerMoved` / `PlayerTeleported` / `PlayerInteractedBlock` / `ChunkLoaded` / `ChunkUnloaded` (drops events without spatial payload silently — bus still routes them normally to non-region subscribers); default membership predicate returns `false` until Wave 5n wires `IStageRegionIndex`. **Konsist +2 rules** (45→47): `inventory domain stays pure (ADR-0111)` (forbids Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin in `core.domain.inventory.*`) + `inventory and world ports stay vendor-neutral (ADR-0111)` (same forbidden set across `core.application.port.outbound.inventory.*` + `core.application.port.outbound.world.*`). **No Postgres migration** (event domain; existing audit-log writer is the persistence path). **Plugin wiring** (`AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`): 3 new `services.register<...>(impl, priority = 0)` lines placed after Wave 5pve-survival block and before Wave 5p advancement / dialog / tutorial block (`IInventoryMutator` + `IBulkInventoryOps` + `IRegionEventFilter`); loot table / kit resolvers seeded with `{ _ -> null }` lambdas — return `UnknownLootTable` / `UnknownKit` until Wave 5pve-content / Wave 5e registries land (single-line lambda swap). **Tests**: 8 new test classes (45+ tests — `ClearScopeTest` × 3 / `SlotTypeLockRuleTest` × 4 / `KitIdTest` × 4 / `InventoryTransferTargetTest` × 3 / `WorldEventTest` × 9 / `InMemoryInventoryMutatorTest` × 17 / `InMemoryBulkInventoryOpsTest` × 5 / `InMemoryRegionEventFilterTest` × 4) — all Bukkit-free pure-JVM. `./gradlew check` + 7 plugin `shadowJar` 全 green. Net catalog: +3 rows (World event domain expansion 🔌 + Per-region event filter 🔌 + Inventory mutation primitives 🔌). ADR-0111 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 5n / Phase 4**: Paper `BukkitEventAdapter` wrap of `PlayerMoveEvent` / `PlayerTeleportEvent` / `PlayerInteractEvent` / `ChunkLoadEvent` / `ChunkUnloadEvent` / `WeatherChangeEvent` / `TimeSkipEvent` w/ per-event throttling (Wave 6); `PaperInventoryMutator` priority-100 overlay over Bukkit `PlayerInventory` w/ max-stack-size enforcement (Wave 6); `IStageRegionIndex` wire into `InMemoryRegionEventFilter.regionMembership` (Wave 5n follow-up); `ILootTableRegistry` / `IKitRegistry` dedicated ports replacing in-memory bulk-ops resolver lambdas (Wave 5pve-content / Wave 5e respectively). **Single commit on branch `wave-5world-events`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5combat-depth (2026-05-24): Combat depth foundation — combo + focus-fire + ranged target lock + boss DSL extension (ADR-0105 Accepted, **fast-forwarded from originally-planned Phase 2-3 Wave 8x** as Wave 5g §"Discovery 候補" combat-section foundation work). Pulls forward the YAML schema + domain vocabulary for the 4 combat-depth axes catalogued in `.claude/plans/idea-combat-depth.md` so Wave 6 Paper sweep has stable targets. **Domain** (`combat.domain.{combo,focus,targetlock,event}.*`, 17 new files): Combo subdomain (`ComboRouteId` lower-kebab + `ComboStep` sealed Hit/SkillCast w/ tick anchor + `ComboState` per-`(playerId, weapon)` w/ 60-tick default decay + `isExpiredAt(nowTick)` + `ComboPattern` sealed 3-variant `HitCount` / `SkillSequence` / `TimingWindow` + `ComboRoute` weapon×pattern→finisher w/ `bonusDamageMultiplier` cap 5.0 + `ComboMatcher` pure object); Focus-fire subdomain (`FocusFireConfig` tuning 3-attacker / 100-tick / 1.15x cap 2.0 + `FocusFireContribution` per-attacker rolling + `FocusFireState` per-victim aggregate w/ `isActive(cfg, nowTick)` computed activation); Target-lock subdomain (`TargetLockConfig` 10-tick acquisition / 50-block / 5° / 40-tick lifetime / 1.25x cap 2.0 + `TargetLockState` single-target invariant + `isLocked` + `isLockExpiredAt(cfg, nowTick)`); 6 combat domain events (`ComboStepRecorded` + `ComboRouteCompleted` w/ `bonusDamageMultiplier` value + `FocusFireActivated` w/ attackers + totalDamage + `FocusFireDeactivated` w/ durationTicks + `TargetLockAcquired` + `TargetLockReleased` w/ sealed `Reason` 4-variant Expired/WeaponSwitched/TargetLost/ManualRelease). Boss DSL extension (`core.domain.mob.boss.*`, +3 files): `BossPhaseTransition` sealed widened from 1→5 variants — adds `TimerExpired(elapsedTicks)` + `MultiPlayerThreshold(minPlayers)` + `MechanicCompleted(mechanicSkillId: SkillId)` (reuses skill registry per "every mechanic is a player-cast skill" framing) + `AllOf(children)` requires ≥2 children w/ shared from-to + flat (no nested AllOf — disjunction expressed by listing two top-level triggers, lifecycle short-circuits on first match). `BossPhaseEvaluationContext` read-only record (`currentPhase, hpFraction, nowTick, phaseEnteredAtTick, attackerCount, completedMechanics`) + `BossPhaseTransitionEvaluator` pure object `fires(trigger, context): Boolean` — wave-5pve-boss `InMemoryBossLifecycleService.onDamage` keeps HpThreshold-only inline eval (silently no-ops other variants) until Wave 6b switches to the evaluator. **Application** (`combat.application.*`): 3 outbound ports (`IComboTracker` registerRoute/recordHit/recordSkillCast/stateOf(nowTick)/reset + `IFocusFireTracker` configure/recordHit/bonusMultiplierFor + `ITargetLockController` configure/tickAim/breakAim/release/consumeBonusFor — all session-scoped per ADR-0105 §"No persistence for trackers") + 3 InMemory impls (`InMemoryComboTracker` route-map + state-map + one-shot match-reset + step publishing + `InMemoryFocusFireTracker` victim-keyed state + edge-detected Active/Inactive transitions + in-line stale prune + `InMemoryTargetLockController` player-keyed state + armed-bonus `Map<(player, target), Boolean>` + per-Reason drop publish) + `ComboRouteYamlConfig` discriminated DTO (hit_count/skill_sequence/timing_window) + `ComboRouteContents : YamlDynamicContents<ComboRouteId, ComboRoute, ComboRouteYamlConfig>` (auto-forward into tracker on bootstrap + reload-aware via `reloadRegistry.register`). `BossYamlConfig.BossPhaseTransitionYaml` extended with `elapsed_ticks` / `min_players` / `mechanic_skill_id` / `children` discriminated decoders + `all_of` child from-to inheritance from parent so YAML stays flat. **Lifecycle wiring**: `InMemoryBossLifecycleService.onDamage` `when` expanded to exhaustively handle all 5 variants (HpThreshold = real eval, others = `false` placeholder w/ documenting comment pointing at Wave 6b adoption + `BossPhaseTransitionEvaluator`). **Konsist +1 rule** (38→39): `combat depth domain must not import event bus or broadcaster` — `combat.domain.{combo,focus,targetlock}.*` must stay pure value records; publishing belongs in `application/service/*Tracker.kt`, subscribing in `application/eventbus/listeners/`. Imports forbidden: `.IDomainEventBus` / `.IBroadcaster` / `org.slf4j.*` / `kotlinx.serialization.*`. **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCombatPlugin.onEnable` registers 3 new ports at p=0 (`IComboTracker`, `IFocusFireTracker`, `ITargetLockController`) + bootstraps `ComboRouteContents` from `content/combat-depth/combos/` + forwards every loaded `ComboRoute` into the tracker + registers combo content with the reload dispatcher. `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` logs that `BossPhaseTransitionEvaluator` surface is available for Wave 6b lifecycle adoption (no service registration needed — pure object). **No V40 migration** — combo / focus-fire / target-lock are session-scoped tactical state per ADR-0105; no persistence justified yet. **Content**: 3 PoC combo YAMLs (`content/combat-depth/combos/{example-sword-finisher,example-spell-rotation,example-timing-window}.yaml` covering HitCount / SkillSequence / TimingWindow patterns) + 1 forward-looking boss YAML (`content/combat-depth/boss-extensions/example-extended-boss.yaml` exercising all 4 new BossPhaseTransition variants — NOT auto-loaded; lives as schema reference until Wave 6b adoption) + `content/combat-depth/README.md` schema notes. **Tests**: 9 new test classes (~50 tests — `ComboStateTest` × 6 (construction invariants + expiry edge case) / `ComboMatcherTest` × 7 (3 pattern variants + wrong-weapon reject + bonus cap reject) / `InMemoryComboTrackerTest` × 7 (record + weapon-switch reset + expiry + route fire + state reset + unregister) / `FocusFireStateTest` × 7 (activation threshold + window expiry + duplicate-attacker reject + victim-as-attacker reject + total damage + config cap) / `InMemoryFocusFireTrackerTest` × 8 (single-attacker no-op + 3-attacker activate + bonus contributor-only + below-threshold bonus 1.0 + window aging Deactivate + reset silent + self-attack reject) / `TargetLockStateTest` × 4 (acquisition build-up null lock + locked survival until lifetime + lockedSince ≤ lastAim invariant + config cap) / `InMemoryTargetLockControllerTest` × 7 (acquisition threshold + bonus one-shot + WeaponSwitched drop + TargetLost drop + lifetime Expired + breakAim reset + ManualRelease) / `BossPhaseTransitionEvaluatorTest` × 8 (5 trigger variants + AllOf child enforcement + nested-AllOf reject + context phaseEntered ≤ now) / `BossYamlConfigCombatDepthTest` × 6 (5 type decoders + child from-to inheritance + missing-field error + unknown-type error) / `ComboRouteYamlConfigTest` × 5 (Kaml round-trip 3 patterns + unknown type + missing field)). `./gradlew check` green (all 9 plugin Konsist tests pass including new combat-depth rule); `:plugin:combat:shadowJar` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` green. RTM rows added: +4 new Category 1 entries (combo / focus-fire / target-lock / boss DSL extension), no denominator change for HP-threshold-only boss already present. **Catalog: +4 🔌 rows** + 1 🟨 row (Boss DSL extension — vocabulary ready, eval Wave 6b). ADR-0105 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 6b**: Paper damage-pipeline subscriber that applies combo bonus multiplier on next strike (needs Wave 6 damage path), Paper look-vector tick sampler that drives `tickAim` / `breakAim` (needs Paper Player.location + entity raycast), HUD scope reticle (depends on Wave 5j custom HUD path), `IBossLifecycleService.onDamage` switch to `BossPhaseTransitionEvaluator.fires` (Wave 6b — needs `phaseEnteredAtTick` / `attackerCount` / `completedMechanics` state in `BossInstance`), `recordMechanicCompletion(mobId, skillId)` lifecycle API for MechanicCompleted trigger driver. **Single commit on branch `wave-5combat-depth`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5gui-depth (2026-05-27): GUI animation + Modal + Tooltip-extension foundation — GUI depth primitive (ADR-0114 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 6 closing alongside Wave 5animation + Wave 5y-ext). Closes the **GUI depth gap** catalogued in `.claude/plans/wave-5g-core-hardening-giggly-sunset.md` §Round 6 audit so Wave 5flex profile card slot reveal / Wave 5refine refinement toast / Wave 5e quickbar swap / Wave 5bet odds tick / Wave 5refine refinement-tier per-viewer tooltip all gain a Core port they can call instead of writing ad-hoc adapter code in Wave 6. **Domain** (`core.domain.guidepth.*` 10 new files): `AnimationHandle` value class (blank-id reject) + `SlotAnimation` sealed 4-variant `Fade(fromAlpha/toAlpha∈0.0..1.0, durationTicks, easing) / Slide(fromSlot/toSlot: SlotIndex, durationTicks, easing) / Bounce(amplitude>0, durationTicks) / Pulse(scaleMin>0, scaleMax>=scaleMin, durationTicks)` **reusing Wave 5h Easing enum (32 standard curves) — no new curve added** + `HoverAnimation` sealed 2-variant `Highlight(overlayColorArgb, overlayAlpha∈0.0..1.0, durationTicks) / Scale(peakScale>1, durationTicks, easing)` + `ModalHandle` value class + `AnchorPosition` sealed 5-variant `Center / TopLeft / TopRight / Cursor / NearSlot(slotIndex)` + `GuiModalSpec` sealed 5-variant `Confirmation(titleKey, messageKey, onConfirm: GuiIntent, onCancel: GuiIntent) / Notification(titleKey, messageKey, dismissAfterTicks?: Long >=0) / QuickbarSelector(slots: List<GuiSlot> non-empty <=18, onSelect: GuiIntent) / TooltipOverlay(content: TooltipContent, anchorPos: AnchorPosition) / Custom(content: GuiSpec)` **reusing Wave 5h GuiSpec/GuiSlot (no parallel GUI system) + GuiIntent value callbacks (no lambdas in domain)** + `TooltipLine` sealed 3-variant `Text(messageKey, color: HudColor = WHITE) / Image(textureRef: HudGlyphRef) / ProgressBar(percent∈0.0..1.0, fillColor: HudColor)` **reusing Wave 5j HudGlyphRef + HudColor (tooltip and HUD share vocabulary)** + `TooltipContent(lines: List<TooltipLine> non-empty)` + `TooltipScope` sealed 4-variant `AllItems / ByRarity(Rarity) / ByCategory(ItemCategory) / ByItem(ItemId)` + `ItemCategory` value class free-form lower-kebab-case (`ItemId` 同じ regex). **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 5 new): `SlotAnimationStarted(playerId, guiId, slotIndex, handle, durationTicks, animationKind: String, atTick)` w/ `animationKind ∈ fade/slide/bounce/pulse/hover/scroll` (snake_case discriminator) + `SlotAnimationCompleted(playerId, guiId, slotIndex, handle, atTick)` + `SlotAnimationCancelled(playerId, guiId, slotIndex, handle, reasonKind: String, atTick)` w/ `reasonKind ∈ explicit/gui_closed/superseded` (`CinematicEnded` 同 pattern) + `ModalShown(playerId, handle, modalKind: String, atTick)` w/ `modalKind ∈ confirmation/notification/quickbar_selector/tooltip_overlay/custom` + `ModalDismissed(playerId, handle, reasonKind: String, atTick)` w/ `reasonKind ∈ explicit/bulk_dismiss/auto_expired/player_quit`. SlotAnimationCompleted と SlotAnimationCancelled は同一 handle で **mutually exclusive**. **Application ports** (`core.application.port.outbound.guidepth.*`, 4 new): `IGuiAnimationController` animateSlot/animateHover/animateScroll/cancel (`GuiAnimationError` sealed `UnknownPlayer/InvalidDuration/Platform`) — `(player, gui, slot)` triple per-uniqueness、supersede 時に prior handle を `reasonKind = superseded` で fire + `IGuiModalGateway` showModal/dismissModal/dismissAllForPlayer/activeModals (`GuiModalError` sealed) — show-order 保持、bulk_dismiss は per-handle で fire + `ITooltipExtensionProvider` fun-interface `extend(item, viewer): List<TooltipLine>` — side-effect-free、per-tick render path 想定 + `ITooltipRegistry` register(scope, provider)/lookup(item, viewer)/rarityOf(item)/categoriesOf(item) — bucket `AllItems → ByRarity → ByCategory → ByItem` order、within bucket は registration order. All `Result<T, sealed Error>` — Konsist が application 層の raw throwable 禁止を強制. **In-mem services** (`core.application.service.guidepth.*`, 3 file): `InMemoryGuiAnimationController(eventBus, tickClock)` w/ active 2-way map (handle ↔ ActiveAnimation) で per-triple uniqueness 保証 + supersede fire + scheduler 持たない (`simulateComplete(handle)` test helper expose、Paper adapter Wave 6 で `IScheduler.runLater(durationTicks)` 注入 — Wave 5cinematic `InMemoryCameraGateway.freeze` precedent) + `activeCount()` test helper + `InMemoryGuiModalGateway(eventBus, tickClock)` w/ byHandle + byPlayer CopyOnWriteArrayList 二重 index で show-order 保持 + `dismissAllForPlayer` bulk fire (per handle で `reasonKind = bulk_dismiss`) + scheduler 持たない (`simulateAutoExpire(handle)` test helper、Paper adapter Wave 6 で `IScheduler.runLater(dismissAfterTicks)` 注入) + `InMemoryTooltipRegistry(rarityResolver, categoriesResolver)` w/ 4 bucket (allItems/byRarity/byCategory/byItem) を CopyOnWriteArrayList で保持 (hot path lookup が register と race しない). **Phase 1 wiring default**: `rarityResolver = { null }` + `categoriesResolver = { emptyList() }` — Wave 5refine / Wave 5flex / Wave 5pve-survival hookup まで `ByRarity` / `ByCategory` scope は fire しない (`AllItems` / `ByItem` は無条件で動作). **No DB migration** — gui-depth は session-scoped per ADR-0114; main 最大 V43 不変. **Konsist +1 rule** (47→48): `gui-depth domain stays pure (ADR-0114)` — `core.domain.guidepth.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin. JOML は許可 (Wave 5h-fix precedent — pure-math、Phase 4 web admin renderer のための余地). **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` registers 3 new ports at p=0 (`IGuiAnimationController` + `IGuiModalGateway` + `ITooltipRegistry`) placed after Wave 5cinematic block + 3 entries appended to `SERVICE_NAMES` companion list. **Tests**: 9 new test classes (53 tests — `SlotAnimationTest` × 7 / `HoverAnimationTest` × 3 / `GuiModalSpecTest` × 6 / `TooltipLineTest` × 3 / `ItemCategoryTest` × 5 / `HandleTest` × 4 / `InMemoryGuiAnimationControllerTest` × 9 / `InMemoryGuiModalGatewayTest` × 8 / `InMemoryTooltipRegistryTest` × 8). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 48 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Catalog**: +1 row (GUI animation + Modal + Tooltip-extension foundation, 🔌 port skeleton — Wave 6 で Paper EntityMetadata/armor-stand display-entity animation + anvil-prompt/boss-bar/overlay-packet modal + tooltip render-hook adapters at priority=100). ADR-0114 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 5refine / Wave 5flex / Wave 5pve-survival / Phase 4**: `PaperGuiAnimationController` over `EntityMetadata` alpha interpolation + armor-stand display-entity tween for Slide (Wave 6), `PaperGuiModalGateway` over anvil-prompt + boss-bar + custom overlay packet stack (Wave 6) + auto-dismiss `IScheduler.runLater(dismissAfterTicks)` (Wave 6), Paper tooltip render-hook calling `ITooltipRegistry.lookup(item, viewer)` per render tick + cache invalidation on lookup-result change (Wave 6), `rarityResolver` / `categoriesResolver` hookup to `IItemRegistry.lookup(item).spec.rarity` + `IItemCategoryRegistry` lookup (Wave 5refine for rarity / Wave 5pve-survival for category bootstrap), `IItemCategoryRegistry` dedicated port + YAML loader if category management proves load-bearing (Phase 4 follow-up), `IGuiAnimationController` z-index control + modal stacking layer (Phase 4 — `GuiModalSpec.zIndex: Int = 0` field add + `dismissAllForPlayer` rework, breaking change受容 by Phase 1 PoC posture), GUI Animation Replay (Phase 4 — modal-aware ReplayEvent variant). **Single commit on branch `wave-5gui-depth`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5cinematic (2026-05-27): Camera + Music gateway foundation — cinematic primitive (ADR-0110 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 5 closing alongside Wave 5effects + Wave 5world-events). Closes the **Camera + Music port gap** catalogued in `.claude/plans/wave-5g-core-hardening-giggly-sunset.md` §Round 5 audit so Wave 5pve-boss boss reveal cutscene / Wave 5p tutorial intro / Wave 5j HUD music sync / Wave 5annihilation phase-transition BGM all gain a Core port they can call instead of writing ad-hoc adapter code in Wave 6. **Domain** (`core.domain.cinematic.*` 8 new files + `core.domain.music.*` 6 new files): `Color(r,g,b,a)` RGBA value w/ `BLACK/WHITE/TRANSPARENT` companion + `CameraTarget` sealed 4-variant `Position(Vec3) / Entity(EntityHandle) / Player(PlayerId) / RelativeToPlayer(Vec3 offset)` + `CameraHandle` / `CinematicHandle` / `ScreenEffectHandle` value classes (blank-id reject) + `CameraWaypoint(target, durationTicksToNext: Long, easing: Easing)` **reusing Wave 5h Easing enum (32 standard curves) — no new curve added** + `CinematicPath(waypoints)` non-empty enforce w/ `totalDurationTicks` = sum of all-but-last + `FreelookSession(playerId, anchorPosition)` value pair; `MusicTrackId` value class `[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*` lowercase-kebab matching `MatchId` discipline + `MusicTrack(id, displayName, source, license, mood, defaultVolume∈0.0..1.0, loop, durationTicks?)` w/ non-negative duration enforce + `MusicSource` sealed `ResourcePack(path) / ExternalUrl(url) / InGameNote` + `MusicLicense` sealed `RoyaltyFree(attribution?) / Licensed(licenseRef) / UserUploaded(uploaderId)` — Phase 4 UGC tier ready + `MusicMood` enum 7-variant `LOBBY/MATCH/BOSS_REVEAL/HIGH_INTENSITY/VICTORY/DEFEAT/CINEMATIC` + `MusicHandle` value class. **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 5 new): `CinematicStarted(playerId, handle, totalDurationTicks, atTick)` + `CinematicEnded(playerId, handle, reasonKind: String, atTick)` w/ snake_case discriminator `completed/cancelled/failed` (`IslandUnloaded.reasonKind` pattern) + `CameraReleased(playerId, handle, atTick)` + `MatchMusicChanged(matchId, previousTrack?, newTrack, atTick)` distinct-tracks enforce + `AdaptiveMusicTriggered(matchId, newMood, selectedTrack, reasonKind, atTick)` w/ `reasonKind ∈ hp_threshold/phase_transition/player_count/manual`. **Application ports** (`core.application.port.outbound.{cinematic,music}.*`, 8 new): `ICameraGateway` lockCamera/panCamera/shakeCamera/zoomCamera/releaseCamera/freeze/activeHandle (`CameraError` sealed) + `ICinematicPathPlayer` play/cancel/active (`CinematicPlaybackError` sealed) + `IFreelookGateway` enableFreelook/disableFreelook/session/all (`FreelookError` sealed) + `IScreenEffectGateway` fade/freeze/cancel/active (`ScreenEffectError` sealed) + `IMusicGateway` play/crossfade/duck/unduck/stop/active (`MusicError` sealed) + `IMusicTrackRegistry : Contents<MusicTrackId, MusicTrack>` + `byMood(mood)` + `IPerMatchBgmService` setTrack/stop/current (`PerMatchBgmError` sealed) + `IAdaptiveBgmService` trigger(matchId, newMood, reasonKind) (`AdaptiveBgmError` sealed). All `Result<T, sealed Error>` — Konsist が application 層の raw throwable 禁止を強制. **In-mem services** (`core.application.service.{cinematic,music}.*`, 7 file): `InMemoryCameraGateway(eventBus, tickClock)` w/ active handle per player + CameraReleased publish on releaseCamera (freeze auto-release is Paper adapter responsibility — in-memory deliberately scheduler-free to avoid task-leak on gateway replace) + `InMemoryCinematicPathPlayer(eventBus, tickClock)` lifecycle-only (waypoint advancement = Paper adapter) + `InMemoryFreelookGateway` session per player replace-silent + `InMemoryScreenEffectGateway` per-player handle set w/ cancel-idempotent + `InMemoryMusicGateway(tracks)` registry-validated UnknownTrack early + per-handle ducked/stopped state + `InMemoryPerMatchBgmService(tracks, eventBus, tickClock)` setTrack-same no-op + MatchMusicChanged publish + `InMemoryAdaptiveBgmService(tracks, perMatch, eventBus, tickClock)` byMood first-track selection (deterministic test-friendly; weighted/round-robin = Phase 4 follow-up) + reasonKind validate + silent skip on empty candidate set. **Content + YAML config**: `MusicTrackContents : YamlDynamicContents<MusicTrackId, MusicTrack, MusicTrackYamlConfig>` (`/astera reload music` 経由 hot-swap、`IReloadableContentsRegistry` 登録) + `MusicTrackYamlConfig` kaml DTO (`application.config.music.*` — Konsist が domain で `kotlinx.serialization` 禁止) for wire format `{id, display_name, source{kind, path|url}, license{kind, attribution?, license_ref?, uploader_id?}, mood, default_volume, loop, duration_ticks}`. **Sample content**: `content/music/{lobby-loop,match-default,boss-reveal-strings,high-intensity-drums}.yaml` (4 royalty-free placeholder tracks covering LOBBY/MATCH/BOSS_REVEAL/HIGH_INTENSITY moods). **No DB migration** — cinematic + music are session-scoped per ADR-0110; main 最大 V43 不変. **Konsist +1 rule** (45→46): `cinematic and music domain stays pure (ADR-0110)` — `core.domain.{cinematic,music}.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin / **JOML** (JOML 禁止は Easing pure-double で十分のため明示的に domain surface 最小化). **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` registers 8 new ports at p=0 (4 cinematic gateways + `IMusicGateway` + `IMusicTrackRegistry` + `IPerMatchBgmService` + `IAdaptiveBgmService`) + bootstraps `MusicTrackContents` from `content/music/` + joins reload dispatcher + 8 entries appended to `SERVICE_NAMES` companion list. **Tests**: 13 new test classes (66 tests — `CinematicPathTest` × 6 / `ColorTest` × 5 / `CameraHandleTest` × 3 / `MusicTrackTest` × 5 / `MusicTrackIdTest` × 6 / `InMemoryCameraGatewayTest` × 9 / `InMemoryCinematicPathPlayerTest` × 4 / `InMemoryFreelookGatewayTest` × 4 / `InMemoryScreenEffectGatewayTest` × 5 / `InMemoryMusicGatewayTest` × 8 / `InMemoryPerMatchBgmServiceTest` × 5 / `InMemoryAdaptiveBgmServiceTest` × 4 / `MusicTrackContentsTest` × 2). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 46 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Catalog**: +1 row (Camera + Music gateway foundation, 🔌 port skeleton — Wave 6 で Paper camera-packet + `Player.playSound` + overlay-packet adapters at priority=100). ADR-0110 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Phase 4**: `PaperCameraGateway` over Bukkit `CameraEntity` + spectator-mode proxy (Wave 6), `PaperCinematicPathPlayer` waypoint-scheduler driven by `IScheduler.runRepeatingTicks` (Wave 6), `PaperFreelookGateway` movement-listener cancel (Wave 6), `PaperScreenEffectGateway` overlay packet + input capture (Wave 6), `PaperMusicGateway` `Player.playSound` from `MusicSource.ResourcePack.path` (Wave 6), per-participant fan-out subscriber bridging `MatchMusicChanged` → `IMusicGateway.play` across all match participants (Wave 5gamemode IGameMode lifecycle hook — separate wave), per-viewer BGM override (Wave 5y-ext — out of scope by intent), `MusicSource.ExternalUrl` streaming playback path (Phase 4 UGC), weighted / round-robin / per-team adaptive selector (Phase 4), `CinematicPathId` + YAML loader for authoring (Phase 4 Tournament highlight reel). **Single commit on branch `wave-5cinematic`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

`IslandPlot` reusing Wave 5n `core.domain.stage.Region` (no parallel Cuboid type) w/ `(PlotId / IslandId / PlayerId / Region / Set<PlayerId> buildAllowed)` + owner-in-buildAllowed invariant + `grantBuild / revokeBuild` (owner-revoke rejected) + `GatheringSpotKind` sealed 4-variant `Fishing/Mining/Logging/Digging` + `GatheringSkillTrigger` sealed 4-variant `FishingRodCast/PickaxeSwing/AxeSwing/ShovelDig` (deliberately separate trigger family from combat `SkillTrigger`) + `GatheringSpot` author-defined `(id / displayName / kind / trigger / world / position / yields / cooldownTicks)` w/ kind↔trigger compatibility matrix enforced + `GatheringYield` `(itemId / weight / minCount..maxCount)` + `GatheringProgress` per-(player, spot) w/ `isOffCooldown(now, cooldownTicks)` / `recordHarvest(now)` / `first(...)` factory + `GameModeContext` sealed `Lobby / PvpMatch / PveWorld / SurvivalIsland` (4-tier world bucket) + `PartyInvitation` runtime-only w/ inviter≠invitee + expiresAt>createdAt + `isExpired(now)`. **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 4 new): `PartyIslandCreated` + `PartyMemberInvited` + `GatheringSpotHarvested(rolledYields: Map<ItemId, Int>)` + `IslandUnloaded(reasonKind: String — snake_case discriminator `leader_offline / admin_unload / archival_freeze`)`. **Application ports** (`core.application.port.outbound.survival.*`, 9 new): `IPartyIslandRepository` (in-mem + Postgres p=100) + `IIslandPlotRepository` (同) + `IPartyInvitationRepository` (in-mem only, Phase 4 で永続化) + `IGatheringProgressRepository` (in-mem + Postgres) + `IGatheringSpotRegistry : Contents<GatheringSpotId, GatheringSpot>` (YAML-loaded `GatheringSpotContents` extends `YamlDynamicContents`) + `IIslandTemplateRegistry` (in-mem; Phase 4 で YAML 化) + `IIslandPersistence` `save / load / place / exists` (schematic-based snapshot 抽象) + `IInventoryShareGuard` `canCarryAcrossMode(itemId, from, to)` + `IGatheringYieldRoller` (テスト用 seed-stable roller 接続可) + `IPartyIslandManager` (lifecycle 高レベル façade `create / load / unload / visit`) + `IIslandPlotManager` (plot lifecycle façade). **Use cases** (`core.application.usecase.survival.*`, 8 use case + 4 error sealed): `CreatePartyIslandUseCase` (template resolve + party 1-island enforce + status=Active + `PartyIslandCreated` publish; maxMembers は template defaultMaxMembers にクランプ) + `InvitePartyMemberUseCase` (inviter party member 確認 + invitee 非 member + party-full + duplicate-invitation 検査 + 5 分 TTL + `PartyMemberInvited` publish) + `AcceptPartyInvitationUseCase` (invitee 一致 + 有効期限 + party-full 最終確認 + party 集合更新 + invitation consume) + `LoadPartyIslandUseCase` (Active 短絡 lastActiveAt bump + Unloaded→schematic place + Active 復帰 / Archived は reject) + `UnloadPartyIslandUseCase` (idempotent on already-Unloaded — event 二重発火しない / schematic save 失敗時 PlatformFailure + `IslandUnloaded` publish) + `VisitPartyIslandUseCase` (`IslandVisibility` 3 分岐 + HOT status enforce) + `AssignIslandPlotUseCase` (1 member 1 plot + +X 軸 line layout `spawnOffset + index * (plotSize.x + 4)` + 4 ブロック gap) + `HarvestGatheringSpotUseCase`
