(per-spot in-instance `ConcurrentHashMap` cooldown + per-player progress repo cooldown + kind↔trigger match + yield roll + progress upsert + `GatheringSpotHarvested` publish). All `Result<T, sealed Error>` — Konsist が application 層の raw throwable 禁止を強制 (`IslandError / PlotError / InvitationError / GatheringError` 各 sealed)。**In-mem services** (`core.application.service.survival.*`, 9 file): `InMemoryPartyIslandRepository` / `InMemoryIslandPlotRepository` / `InMemoryPartyInvitationRepository` (+ `purgeExpired(now)` helper) / `InMemoryGatheringProgressRepository` ((playerId, spotId) key) / `InMemoryIslandTemplateRegistry` / `InMemoryIslandPersistence` (`ISchematicLoader` 注入、`islands/<id>.schem` deterministic mint) / `InMemoryInventoryShareGuard` (default rule: `SurvivalIsland→PvpMatch` と `PveWorld→PvpMatch` で item carry deny、それ以外 allow — currency tier は item 扱いではないので allow) / `UniformGatheringYieldRoller` (weighted-pick、`java.util.Random` 注入で deterministic test 可) / `DefaultPartyIslandManager` + `DefaultIslandPlotManager` (use case 集約 façade). **Content + YAML config**: `GatheringSpotContents : YamlDynamicContents<GatheringSpotId, GatheringSpot, GatheringSpotYamlConfig>` (`/astera-survival reload gathering` 経由 hot-swap、`IReloadableContentsRegistry` 登録) + `GatheringSpotYamlConfig` kaml DTO (`application.config.survival.*` — Konsist が domain で `kotlinx.serialization` 禁止) で wire 形式 `{id, display_name, kind, trigger, world, position, cooldown_ticks, yields[item_id/weight/min_count/max_count]}`. **Sample content**: `content/gathering/{oak-grove,iron-vein,fishing-pond}.yaml` (3 spot covering logging / mining / fishing kinds、すべて `survival-default` world). **Persistence**: V41 `party_islands` (PK `island_id VARCHAR(64)` + UNIQUE `party_id` 1-island-per-party + status CHECK `(Active/Unloaded/Archived)` + `visibility_kind` discriminator + `visibility_payload JSONB` w/ ADR-0014 hybrid 規則 [discriminator 頻繁 query / friend list visit 時のみ scan] + 3 index `party_unique / status / last_active`) + V42 `island_plots` (PK `plot_id UUID` + FK `island_id` ON DELETE CASCADE + 6 flat column min/max bounds for B-tree range index [cuboid position→plot lookup] + `build_allowed JSONB` UUID-as-string array + UNIQUE `(island_id, owner_id)` 1-plot-per-member) + V43 `gathering_progress` (composite PK `(player_id, spot_id)` + `total_harvests >= 0` CHECK + 2 index `player_last DESC / spot` for leaderboards、`gathering_spots` への FK は YAML SSOT 方針で意図的に張らない). Exposed tables `PartyIslandsTable / IslandPlotsTable / GatheringProgressTable` (新 package `persistence.table.survival.*`) + JSONB DTO `IslandVisibilityJson / IslandPlotAllowedJson` + Postgres repos `PostgresPartyIslandRepository / PostgresIslandPlotRepository / PostgresGatheringProgressRepository` (`repository.survival.*`) all priority=100 overlay in `AsteraPersistencePlugin`. **Konsist +1 rule** (44→45): `survival domain stays pure (ADR-0107)` — `core.domain.survival.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin. **i18n**: 5 セクションの全 keys を `en.yaml` + `ja.yaml` 両方に追加 (parity test green) — `astera.social.party.error.*` (Wave 5e から dangling だった `PartyError.messageKey` の i18n 接続 5 key) + `astera.social.island.error.*` 7 key + `astera.social.island.plot.error.*` 4 key + `astera.social.island.invite.error.*` 8 key + `astera.survival.gathering.error.*` 4 key. **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` registers 9 new ports at p=0 (`IPartyIslandRepository / IIslandPlotRepository / IGatheringProgressRepository / IPartyInvitationRepository / IIslandTemplateRegistry / IIslandPersistence / IInventoryShareGuard / IGatheringYieldRoller / IGatheringSpotRegistry`) + bootstraps `GatheringSpotContents` from `content/gathering/` + builds 5 use cases + 2 manager façades (`DefaultPartyIslandManager / DefaultIslandPlotManager`) + registers `IPartyIslandManager / IIslandPlotManager` ports + joins reload dispatcher. `AsteraPersistencePlugin.onEnable` registers 3 Postgres overlays at p=100. **Tests**: 12 new test classes (79 tests — `PartyIslandTest` × 7 / `IslandPlotTest` × 5 / `GatheringSpotTest` × 7 / `GatheringProgressTest` × 5 / `CreatePartyIslandUseCaseTest` × 5 / `InvitePartyMemberUseCaseTest` × 6 / `AcceptPartyInvitationUseCaseTest` × 5 / `UnloadAndLoadPartyIslandUseCaseTest` × 7 / `VisitPartyIslandUseCaseTest` × 6 / `AssignIslandPlotUseCaseTest` × 5 / `HarvestGatheringSpotUseCaseTest` × 5 / `InMemoryInventoryShareGuardTest` × 6 / `InMemoryIslandPersistenceTest` × 5). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Catalog**: +1 row (Skyblock party-shared instance foundation, 🔌 port skeleton — Wave 6 で gathering listener + Paper schematic snapshotter + leader-offline sweep + region permission 連動). ADR-0107 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Phase 4**: Paper-side gathering listener (Wave 6 — `HarvestGatheringSpotUseCase` 駆動), `PaperSchematicLoader` (Wave 6 — FAWE adapter、`InMemoryIslandPersistence` 差し替え), leader-offline detector sweep (Wave 6 — `UnloadPartyIslandUseCase` 駆動), Velocity proxy 経由 survival server 分離 (Wave 6 — ADR-0084), island template YAML 化 (Phase 4 — `IIslandTemplateRegistry` を `YamlDynamicContents` に差し替え), archival sweep job 7 day idle → `IslandStatus.Archived` (Phase 4), per-player housing `idea-personal-housing.md` (Phase 4), Warp / Home / TPA teleport 系 (idea md §G、Phase 3 別 wave). **Single commit on branch `wave-5pve-survival`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5y-ext (2026-05-27): Per-viewer state extension foundation — NPC packet + custom block + GUI overrides (ADR-0112 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 6 closing alongside Wave 5animation + Wave 5gui-depth). Closes the per-viewer **state divergence** gap that Wave 5y (ADR-0095) had left open (Wave 5y covered hide/show binary only) — Wave 5pve mob skin pool / Wave 5flex glow-per-viewer / Wave 5h spy & disguise ability / Wave 5p tutorial highlight NPC all gain a Core port they can call instead of waiting for Wave 6. **Domain** (`core.domain.perviewer.*` 5 new files NEW package): `NpcAttributeKind` sealed 4-variant `Scale(value>0.0, ≤16.0 matching MC 1.20.5+ cap) / Glowing(on) / Invisible(on) / NameVisible(on)` (sealed not enum so future variants can carry structured payload like `Pose(...)`) + `NpcViewerOverride(skin: NpcSkin?, displayName: MessageKey?, equipment: Map<EquipSlot, ItemId?>, attributes: Map<String, NpcAttributeKind>)` snapshot data class w/ `isEmpty` accessor — equipment-map value is `ItemId?` not `Optional` so null value = "explicit clear", absence = "no override" + `PerViewerOverrideError` sealed 4-variant `UnknownViewer / UnknownTarget / InvalidOverride(reason) / BackendError(reason)` (distinct from `VisibilityError` because override has different failure cells than visibility toggle) + `GuiHandle(guiId: GuiId, viewer: PlayerId)` value pair (domain-pure addressing token; platform `IGuiAdapter` mints on `openFor`) + `ClearReason` sealed 3-variant `PlayerQuit / Explicit / TargetDespawned` (drives `PerViewerOverridesCleared.reason` payload). **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 4 new): `NpcOverrideApplied(viewer, npc, snapshot)` (post-mutation snapshot; subscribers needing before/after must cache themselves) + `BlockOverrideApplied(viewer, world, position, overrideType?, overrideTexture?, hidden)` w/ init-block exclusivity (exactly one of type / texture / hidden non-default per emission — so subscribers can `when` over a single dimension) + `GuiOverrideApplied(handle, slotIndex?, slotOverride?, titleOverride?)` w/ init-block `slotOverride.xor(titleOverride)` + `slotOverride` requires `slotIndex` / `titleOverride` forbids it + `PerViewerOverridesCleared(viewer, gatewayFamily, reason, clearedCount)` w/ string-discriminator `gatewayFamily ∈ {npc, block, gui}` + KNOWN_FAMILIES set + init-block validates family ∈ set + clearedCount ≥ 1 (gateways holding no state for viewer must NOT emit). **Application ports** (`core.application.port.outbound.perviewer.{npc,block,gui}.*`, 3 new sub-packages): `IPerViewerNpcStateGateway` `overrideSkin/resetSkin/overrideName/resetName/overrideEquipment/resetEquipment/overrideAttribute/clearAllOverrides/clearAllOverridesForViewer/currentOverrides` + `IPerViewerBlockStateGateway` `overrideBlockType/overrideTexture/hideBlock/showBlock/clearOverride/clearAllOverridesForViewer` (4 orthogonal axis methods — hide/show preserve underlying type+texture so showBlock restores in one step) + `IPerViewerGuiStateGateway` `overrideSlotContent/overrideTitle/clearOverridesForViewer(handle)/clearAllOverridesForViewer(viewer)` w/ handle-owner check on every mutator (`handle.viewer ≠ viewer` → UnknownTarget). All fallible methods return `Result<Unit, PerViewerOverrideError>` per the application-API rule (Konsist が raw throwable 禁止を強制). Three separate ports rather than one (use different packet families + different Bukkit primitives; Wave 6 will override independently). **In-mem services** (`core.application.service.perviewer.*`, 3 new files): `InMemoryPerViewerNpcStateGateway(eventBus?)` two-tier index viewer → npcUuid → snapshot + equality-detection no-op suppression on every mutator (re-applying same payload = success without event) + `trackViewer/trackEntity/untrackViewer/untrackEntity` lifecycle helpers (`untrackEntity` cascades to per-viewer `PerViewerOverridesCleared(TargetDespawned)`) + `InMemoryPerViewerBlockStateGateway(eventBus?)` viewer → (world, position) → entry (axis tuple); mutating an axis displaces previous per `BlockOverrideApplied` exclusivity invariant; `hideBlock` preserves underlying type/texture in entry so `showBlock` restores in one step + emits restored-axis event + `InMemoryPerViewerGuiStateGateway(eventBus?)` viewer → handle → state (slots map + title); no viewer-tracking required (handle encodes viewer+gui); exposes `sweepOnQuit(viewer)` as public entry-point distinct from `clearAllOverridesForViewer(viewer)` so PlayerQuit emit carries correct ClearReason. All three NOT thread-safe (Paper main thread only per ADR-0090 convention). **Session-scoped only** — no Postgres migration (every override dropped on server restart; main 最大 V43 不変). **Konsist +1 rule** (48→49): `per-viewer state domain stays pure (ADR-0112)` — `core.domain.perviewer.*` + `core.application.port.outbound.perviewer.{npc,block,gui}.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin (events under `core.domain.event.*` covered by existing domain-purity blanket). **Plugin wiring** (`AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`): 3 new `services.register<...>(impl, priority = 0)` lines in new "Wave 5y-ext" section after Wave 5cinematic block (`IPerViewerNpcStateGateway` + `IPerViewerBlockStateGateway` + `IPerViewerGuiStateGateway`) + 1 PlayerQuitServer subscription that fans out to NPC `untrackViewer` + Block `untrackViewer` + GUI `sweepOnQuit`; subscription handle retained on plugin field `perViewerQuitSub` so `onDisable` can cancel cleanly before bus tears down. 3 new entries appended to `SERVICE_NAMES` companion list. **Test fixtures** (`plugin/test-fixtures/.../{FakePerViewerNpcStateGateway,FakePerViewerBlockStateGateway,FakePerViewerGuiStateGateway}.kt`): cross-plugin Fakes mirroring InMemory* semantics; recorded `calls` list per gateway w/ configurable failure modes (`rejectViewer / rejectNpc / failBackend`) — used by downstream waves to assert ordering without instantiating Paper. **Tests**: 7 new test classes (32 tests — `InMemoryPerViewerNpcStateGatewayTest` × 10 / `InMemoryPerViewerBlockStateGatewayTest` × 8 / `InMemoryPerViewerGuiStateGatewayTest` × 7 / `NpcAttributeKindTest` × 3 / `BlockOverrideAppliedInvariantTest` × 2 / `GuiOverrideAppliedInvariantTest` × 5 / `PerViewerOverridesClearedInvariantTest` × 3). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 49 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Catalog**: Category 8d-ext +8 rows (3 gateway ports 🔌 + error/reason sealed family 🔌 + 4 domain events 🔌 + PlayerQuit subscriber ✅ + test fixtures ✅ + Wave 6 PacketEvents-backed overlays ❌). ADR-0112 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Phase 4**: Paper PacketEvents-backed `PaperPerViewerNpcStateGateway` (PlayerInfoUpdate + EntityMetadata + EntityEquipment packets per viewer — Wave 6), `PaperPerViewerBlockStateGateway` (BlockChange packet per viewer — Wave 6), `PaperPerViewerGuiStateGateway` (Paper Inventory mutation routed through `IGuiAdapter` per viewer + anvil-GUI title re-open round-trip — Wave 6), `IGuiAdapter` `GuiHandle` minting on `openFor(player, spec)` + close-callback to `clearOverridesForViewer` (Wave 6 — gateway has no view-lifecycle awareness on its own; PlayerQuit subscriber is the backstop), per-attribute reset on NPC gateway like `resetAttribute(Scale)` (rejected by design — re-`overrideAttribute` with default value is equivalent in Paper; would balloon port surface 12+ methods for use case not in any wave prompt), per-viewer BGM override on `IMusicGateway` (Wave 5cinematic explicitly punted to 5y-ext but turned out of scope — `IPerViewerSoundGateway.playForViewer` from Wave 5effects already covers per-viewer sound + per-viewer music = `setPerViewerBgm` is one method on `IMusicGateway` add-later, no new gateway needed). **Single commit on branch `wave-5y-ext`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**
- Wave 5wireup (2026-05-27): E2E gameplay loop wireup — closes the 11 listener / command / use-case connection gaps catalogued in `.claude/plans/wave-5g-core-hardening-giggly-sunset.md` §Round 8 (ADR-0116 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 8 closing — Phase 1 PoC pre-Paper land). **3 new domain events** (`core.domain.event.{FirstTimeProfileCreated, PlayerTraveledToPveWorld, PlayerArrivedAtIsland, PlayerReturnedToLobby}`). **3 onboarding listeners** (G1 subscribe-fix to existing `CreateFirstTimeProfileOnPlayerJoinedListener` w/ +2 ctor params eventBus/scheduler so it publishes `FirstTimeProfileCreated` on success + 2 new `core.application.eventbus.listeners.onboarding.{StartTutorialOnFirstTimeProfileCreated, ShowWelcomeDialogOnFirstTimeProfileCreated}Listener` subscribed to the new event). **3 HUD subscribers per ADR-0022 sibling isolation** (G6 `CurrencyChanged` → core, G7 `LeveledUp` → stats, G8 `ComboStepRecorded` → combat — each pushes a stub `HudLayout.ActionBar` to `IHudGateway` so Wave 6b's Paper renderer plugs in without further wire). **2 match listeners** (G4 `RespawnOnPlayerKilledListener` in `match.application.eventbus.listeners.respawn` — subscribes `PlayerKilled` → `RespawnUseCase` with `SpawnStrategy.Random` + 5s protection + 20.0 HP defaults; G5 `TeleportToLobbyOnMatchEndedListener` in `match.application.eventbus.listeners.lobby` — subscribes `MatchEnded` → `IWorldGateway.lobbySpawn` per participant + `PlayerReturnedToLobby` publish). **3 use cases** (G13 `TravelToPveWorldUseCase` w/ hard-coded `pve-overworld` world id + `Vec3(0.5, 100.0, 0.5)` spawn; G14 `TravelToSurvivalIslandUseCase` w/ `IPartyRepository.forPlayer` → `IPartyIslandManager.create-or-load` → teleport + `PlayerArrivedAtIsland`; G15 `ReturnToLobbyUseCase` wraps `IWorldGateway.lobbySpawn` + `PlayerReturnedToLobby`). **4 commands** (G9 register existing `buildQueueCommandSpec` defined in Wave 5w but never wired + bootstrap `InMemoryMatchmaker` itself which was also never wired; G10 `/astera-travel pve|survival|lobby` via new `TravelCommandSpec`; G11 `/astera-menu` via new `MainMenuCommandSpec` opening `MainMenuGuiSpec` through `IGuiAdapter`; G12 `/astera-help` via new `HelpCommandSpec`). **1 GUI spec** (G17 `MainMenuGuiSpec` 6-tile CHEST_9 navigation at slots 1/2/3/5/6/7 — queue/shop/refinement/customize/travel/profile — `lobby.*.show` / `lobby.*.open` GuiIntent keys for `IGuiIntentRouter` dispatch + author-editable `content/gui/main-menu.yaml`). **G16 Annihilation 3-team fix** — `MatchStarted` gains `modeKey: String? = null` (vendor-neutral String rather than `GameModeId` because GameModeId lives in `:plugin:match`; mirrors `MatchFormed.modeKey`); `StartMatchUseCase.execute(stageId, modeKey, teamIds)` selects `DEFAULT_ANNIHILATION_TEAMS = [red, blue, yellow]` when `modeKey == "annihilation"` (else legacy `DEFAULT_TEAMS = [red, blue]`); `IMatchLifecycle.start(stageId, modeKey = null)` propagates; `AsteraMatchPlugin`'s `MatchStarted` subscription now dispatches via new `resolveModeId(modeKey)` helper (`annihilation` → AnnihilationGameMode.ID, else NormaGameMode.ID) and pre-seeds `GameModeContext.scoringState[AnnihilationGameMode.TEAMS_STATE_KEY]` with the Match's `TeamId` set so `AnnihilationGameMode.inferTeams` finds a non-empty pillar set; `MatchEnded` subscription dispatches through every registered mode (Annihilation cleanup no longer silently skipped). **Wired foundations the listeners depend on but no prior wave registered**: `InMemoryDialogGateway(eventBus)`, `InMemoryHudGateway(eventBus, scheduler)`, `InMemoryPlayerProfileService(starterGrant from existing StarterGrantLoader.load(content/balance/starter-grant.yaml), itemContents, ledgerProvider, tutorialContents)` — all at p=0 in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`. **No new Konsist rule** (50 → 50). **No DB migration** (main 最大 V43 不変). **Tests**: 5 new test classes (`StartTutorialOnFirstTimeProfileCreatedListenerTest` × 3 / `ShowWelcomeDialogOnFirstTimeProfileCreatedListenerTest` × 2 / `RespawnOnPlayerKilledListenerTest` × 2 / `TeleportToLobbyOnMatchEndedListenerTest` × 2 / `StartMatchUseCaseAnnihilationTest` × 3) + 2 existing test updates (`CreateFirstTimeProfileOnPlayerJoinedListenerTest` ctor-arg propagation / `MatchFoundUseCaseTest.FakeLifecycle.start` signature update). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 50 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Behavioural delta**: new joiners now actually get a profile + tutorial + welcome dialog; deaths now actually respawn; queue join is reachable (`/astera-queue join norma`); Annihilation forms a real 3-team match with non-empty pillar state; post-match teleports actually happen; HUD subscriber side is wired (renderer still Wave 6b). **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6b**: `PlayerJoinedServer` emission (currently triggered only by `AsteraSessionPlugin` reconnect path + test fixtures — Wave 6b BukkitEventAdapter), HUD layout placeholders → real `HudComponent` glyphs (Wave 6b — subscribers wired, renderer pending), `IDialogRegistry` for `welcomeDialogId` → `DialogSpec` lookup (Phase 4 — listener composes a minimal spec inline using `astera.dialog.welcome.<id>.title` MessageKey discriminator), per-mode kit resolution on respawn (Wave 6+), `IPlayerTutorialStateRepository` per-player enrollment persistence (Wave 5e shipped registry-only — flip listener wiring when the repo lands), cross-server proxy for Travel (Wave 5r `IProxyMessenger`), GUI main-menu YAML loader → in-Kotlin spec override (Wave 5h reload-aware path — author YAML in place, loader integration when `IGuiRegistry.find` grows a load path), `CommandResult.Reply(MessageKey)` variant so `HelpCommandSpec` stops abusing `Failure` to surface its summary text (small framework follow-up). **Single commit on branch `wave-5wireup`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**
- Wave 5transitions (2026-05-27): DisplayEntity transition system + per-type hot-update API — Wave 5h hologram tween foundation (ADR-0115 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 7 closing, final pre-Paper wave). Closes the **two structural gaps** Wave 5h shipped open: per-type updater surface (gateway exposed only `updateKind` full-payload swap) + transition system (Wave 5h Easing 32 curves hooked into Camera + GUI but never holograms). **Per-type updater expansion** (`IHologramGateway` +16 methods + 1 new read accessor `snapshot(handle): Hologram?` needed by transition controller for kind-validation + tween from-state capture): 5 Display base (`updateShadowRadius / updateShadowStrength / updateViewRange / updateBillboard / updateDisplayDimensions`) + 8 Text-specific (`updateTextContent / updateTextLineWidth / updateTextBackgroundColor / updateTextOpacity / updateTextAlignment / updateTextSeeThrough / updateTextShadowed / updateTextDefaultBackground`) + 2 Item-specific (`updateItemId / updateItemDisplayMode`) + 1 Block-specific (`updateBlockData`) — per-type methods validate live kind via `snapshot(handle)` + return new `HologramError.TypeMismatch(expected: KClass<out HologramKind>, actual: KClass<out HologramKind>)`. Existing 10 gateway methods (`spawn / despawn / updateTransform / updateKind / teleport / updateBrightness / updateInterpolation / updateGlowColor / updateVisibility / viewers / all`) unchanged. **Transition domain** (`core.domain.hologram.transition.*` 5 new files): `HologramTransitionHandle` value class blank-reject + `HologramTransition` sealed interface 19-variant — **9 Smooth** (`Transform(targetTransform, durationTicks≥0, easing) / Brightness(targetBrightness?, durationTicks≥0, easing) / GlowColor(targetColor?, durationTicks≥0, easing) / ShadowRadius(targetRadius, durationTicks≥0, easing) / ShadowStrength(targetStrength, durationTicks≥0, easing) / ViewRange(targetRange, durationTicks≥0, easing) / TextOpacity(targetOpacity: Byte, durationTicks≥0, easing) / TextBackgroundColor(targetColor?, durationTicks≥0, easing) / TextLineWidth(targetWidth: Int, durationTicks≥0, easing)`) **reusing Wave 5h Easing enum (31 standard curves — handoff says "32", live enum has 31 LINEAR + 30 named) — no new curve added** + **9 Categorical** (`TextContent(targetLines, delayTicks≥0=0) / TextAlignmentSet(targetAlignment, delayTicks≥0=0) / TextSeeThrough(targetSeeThrough, delayTicks≥0=0) / TextShadowed(targetShadowed, delayTicks≥0=0) / TextDefaultBackground(target, delayTicks≥0=0) / ItemSwap(targetItemId, delayTicks≥0=0) / ItemDisplayModeSet(targetMode, delayTicks≥0=0) / BlockData(targetBlockKey non-blank, delayTicks≥0=0) / Billboard(targetBillboard, delayTicks≥0=0)`) + **1 structural** `Composite(transitions: List<HologramTransition> non-empty)` parallel execution. `HologramTimeline(transitions: List<HologramTransition> non-empty, loopMode: LoopMode = PlayOnce)` sequential — next starts after prev completes + `LoopMode` sealed 3-variant `PlayOnce / Loop(times: Int? where times>0 or null infinite) / PingPong(times: Int? where times>0 or null infinite)` (`times` = total iterations for Loop / total cycles for PingPong where 1 cycle = 1 forward + 1 backward = 2 traversals). **Math interpolators** (domain — JOML direct import OK per `feedback_joml_in_domain` memory + Wave 5h-fix precedent): `HologramRotationInterpolator.slerp(from, to, factor)` wraps JOML `Quaternionf.slerp(other, factor)` non-destructively (always returns fresh Quaternionf, inputs not mutated) + `HologramColorInterpolator.lerp(from?, to?, factor)` RGBA channel-wise byte tween w/ `roundToInt().coerceIn(-128, 127)` and null start/end treated as transparent black for fade-in/out semantics, both null returns null. **Cross-plugin events** (`core.domain.event.*` 3 new): `HologramTransitionStarted(handle, transitionHandle, atTick)` + `HologramTransitionCompleted(handle, transitionHandle, atTick)` (mutually exclusive with Cancelled; infinite loops never publish Completed) + `HologramTransitionCancelled(handle, transitionHandle, reasonKind: String, atTick)` w/ snake_case discriminator `reasonKind ∈ manual/hologram_despawned/timeline_completed/scheduler_shutdown` (matches `CinematicEnded` / `SlotAnimationCancelled` precedent). **Application port** (`core.application.port.outbound.hologram.IHologramTransitionController` 5 methods): `play(handle, transition) / play(handle, timeline) / cancel(transitionHandle) / cancelAll(handle) / active(handle)` w/ `HologramTransitionError` sealed 4-variant `UnknownHologram(handle) / TypeMismatch(expected, actual) / TransitionAlreadyCancelled(transitionHandle) / SchedulerUnavailable`. Validation at play time — per-type transitions check `transition.kind` against live `hologram.kind` via gateway `snapshot(handle)` and fail synchronously if mismatched. Cancellation idempotent (unknown handles return Success with no event publish). **In-mem service** (`core.application.service.hologram.InMemoryHologramTransitionController(gateway, scheduler, eventBus, tickClock?)`): single `IScheduler.runRepeatingTicks(initialDelay=1, period=1)` tick driver for all active transitions (cheaper than N per-transition scheduler tasks at scale). Per tick: Smooth → `factor = easing.apply((tick - startTick) / durationTicks)` → lerp from-state → target → write through per-type gateway updater; Categorical → wait delayTicks → fire instant set; Composite → open all children at same startTick → complete parent when every child completes; Timeline → open one child at a time → advance per LoopMode + ping-pong traversal direction at each child completion. `shutdown()` cancels tick task + publishes `HologramTransitionCancelled(reasonKind = "scheduler_shutdown")` for every in-flight transition; idempotent. **Test fixture** (`plugin/test-fixtures/.../FakeHologramTransitionController.kt`): record + manual-advance API (no scheduler dependency — for use case tests where you just want to assert play call shape). Plus `FakeHologramGateway` gets 16 new HologramOp variants for per-type assertion + `snapshot(handle)` accessor. **Konsist +1 rule** (49→50): `hologram transition domain stays pure (ADR-0115)` — `core.domain.hologram.transition.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / Exposed / Postgres / SQL / Lettuce / Koin / `IDomainEventBus` / `IBroadcaster` / `IScheduler` / `IHologramGateway`. JOML deliberately allowed (Wave 5h-fix precedent — pure-math, no Minecraft coupling). **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` registers 2 new ports at p=0 (`IHologramGateway` with `InMemoryHologramGateway()` — Wave 5h shipped the impl but never wired it; `IHologramTransitionController` with `InMemoryHologramTransitionController(holoGateway, scheduler, eventBus)`) placed after Wave 5gui-depth block + controller reference retained on plugin field `holoTransitionController` so `onDisable` calls `shutdown()` before scheduler tears down + 2 entries appended to `SERVICE_NAMES` companion list. **Tests**: 7 new test classes (71 tests — `HologramTransitionTest` × 9 / `HologramTimelineTest` × 8 / `HologramRotationInterpolatorTest` × 5 / `HologramColorInterpolatorTest` × 9 / `InMemoryHologramTransitionControllerTest` × 17 / `HologramTransitionEasingTest` × 2 / `IHologramGatewayPerTypeTest` × 21 / `HologramTransitionEventTest` × 3). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 50 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **Catalog**: +1 row (DisplayEntity transition system + per-type hot-update API foundation, 🔌 port skeleton — Wave 6b で `PaperHologramGateway` over Bukkit Display + per-type setter dispatch; optionally a `PaperHologramTransitionController` using `Display.interpolationDuration` for vanilla server-side tween at priority=100). ADR-0115 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6b / Phase 4**: `PaperHologramGateway` over `org.bukkit.entity.{TextDisplay,ItemDisplay,BlockDisplay}` w/ 28 method impls (Wave 6b), optional `PaperHologramTransitionController` using vanilla `Display.interpolationDuration` for server-side tween bypassing per-tick step entirely (Wave 6b — performance tradeoff vs jitter), nested `Composite(List<Composite(...)>)` (rejected by design — sequential composition uses `HologramTimeline`; nested parallel would balloon controller cancellation cascade), per-viewer hologram visibility tween (out of scope — visibility is binary via `HologramVisibilityScope`, transition controller only operates on per-hologram fields), persistent transitions surviving plugin reload (out of scope — transitions are session-scoped per ADR-0115 §F; cross-server reproducibility is Wave 7+), authoring DSL for `HologramTransition` in YAML (Phase 4 — Wave 5h already authors `Hologram` from `ItemSpec`; transitions are programmatic for now). **Single commit on branch `wave-5transitions`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**
- Wave 5complete (2026-05-27): **PvP server completeness — orthogonal axes 2-6 gap close** (ADR-0117 Accepted, Wave 5g §Round 8 follow-up). Closes the **10+ orthogonal gaps** Round 8's single-axis MECE audit missed. **Six axes now fully MECE-closed** per `.claude/plans/wave-5g-core-hardening-giggly-sunset.md` §"Player experience MECE map": Axis 1 (player main state — Wave 5wireup), **Axis 2 (Communication — Wave 5complete)**: `/astera-chat {say|team|party|msg}` driven by Wave 5e `SendChatMessageUseCase` + lazy `IMatchTeamLookup` lookup (Match plugin may be unloaded) + `IPartyRepository.forPlayer` + `IPlayerGateway.findByName`; `/astera-announce <messageKey> [chat|actionbar|title]` driven by Wave 5t `IScheduledAnnouncementSystem.schedule(AnnouncementTrigger.Once)` + admin-gated `astera.admin.announce`. **Axis 3 (Moderation — Wave 5complete)**: `/astera-{kick,mute,unmute,ban,tempban,unban,warn}` flat top-level alternates to Wave 5h `/astera mod {...}` tree (EssentialsX-muscle-memory parity) + `ModerationAction.Warn` 5th sealed variant (record-only, no auto-chat/connection effect — exhaustive `when` updated at 4 sites: `InMemoryModerationService.withLifted` / `FakeModerationService.withLifted` / `ApplyModerationActionUseCase.requiredPermission` / `PostgresModerationService.encode+decode`) + `PERM_WARN = "astera.mod.warn"` + V44 migration extends `moderation_actions.kind` CHECK to include `'warn'` + `KickPlayerOnAfkKickedListener` bridges Wave 5q `PlayerAfkKicked` event → `ModerationAction.Kick(issuedBy = SYSTEM_ISSUER_ID = PlayerId(UUID(0,0)))` (bypasses permission gate — system action) + `/astera-match {abort,cancel,restart}` sub-commands (abort = `IMatchLifecycle.end(AdminEnded)`; cancel = `IMatchLifecycle.end(Cancelled("admin-cancel"))`; restart = end + start fresh on saved stage). **Axis 4 (UI overlay — Wave 5complete)**: `UpdateMatchSidebarListener` subscribes `MatchStarted` + `EntityPickedUpItem` → per-team `HudLayout.Sidebar` push via Wave 5j `IHudGateway` (Norma scoring is the Phase 1 canonical view; Annihilation can subscribe `NexusDamaged` symmetrically Wave 6) + `KillfeedOnPlayerKilledListener` subscribes `PlayerKilled` → 3 HUD surfaces (victim `HudLayout.ActionBar` / killer `HudLayout.Subtitle` / bystander-broadcast `HudLayout.ActionBar`; PvE kills handled separately). **Axis 5 (System process — Wave 5complete)**: `NormaVictoryConditionEvaluatorListener` subscribes `EntityPickedUpItem` + re-runs existing Wave 4.5 `VictoryConditionEvaluator` + auto-fires `SwitchPhaseUseCase.execute(matchId, ScoreThresholdReached)` on unique winner — eliminates admin-manual `/astera-match phase` from the Norma win path. AFK detector reused as-is (Wave 5q `IAfkDetector` + `AfkSweepScheduler` were already wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`; only the kick-event bridge is new). **Axis 6 (Item lifecycle — Wave 5complete)**: 1 new outbound port `IItemEntityGateway` (`core.application.port.outbound.item.IItemEntityGateway` — `spawnItemEntity(world, position, itemId, count, lifetime?)` + `removeItemEntity(handle)` + `findNearby(world, position, radius)` + `peek(handle)` + `ItemEntityHandle` value class UUID + `ItemEntityError` sealed 4-variant `UnknownWorld / UnknownHandle / InvalidCount / WorldGatewayError`) + `core.domain.item.ItemEntityHandle` value class + `core.application.service.item.InMemoryItemEntityGateway(scheduler?)` honours `lifetimeSeconds` via injected `IScheduler.runLaterTicks` (300s default per MC convention; null scheduler = no auto-despawn for headless tests) + `plugin/test-fixtures/.../FakeItemEntityGateway` w/ `spawnCalls`/`removeCalls` ledgers + 1 new outbound port `IPlayerLocationLookup` (`core.application.port.outbound.IPlayerLocationLookup` w/ `PlayerLocation(world, position)` value + `locate(playerId): PlayerLocation?`) + `core.application.service.PlayerMovedLocationTracker(eventBus)` default impl subscribes to Wave 5world-events `PlayerMoved` + keeps `ConcurrentHashMap<PlayerId, PlayerLocation>` (Wave 6b Paper overlay can read `IMcServer.findPlayer(id).location` directly at priority 100) + `SpawnItemEntityOnLootDroppedListener` subscribes Wave 5pve-content `LootDropped` + spawns one entity per `(itemId, count)` at killer's last-known position (skips drop if location unknown) + `AutoPickupOnPlayerMovedListener` subscribes `PlayerMoved` + `findNearby(radius=1.5 default)` + `IInventoryMutator.give` BEFORE despawn (inventory-full keeps entity in world for retry) + publishes new Core `LootPickedUp(playerId, itemId, count, world, position, atTick)` event (Core variant distinct from Match-side `EntityPickedUpItem`, per ADR-0022). **Konsist +1 rule** (50→51): `item entity domain stays pure (ADR-0117)` — `core.domain.item.*` MUST NOT import Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / exposed / postgres / sql / lettuce / koin / `IDomainEventBus` / `IBroadcaster` / `IScheduler` / `IItemEntityGateway`. **DB migration V44** `moderation_actions_warn.sql` drops + recreates the kind CHECK constraint to add `'warn'` (forward-only per Phase 1 posture). **Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` registers `IModerationService` (`InMemoryModerationService` default p=0 — Wave 5h shipped the impl but never wired it; Persistence Postgres overlay can take priority 100) + `IPlayerLocationLookup` + `IItemEntityGateway`; subscribes 3 listeners (`SpawnItemEntityOnLootDropped` / `AutoPickupOnPlayerMoved` / `KickPlayerOnAfkKicked`); starts `PlayerMovedLocationTracker` (returns IDomainEventBus.Subscription on `start()`, cancelled in `onDisable`); registers 9 commands (`/astera-{kick,mute,unmute,ban,tempban,unban,warn,chat,announce}`). `AsteraMatchPlugin.onEnable` subscribes 3 listeners (`UpdateMatchSidebar` (×2 sub on MatchStarted+EntityPickedUpItem) / `KillfeedOnPlayerKilled` / `NormaVictoryConditionEvaluator`) + extends existing `buildMatchCommandSpec` with abort/cancel/restart sub-commands. **i18n**: en.yaml + ja.yaml both gain `mod.{command.{kick,mute,unmute,ban,tempban,unban,warn},error.{invalid-player,invalid-reason,no-active-action,storage},reason.afk-auto-kick}` + `chat.{command.{root,say,team,party,msg},error.{blank-message,muted,rate-limited,no-team,no-party,unknown-player}}` + `killfeed.{victim,killer,entry}` + `match.{sidebar.{title,team-line,empty},error.match-not-found,command.{abort,cancel,restart}}` (merged into existing `match:` block, no duplicate top-level key) + `item-entity.error.{unknown-world,unknown-handle,invalid-count,world-gateway}` + `announce.{command.root,error.invalid-kind}`. Key parity test green. **Tests**: 7 new test classes (`InMemoryItemEntityGatewayTest` × 7 / `SpawnItemEntityOnLootDroppedListenerTest` × 3 / `AutoPickupOnPlayerMovedListenerTest` × 3 / `KickPlayerOnAfkKickedListenerTest` × 1 / `ChatCommandSpecTest` × 6 / `AnnouncementCommandSpecTest` × 4 / `UpdateMatchSidebarListenerTest` × 3 / `KillfeedOnPlayerKilledListenerTest` × 2 / `NormaVictoryConditionEvaluatorListenerTest` × 3). すべて Bukkit harness 不要 (純 JVM)。`./gradlew check` green; Konsist 51 rules pass; 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. **AFK detector decision**: existing Wave 5q `IAfkDetector` port + `InMemoryAfkDetector` + `AfkSweepScheduler` reused as-is (not a new port). Only the kick-event bridge (`KickPlayerOnAfkKickedListener`) is new — the wave handoff suggested "new `AfkDetectorService`" but the existing service already publishes `PlayerAfkKicked` from the sweep scheduler; the gap was solely the moderation subscriber. **6-axes MECE annotation**: Axis 1 (Wave 5wireup) + Axis 2 (Wave 5complete) + Axis 3 (Wave 5complete) + Axis 4 (Wave 5complete partial — Paper sweep render Wave 6b) + Axis 5 (Wave 5complete) + Axis 6 (Wave 5complete) — **all 6 orthogonal axes now have wave-level closure** at the application/listener tier. Wave 6b Paper render closes the remaining sub-rows (cooldown ring + buff display + hotbar tooltip + Bukkit chat hijack + Bukkit item entity adapter) — InMemory ports / domain / listeners committed today are sufficient for Phase 1 PoC behavioural completeness. ADR-0117 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6b / Phase 4**: `PaperItemEntityGateway` over `org.bukkit.entity.Item` w/ chunk-indexed `findNearby` (Wave 6b — InMemory's O(N) acceptable at Phase 1 player counts), Paper chat-hijack (`AsyncChatEvent` cancellation routed through `ChatChannel` filter) so player chat actually consumes the use case (Wave 6b), `Player.kick(reason)` enforcement on `PlayerAfkKicked` (currently writes audit record only — Wave 6b connection-listener bridge), per-match killfeed history rendering as a scrolling sidebar (Wave 6b — actionbar fallback today), Annihilation-mode-specific sidebar variant subscribing `NexusDamaged` (Wave 6 — Phase 1 PoC uses Norma sidebar as canonical), `MatchScoreChanged` granular event (rejected by design — re-evaluation on `EntityPickedUpItem` is cheap enough at Phase 1 player counts; adding new event would expand the cross-plugin surface unnecessarily). **Single commit on branch `wave-5complete`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5server-harden (2026-05-27): **Server hardening + safety substrates** (ADR-0123 Accepted, §Round 10+11). Closes the 7 server-level audit gaps surfaced in Round 10/11 (no boot-time content validation, crash recovery untested, no structured error report, plugin reload safety untested, no vanilla suppression register, listener cleanup audit not run, no concurrent-write IT). **A — Boot-time validation**: new port `core.application.port.outbound.validation.IContentValidator` w/ `validateAll(): ContentValidationReport` + `register(ContentReferenceCheck): Registration` + `core.application.service.validation.ContentValidatorService` impl (`CopyOnWriteArrayList` of plugin-contributed checks, severity routing by sealed variant, per-check exception isolation) + `core.domain.validation.ContentValidationError` sealed 3-variant (`MissingReference(sourceId, referenceKind, referencedId, field)` / `DuplicateId(sourceId, registryName)` / `InvalidSchema(sourceId, reason)`) + `ContentValidationReport` (pre-bucketed critical/warning/info + entries/registries counted) + `ValidationSeverity` enum + `BootValidateContentLifecycleListener(validator, mode)` (`IServerLifecycleListener` impl, fires on `onReady`, Strict mode rethrows `ContentValidationStrictFailure(criticalCount)`, Lenient logs + continues, high priority so Strict aborts precede player-facing paths) + first example check `economy.application.service.validation.ShopReferencesItemCheck(shops, items)` walking every `ShopSpec.offers[*].itemId` against `IItemRegistry`. **B — Crash recovery test**: `plugin/match/src/test/.../scenario/CrashRecoveryScenarioTest.kt` × 3 (match active state across simulated restart / in-flight phase transitions don't phantom-commit / disconnected sessions retain `currentMatch`) — Bukkit-free, uses in-memory repos as the test-layer analogue of Postgres-survives-restart. **C — Structured error report**: new port `core.application.port.outbound.error.IErrorReporter` w/ `report(error: ServerError)` (MUST NOT throw) + `subscribeCritical(handler)` + `Subscription` shape mirroring `IDomainEventBus.Subscription` + `core.domain.error.ServerError` sealed Critical/Warning/Info each w/ `occurredAt`+`summary`+`source: ErrorSource` (sealed 6-variant `Lifecycle(plugin, phase) / UseCase(name) / Scheduler(jobId) / Content(category) / Persistence(repositoryName) / Other(name)`) + `core.application.service.error.InMemoryErrorReporter(audit, logger)` impl (per-severity SLF4J + audit append + Critical-only fan-out, subscriber+audit exceptions swallowed at WARN) + `plugin/test-fixtures/.../error/FakeErrorReporter` recorder + new `AuditEvent.ServerErrorReported(id, severity, summary, sourceIdentifier, occurredAt)` variant + matching `PostgresAuditLogRepository.KIND_SERVER_ERROR_REPORTED = "server_error_reported"` encode/decode arm + `ServerErrorReportedJson` private DTO (NO DB migration — `audit_log.kind` is varchar(64), `data` is JSONB). **D + F — Listener cleanup contract + audit fixes**: contract documented in ADR-0123 ("every `eventBus.subscribe(...)` inside `platform/Astera*Plugin.kt` MUST be `+= eventBus.subscribe(...)` into a holder list; `onDisable` MUST `subscriptions.forEach { it.cancel() }`"). Pre-wave audit: Core 16 subs / **0 holder** (16 leaks), Match 16/15 (1 leak), Combat/Stats/Economy clean, Items/Persistence have no subscribes. Phase B fixes: Core's 9 bare subscribes converted to `wave5completeSubs += eventBus.subscribe(...)` (RestoreInventoryOnPlayerJoined + Wave 5k resource-pool init + 3 onboarding listeners + Currency HUD + 3 ResourcePool HUD listeners); Match's spawn-on-MatchStarted bridge converted to `subscriptions += eventBus.subscribe(...)`. **E — Vanilla feature suppression register**: new port `core.application.port.outbound.vanilla.IVanillaFeatureSuppressor` w/ `suppress / restore / isSuppressed / suppressed` (all idempotent + thread-safe contract) + `core.domain.vanilla.VanillaFeature` sealed 8-variant (`VanillaAdvancement / VanillaDeathMessage / VanillaXpBar / VanillaHungerBar / VanillaMobSpawning / VanillaRecipe / VanillaWeatherCycle / VanillaPortalTraversal`, each w/ stable string `id`, lazy `VanillaFeature.all` companion list) + `core.application.service.vanilla.InMemoryVanillaFeatureSuppressor` (`ConcurrentHashMap`-backed set) + `plugin/test-fixtures/.../vanilla/FakeVanillaFeatureSuppressor` w/ `suppressCalls`+`restoreCalls` recorders. Domain code can branch on `isSuppressed(feature)` today; Wave 6b's `PaperVanillaFeatureSuppressor` adapter wires Bukkit event cancel + gamerule writes. **G — Concurrent-write IT (P0)**: `plugin/persistence/src/test/.../integration/ConcurrentWriteIT.kt` × 3 (Wallet double-spend = 1 success + 1 InsufficientFunds / InventorySnapshot race = both upserts commit, last-write-wins survivor / Party concurrent-join 5 threads on maxSize=4 = 3 admitted + 2 PartyFull). **Required product fixes**: `PostgresCurrencyLedger` now uses per-`PlayerId` lock striping (single-server safe; multi-server requires `SELECT FOR UPDATE` — follow-up ADR) w/ ordered two-lock acquisition on `transfer` to avoid opposite-direction deadlock; `JoinPartyUseCase` now uses per-`PartyId` lock striping (Party is in-memory-only by design, so the IT uses `InMemoryPartyRepository`). **H — Konsist +1 rule** (52 → 53): `every plugin onEnable subscribe must be assigned into a subscription holder (ADR-0123)` — scans `platform/Astera*Plugin.kt` line-by-line, fails on any bare `eventBus.subscribe(...)` not prefixed with `+= eventBus.subscribe(`. **Plugin wiring** (`AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`): 3 new `services.register<...>` at priority 0 (`IContentValidator` / `IErrorReporter` w/ audit-log dependency lookup falling back to local var / `IVanillaFeatureSuppressor`) + lazy bootstrapper creation when `IServerBootstrapper` not yet registered + `BootValidateContentLifecycleListener` (Mode.Lenient default) attached to bootstrapper. `AsteraEconomyPlugin.onEnable`: optional `IContentValidator` lookup + register `ShopReferencesItemCheck` w/ `validationRegistrations: MutableList<IContentValidator.Registration>` holder cleared in `onDisable`. **Tests**: 7 new test classes (29+ tests — `ContentValidatorServiceTest` × 7 / `BootValidateContentLifecycleListenerTest` × 4 / `InMemoryVanillaFeatureSuppressorTest` × 7 / `InMemoryErrorReporterTest` × 7 / `ShopReferencesItemCheckTest` × 3 / `CrashRecoveryScenarioTest` × 3 / `ConcurrentWriteIT` × 3) — all Bukkit-free pure-JVM (IT Testcontainers-gated). `./gradlew check` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. ADR-0123 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 6b**: `PaperVanillaFeatureSuppressor` adapter w/ per-feature Bukkit event listener + gamerule writes (Wave 6b); `Strict` mode flip on `BootValidateContentLifecycleListener` once `content/` shows clean for 3 consecutive nightly runs (Wave 5h follow-up); per-plugin `ContentReferenceCheck` contributions for skill / mob / stage / loot / NPC dialog cross-refs (Wave 5h follow-up — only Economy's `ShopReferencesItemCheck` lands today as a pattern reference); Postgres `SELECT FOR UPDATE` / advisory-lock on multi-server (post-Phase-1 ADR); `Match` + `PlayerSession` Postgres impls so `CrashRecoveryScenarioTest` can re-run as a real IT (Wave 6); admin command `/astera-debug validate-content` to trigger `IContentValidator.validateAll()` on demand (Wave 5h follow-up); free-form `logger.error(...)` migration to typed `IErrorReporter.report(ServerError.Critical(...))` calls (incremental across follow-up waves); `Registration`-hygiene Konsist rule (currently only `Subscription` is enforced — broaden when `Registration` helpers stabilise). **Single commit on branch `wave-5server-harden`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave G 5player-prefs-social (2026-05-27): **PlayerPreferences + Settings/Friends/Party GUI functional** (ADR-0124 Accepted, §Round 12). Closes the Wave 5lobby-content skeleton gap (ADR-0119: GUI surfaces shipped but Settings/Friends/Party application handlers were explicitly deferred to "Wave G") + the Wave 5e PlayerSettings shape gap (no locale, no per-channel volume). **A — PlayerSettings extension**: 3 new fields (`locale: LocaleCode = EN`, `musicVolume / soundVolume / voiceVolume: Int = 100`) + `VolumeChannel` enum (Music / Sound / Voice) + helpers `volume(channel)` / `withVolume(channel, value)` centralising the (channel, value) → field switch + `PlayerSettings.SUPPORTED_LOCALES = {EN, JA}` gating which locales the use case accepts; `init` block enforces 0..100; `PlayerSettingsJson` 1:1 extension w/ `ignoreUnknownKeys` fallback (no DB migration). **B — 2 new setter use cases** (`SetLocaleUseCase` returning `Result<Unit, SettingsError.UnsupportedLocale>`; `SetVolumeUseCase(playerId, channel, value)` returning `Result<Unit, SettingsError.InvalidVolume>`) + `SettingsError.{UnsupportedLocale(locale), InvalidVolume(value)}` cases. **C — 2 new party use cases** (`KickPartyMemberUseCase(leader, target)` leader-only w/ self-kick rejection; `TransferPartyLeaderUseCase(currentLeader, newLeader)` leader-only w/ same-person rejection + member-required) both returning `Result<Unit, PartyError>` reusing the Wave 5e hierarchy. **D — Lobby Party invite is synchronous** (PoC trade-off documented in ADR-0124 §D): lobby `LobbyPartyInviteIntentHandler` calls `JoinPartyUseCase` directly rather than `InvitePartyMemberUseCase` because the latter is island-scoped (requires `IslandId`) and the anvil-modal `IGuiModalGateway` primitive is Wave 10c+ scope — when the modal lands the handler switches to a lobby-flavoured invitation use case + async accept flow. **E — 14 intent handlers wired on `IGuiIntentRouter`**: 3 settings show handlers (Wave 5lobby-content skeletons finally wired) + 3 settings application handlers (`LobbySettingsLocaleIntentHandler` w/ en↔ja cycle / `LobbySettingsVolumeIntentHandler` w/ 100→75→50→25→0→100 step on the Music channel today / `LobbySettingsControlsIntentHandler` w/ RightClick↔Button cycle) + 5 friends handlers (Show + Add + Accept + Remove + Pending — Pending lists rows where `playerB == player.id` in `Friendship`, derived without a new field per Wave 5e's unordered-pair convention) + 5 party handlers (Show + Invite + Kick + TransferLeader + Leave); each handler routes `payload[target]` / `payload[requester]` into the use case and renders the typed `Result` arm into a per-viewer chat message via `IMessageRenderer`; blank-payload tile-clicks close the GUI and fall back to the Brigadier command path. **F — 4 Brigadier command families** (modelled as flat top-level commands per the Wave 5complete `flatModCommands` precedent — the CommandSpec DSL doesn't compose deeply nested literal trees): `/astera-language <en|ja>` w/ `astera.settings.language` permission; `/astera-volume <music|sound|voice> <0-100>` w/ `astera.settings.volume`; `/astera-friend-{add,accept,remove,list}` w/ `astera.friends.use`; `/astera-party-{invite,kick,transfer,leave,list}` w/ `astera.party.use`; `astera-party-invite` auto-creates a solo party w/ `DEFAULT_PARTY_MAX_SIZE=4` for the caller if they aren't already in one, then `JoinParty(target)`. **G — Wave 5e friendship i18n gap closure**: Wave 5e's `FriendshipError` referenced `astera.social.friendship.error.*` keys (not-found / already-exists / blocked / not-pending / self-friend) but never added them; Wave G fills both en + ja. New keys also added under `astera.command.player-only`, `astera.settings.{locale,volume,controls,language}.*`, `astera.friends.{command,add,accept,remove,pending}.*`, `astera.party.{command,invite,kick,transfer,leave}.*`. **H — Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` constructs 11 use case instances + 14 handler instances + 14 `IGuiIntentRouter` registrations collected into `waveGIntentRegistrations: List<ServiceRegistry.Registration>` field; `onDisable` `unregister()`s each so `/reload` doesn't leak captured services; 4 command-spec builders register their products via `commandHandler.register(...)`. **I — Tests**: 6 new test classes (24 tests — `SetLocaleUseCaseTest` × 4 / `SetVolumeUseCaseTest` × 5 / `KickPartyMemberUseCaseTest` × 5 / `TransferPartyLeaderUseCaseTest` × 5 / `FriendPartyFlowE2ETest` × 3 / `PlayerSettingsTest` × 8) all Bukkit-free pure-JVM; `PostgresPlayerSettingsRepositoryIT` extended with `locale and per-channel volume round-trip` + `default locale is EN and default volumes are 100` (2 new). **Phase A pre-existing fixes**: `AdminOpsCommands.mentionsPlayer` was missing the `is AuditEvent.ServerErrorReported -> false` arm (Wave 5server-harden added the variant without updating the `when`); `RestoreInventoryOnPlayerJoinedListener(restoreSnapshot, sessionRepo)` call site was missing the `sessionRepo` arg (same wave gap). Both fixed in Phase A so the build went green before Wave G's own additions. **Konsist** unchanged at **53** (no new rule — additions are pure handler + use case + command + i18n). `./gradlew check` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. ADR-0124 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave H / Wave 10c+**: friend tile dynamic binding (`player.friends.0..8` resolver) + party member dynamic binding (`player.party.members.0..7`) — current GUIs render the YAML default in the per-friend / per-member slot region; volume mix-down to client packets (Wave 7+ audio backend); 3-tile Settings GUI volume row (one per channel — Phase 1 single tile cycles Music only); anvil-modal `IGuiModalGateway` primitive that would let the GUI handler accept a typed target name without falling back to the Brigadier command path (Wave 10c+); GUI in-place refresh after a setter so the tile re-renders the new value (Wave 10c+); per-viewer locale propagation latency profiling (the renderer reads from the settings repo on every render — fine at Phase 1 scale, may warrant caching at Phase 4 scale). **Single commit on branch `wave-5player-prefs-social`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**
- Wave 5block-embed (2026-05-27): **Block command / event embedding** (ADR-0120 Accepted, §Round 10). Closes the 4 block-audit gaps (no `IBlockInteractionRouter`, no `ISignActionService`, no portal abstraction, no region-transition detector) + `CustomBlockSpec.onRightClick: GuiIntent?` field extension of ADR-0090. **New ports** (3): `IBlockInteractionRouter` (`core.application.port.outbound.block.*`) `bindRightClick / bindLeftClick / unbind / lookup` over `(world, position, action)` with `BlockBindingHandle` value class + `BlockInteractionAction` sealed 5-variant (`RunCommand(CommandSpec)` / `OpenGui(GuiSpec)` / `TriggerSkill(SkillId)` / `TeleportTo(WorldId, Vec3)` / `Custom(intentKey)`); `ISignActionService` (`core.application.port.outbound.sign.*`) `bindSignAction / unbindSign / lookup` over `(world, position)` with `SignAction` sealed 4-variant (`RunCommand` / `OpenGui` / `TeleportTo` / `Display(MessageKey)`); `IPortalGateway` (`core.application.port.outbound.portal.*`) `register / unregister / findByPosition / all` over `Portal` records with dual-index (block-position map + per-world region list). **InMemory + Fake** for all three (`InMemoryBlockInteractionRouter` / `InMemorySignActionService` / `InMemoryPortalGateway` + `FakeBlockInteractionRouter` / `FakeSignActionService` / `FakePortalGateway`). **Domain — portal** (`core.domain.portal.*`, 2 files): `PortalId` (kebab-case `JvmInline`) + `Portal(id, sourceWorld, sourcePosition, sourceShape, targetWorld, targetPosition, activationRule)` with `PortalShape` sealed 2-variant (`Block(position)` / `Region(region)`) + `PortalActivationRule` sealed 4-variant (`WalkOn` / `RightClick` / `SneakAct` / `Both` w/ `acceptsWalkOn() / acceptsRightClick()` helpers). **Domain — sign** (`core.domain.sign.*`, 1 file): `SignAction` sealed (see above). **Domain — events** (`core.domain.event.*`, 5 new): `PortalActivated(playerId, portalId, atTick)` + `PortalRegistered` + `PortalUnregistered` + `PlayerEnteredRegion(playerId, regionId, atTick)` + `PlayerExitedRegion(playerId, regionId, atTick)`. **Listeners** (4 new + 1 skeleton): `RouteBlockInteractionListener` (subscribes `PlayerInteractedBlock` — finally closes the zero-subscriber gap audited 2026-05-27; dispatches `OpenGui` → `IGuiAdapter.open`, `TriggerSkill` → publishes `SkillCastRequested` w/ `astera-block-interaction` source sentinel, `TeleportTo` → `IWorldGateway.teleport`; `RunCommand` + `Custom` log INFO + drop pending Wave 6 `IBlockIntentDispatcher` + command-context synthesis) + `ActivatePortalOnPlayerMovedListener` (subscribes `PlayerMoved`, walks `IPortalGateway.findByPosition`, gates by `activationRule.acceptsWalkOn()`, applies loop-back guard, publishes `PortalActivated` on successful teleport) + `ActivatePortalOnPlayerInteractedBlockListener` (subscribes `PlayerInteractedBlock`, filters `action == RightClick`, gates by `activationRule.acceptsRightClick()`) + `RegionTransitionDetectorListener` (subscribes `PlayerMoved`, holds per-player `MutableMap<PlayerId, Set<RegionId>>` cache, derives `PlayerEnteredRegion` / `PlayerExitedRegion` from `regionsAt(from)` vs `regionsAt(to)` delta, first-move seeds baseline so session-start doesn't fire spurious enters, `forget(playerId)` for `PlayerLeftServer` cleanup) + `ParseAndBindSignOnSignChangedListener` skeleton (Bukkit `SignChangeEvent` → domain bridge lands Wave 6b; constructed at wire-time but not yet subscribed). **Pure helper**: `SignTextParser` `object` (line 0 magic header `[ASTERA]` case-insensitive + trim-tolerant; line 1 type `teleport` / `display`; lines 2-3 type-specific params; returns `null` for malformed). **`CustomBlockSpec` extension** (ADR-0090 → ADR-0120): `onRightClick: GuiIntent? = null` field added; `CustomBlockYamlConfig.on_right_click: OnRightClickYamlConfig?` sub-block (`key` + `payload`); defaults to null so existing YAML loads unchanged per Phase 1 forward-only posture; runtime consumption pending Wave 6b Paper materializer. **Konsist +1 rule** (51 → 52): `block interaction and portal domain stays pure (ADR-0120)` covering `core.domain.{block,portal,sign}.*` — forbids Bukkit family / `kotlinx.serialization` / persistence / Koin / `IDomainEventBus` / `IBroadcaster`; allows `GuiIntent` + `CommandSpec` (pure value vocabularies). **No Postgres migration** (all in-memory; persistence overlay candidate for a future wave). **Plugin wiring** (`AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable`): 3 new `services.register<...>` at priority 0 (router / sign / portal) + 4 new `eventBus.subscribe(...)` calls + 1 listener constructed-but-not-subscribed (`ParseAndBindSign` waiting on Wave 6b event); world gateway lookup reuses the effects-subsystem `NoopWorldGatewayForEffects` fallback (teleport returns false → listeners log DEBUG + skip pending Wave 6 Paper adapter). **Tests**: 7 new test classes (40+ tests — `InMemoryBlockInteractionRouterTest` × 8 / `RouteBlockInteractionListenerTest` × 8 / `SignTextParserTest` × 8 / `InMemorySignActionServiceTest` × 4 / `PortalTest` × 4 / `InMemoryPortalGatewayTest` × 7 / `PortalActivationListenersTest` × 7 / `RegionTransitionDetectorListenerTest` × 7 / `CustomBlockSpecTest` + 1 / `CustomBlockYamlConfigTest` + 3) — all Bukkit-free pure-JVM. `./gradlew check` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. ADR-0120 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 6b**: Paper `SignChangeEvent` → domain event bridge (Wave 6b); `IBlockIntentDispatcher` for `BlockInteractionAction.Custom` + `CommandContext` synthesis for `BlockInteractionAction.RunCommand` (Wave 6); Paper materializer consumption of `CustomBlockSpec.onRightClick` (Wave 6b); `PlayerLeftServer` subscription to `RegionTransitionDetectorListener.forget` for cache cleanup (Wave 6); content/portals/ YAML + `PortalContents` loader (Wave 6 onwards); region-shape `IPortalGateway` AABB-tree optimisation if stage authors define >10 region portals per world. **Single commit on branch `wave-5block-embed`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5game-complete (2026-05-27): **Game system completion** (ADR-0121 Accepted, §Round 10+11). Closes the 7 game-system audit gaps catalogued at HEAD `587c387`. **§A — Match-end wiring**: new `AnnihilationWinConditionListener` (`plugin/match/.../application/eventbus/listeners/victory/`) subscribes `TeamEliminated` + `AnnihilationPhaseTransitioned`; on Tier-3 fall re-asks `AnnihilationMatchTracker.evaluateWinCondition` and calls `IMatchLifecycle.end(reason=ObjectiveCompleted)` for `Winner`/`Draw`; on `Phase4SuddenDeath` entry schedules `IScheduler.runLater(suddenDeathTicks)` one-shot that ends with `SuddenDeathResolved`. Annihilation matches now actually end via win-condition (was read-only `@Suppress("UNUSED_VARIABLE")` before). **§B — Loadout selector GUI**: new `LoadoutSelectorGuiSpec` (`core.application.gui.lobby.*`) 9-slot CHEST_9, each tile's left/right/shift/middle click maps to `loadout.selector.{activate,rename,reset,save}` with `{slot, mode}` payload; per-slot mode distribution norma×4 / annihilation×3 / bet-1v1×2; reload-aware YAML at `content/gui/loadout-selector.yaml`; `LoadoutSelectorShowIntentHandler` + new placeholder item `gui-tile-loadout`. `CustomizeMenuGuiSpec` repoints loadout tile from legacy `weapons.gui.loadout.open` → `loadout.selector.show`. Handler wiring deferred (mirrors the 9 unwired ADR-0119 lobby intent handlers). **§C — Match countdown**: new `MatchCountdownListener` (`plugin/match/.../application/eventbus/listeners/match/`) subscribes `MatchStarted`, schedules 5 × `IScheduler.runLater` for `HudLayout.Subtitle("astera.match.countdown.<5..1>")` per participant at 1-second cadence; subtitle dominates screen ~0.75s per beat without colliding with the action-bar HUD stack. **§D — Match end summary**: new `MatchEndSummaryListener` subscribes `MatchEnded`, pushes per-team verdict (`astera.match.summary.{victory,defeat,draw}`) as Subtitle + per-player chat (`astera.match.summary.header` + `astera.match.summary.reason.<reason-kind>` from 6 `EndReason` variants mapped to lowercase kebab-case). GUI summary screen (MVP / per-player kills + damage) **deferred to Wave 6b / Phase 4** — Phase 1 PoC chat + subtitle only. **§E — PvP death policy**: new sealed `PvpDeathRule` (`plugin/match/.../domain/death/`) 4-variant (`KeepInventory` / `DropAll` / `DropEquipped` / `DropOnly(percent: Float ∈ 0..1, includes: Set<ItemId>)`). New outbound port `IPvpDeathPolicyResolver` + `DefaultPvpDeathPolicyResolver` (in-mem) — resolution chain: **stage-override → mode-default → KeepInventory (safest fallback)**. New `ApplyPvpDeathPolicyListener` subscribes `combat.domain.event.PlayerKilled`, fast-skips non-match deaths via `matches.findMatchOf(victim)`, translates rule into `IInventoryMutator.clear(...)` calls (`All` / `Armor`+`OffHand`); `DropOnly` ships rule-shaped but listener defers per-item probability roll to Wave 6 PaperInventoryMutator (no live held-slot or RNG in domain). Per-mode defaults: `null`/`norma`/unknown → `KeepInventory`, `annihilation` → `DropEquipped`, `bet-1v1` → `DropAll`. **§F — Mode min-player guard**: new pure `MatchMinPlayerPolicy` (`plugin/match/.../domain/match/`) — `requirementFor(modeKey)` returns floor (Norma 2 / Annihilation 3 / Bet 1v1 2 / unknown 2), `check(modeKey, match)` returns sealed `Outcome.Ok` or `Outcome.Insufficient(required, actual)`. New outbound port `IMatchModeRegistry` + `InMemoryMatchModeRegistry` mirroring `MatchStarted.modeKey` (and `unregister` on `MatchEnded`). `SwitchPhaseUseCase` ctor gained nullable `IMatchModeRegistry` param; on `CountdownComplete` trigger only, consults `MatchMinPlayerPolicy` and returns new `SwitchPhaseError.InsufficientPlayers(matchId, required, actual)` on miss so the match stays in COUNTDOWN waiting for slots. Pre-existing `SwitchPhaseUseCaseTest` + `PhaseTimerUseCaseTest` updated to seed 1-per-team rosters. **§G — E2E scenario harness**: new `MatchScenarioHarness` (`plugin/match/src/test/.../scenario/AsteraGameplayScenarioTest.kt`) — JVM-only end-to-end harness wiring lifecycle use cases + all 4 new listeners + modeRegistry + sniffer; lives in `:plugin:match` (separate from `:plugin:stats` `AsteraTestHarness`) to avoid sibling dep loops. 3 scenarios: `NormaTwoVTwoWinScenarioTest` (full event chain MatchStarted×1 + PlayerKilled×2 + MatchEnded×1 + ENDED phase, plus min-player guard reject), `AnnihilationThreeVThreeCoreBreakScenarioTest` (9 players / 3 teams; asserts mode registry round-trip + min-player floor 3 + PvP default DropEquipped + full lifecycle, plus min-player guard reject at 2 players), `InventorySnapshotRestoreScenarioTest` (save / disconnect / reconnect round-trip + null-on-no-prior path). Plus unit tests `MatchMinPlayerPolicyTest` × 6, `PvpDeathRuleTest` × 5, `DefaultPvpDeathPolicyResolverTest` × 6. **Plugin wiring** (`AsteraMatchPlugin.onEnable`): adds `IMatchModeRegistry` p=0 registration + replaces `SwitchPhaseUseCase` ctor with mode-aware variant; extends `MatchStarted` subscriber to mirror modeKey; extends `MatchEnded` subscriber to unregister; instantiates `AnnihilationWinConditionListener.subscribe()` + `MatchCountdownListener` + `MatchEndSummaryListener`; registers `IPvpDeathPolicyResolver` p=0 + subscribes `ApplyPvpDeathPolicyListener` opportunistically (skips if `IInventoryMutator` absent — Wave 6 lands the production binding). **i18n** (en + ja parity maintained): new keys under existing `match:` block (`error.insufficient-players`, `countdown.{1..5}`, `summary.{victory,defeat,draw,header,reason.{score-threshold,timer-expired,admin,sudden-death,objective,cancelled}}`) + new `gui.loadout-selector.{title,slot.tooltip.{activate,rename,reset,save}}` block. **Konsist**: no new rules (52 → 52); existing rules pass including `application layers must not import raw JVM throwable types`. **No DB migration**: every new piece is domain + in-memory; persistence overlay candidate for future wave once stage-YAML `pvp_death:` block lands. **Tests**: new domain + service + scenario test classes added; `./gradlew check` + 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. ADR-0121 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Phase 4**: `LoadoutSelectorShowIntentHandler` plugin wiring (paired with the 9 unwired ADR-0119 lobby intent handlers — same wave); GUI match summary screen with MVP / kill / damage breakdown (Wave 6b / Phase 4); `PvpDeathRule.DropOnly` per-item probability rolling (Wave 6 PaperInventoryMutator); main-hand item drop on `DropEquipped` (Wave 6 — needs Paper-side held-slot query); stage YAML `pvp_death:` block schema + `DefaultPvpDeathPolicyResolver.setStageOverride` activation from YAML loader (future stage-content wave); `BetMatch` formation in `StartMatchUseCase` (Wave 5bet follow-up — currently relies on `bet-1v1` modeKey + manual 2-team formation). **Single commit on branch `wave-5game-complete`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5server-content-ops (2026-05-30): **Server ops restart command + News/Changelog/LoginBonus content scaffolding** (ADR-0127 Accepted, §Round 13). Closes the 5 private-beta-readiness gaps a Round 12 polish audit surfaced: (1) no graceful in-process restart command — `IScheduledAnnouncementSystem` (Wave 5t / ADR-0086) + `IServerBootstrapper` (Wave 5w / ADR-0089) were already in place but no operator-facing verb wired them; (2) no `content/news/` directory; (3) no `content/changelog/` directory; (4) no `content/login-bonus/` directory; (5) no `/astera-news` or `/astera-patch` player command. **§A — `/astera-restart <minutes> [reason]`** (`plugin/core/.../application/command/admin/AsteraRestartCommandSpec.kt`): `IntegerArg(min=1, max=60)` minutes + Greedy reason; orchestration only — 1 chat broadcast at `now` via `AnnouncementBroadcastKind.Chat` + 4 Title waypoints at -30s / -10s / -3s / -1s before the restart instant (waypoints past `minutes * 60` are skipped) + `IScheduler.runLater((minutes*60-1).seconds) { bootstrapper.shutdown(ShutdownReason.Restart) }` so the Wave 5w 5-phase shutdown sequence fires at N-1s; gated by `Permission("astera.admin.restart")` via the existing `requirePermission` decorator. **§B — News domain + content** (`core.domain.news.{NewsEntry, NewsId, NewsSeverity}`): `NewsId` `JvmInline` value class w/ lower-kebab-case regex `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$` mirroring `AdvancementId`; `NewsSeverity` enum 3-variant (`Info / Important / Critical`); `NewsEntry(id, titleKey, bodyKey, publishedAt: Instant, expiresAt: Instant?, severity)` with `isExpired(now)` helper + `init` check that `expiresAt >= publishedAt`; new outbound port `INewsRegistry : Contents<NewsId, NewsEntry>` with `latest(limit: Int, now: Instant = Instant.now()): List<NewsEntry>` (filters expired, sorts publishedAt DESC, caps at limit); `NewsYamlConfig` kaml DTO (`title_key` / `body_key` / `published_at` / `expires_at?` / `severity`) → `NewsEntry`; `NewsContents : YamlDynamicContents<NewsId, NewsEntry, NewsYamlConfig>(categoryName = "news")`. **§C — `/astera-news [limit]`** (`AsteraNewsCommandSpec`): default limit 5, max 20; permission-free; renders 4 chat lines per entry (header → title with severity tag → body → footer with count) via `IPlayerGateway.sendMessage` + `IMessageRenderer.render`; player-only (console rejected with `astera.command.player-only`); read-tracking deferred to Phase 2 per ADR-0127. 2 sample YAMLs: `welcome-to-phase-1.yaml` (Info, server welcome) + `wave-6-incoming.yaml` (Important, advance notice). **§D — Changelog domain + content** (`core.domain.changelog.{ChangelogEntry, ChangelogVersion}`): `ChangelogVersion` `JvmInline` value class w/ strict `^\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+$` regex (rejects `-rc1` / `+build42` suffixes — broaden via follow-up ADR if Phase 2 needs pre-releases) + `Comparable<ChangelogVersion>` via lazy `components: Triple<Int,Int,Int>`; `ChangelogEntry(version, releasedAt: Instant, highlights: List<MessageKey>, full: MessageKey)` with `init` non-empty highlights check; `IChangelogRegistry : Contents<ChangelogVersion, ChangelogEntry>` with `latest()` / `forVersion(v) = find(v)` / `allNewestFirst()`; `ChangelogYamlConfig` → entry; `ChangelogContents : YamlDynamicContents(categoryName = "changelog")`. **§E — `/astera-patch [version]`** (`AsteraPatchCommandSpec`): no arg → latest entry + "use /astera-patch <version> for details" footer; with arg → that entry + full body; permission-free; player-only; 3 typed failure modes (`no_entries` / `invalid_version` / `unknown_version`). 1 sample YAML: `0.1.0.yaml` w/ 3 highlights + full body referencing RTM tracker. **§F — Login bonus skeleton** (Phase 2 ready): `core.domain.loginbonus.{LoginStreakRecord, LoginBonusReward, LoginBonusSpec, LoginBonusError}` — `LoginStreakRecord(playerId, streakDays: Int, lastClaimDate: LocalDate)` w/ `streakDays >= 0` init check; `LoginBonusReward(currency: Currency, amount: Long, items: List<ItemId>)` w/ `amount >= 0` init check; `LoginBonusSpec(day: Int, reward)` w/ `day >= 1` init check; `LoginBonusError` sealed 3-variant (`NotImplemented` Phase 1 placeholder / `AlreadyClaimedToday` / `NoRewardForDay(day)`), each w/ `messageKey: MessageKey`. 3 ports: `ILoginBonusRegistry : Contents<Int, LoginBonusSpec>` w/ `forDay(day) = find(day)`; `ILoginStreakRepository` w/ `findByPlayer / upsert`; `ILoginBonusService` w/ `claimDaily(playerId, today: LocalDate): Result<LoginBonusReward, LoginBonusError>` + `streakOf(playerId): LoginStreakRecord?`. 3 services: `LoginBonusContents` (custom — does **not** extend `YamlDynamicContents` because each YAML file describes a `days: [...]` list and the substrate's `toEntry: C → Pair<K, V>?` only allows one pair per file; instead reuses `ContentLoader` directly + atomic `AtomicReference<Map<Int, LoginBonusSpec>>` store + per-file expand to N entries + same atomic-reload semantics — ~70 LoC); `InMemoryLoginStreakRepository` (`ConcurrentHashMap`-backed; Phase 2 Postgres overlay at p=100); `DefaultLoginBonusService(registry, streakRepo)` returns `Result.failure(LoginBonusError.NotImplemented)` from `claimDaily` unconditionally + `streakOf` proxies to the repository (so Phase 1 GUI surfaces that *read* a streak already work). 1 sample YAML: `content/login-bonus/default.yaml` — 7-day STAR-currency rotation (10/15/20/30/45/65/100) + `astera:welcome-pack` item on day 7. **§G — i18n** (en+ja parity preserved): new keys under `astera.{restart,news,patch,changelog,loginbonus}.*` — `restart.{command.root, default-reason, error.out_of_range, announcement.{chat, title_30s, title_10s, title_3s, title_1s}}` × 9 / `news.{command.root, empty, header, footer, entry.{title, body}, welcome-to-phase-1.{title, body}, wave-6-incoming.{title, body}}` × 10 / `patch.{command.root, header, highlight, footer.{latest, full}, error.{no_entries, invalid_version, unknown_version}}` × 8 / `changelog.0_1_0.{highlight.1, highlight.2, highlight.3, full}` × 4 / `loginbonus.error.{not_implemented, already_claimed_today, no_reward_for_day}` × 3. Konsist `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files` passes. **§H — Plugin wiring** (`AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` Wave J block placed after the Wave G block): bootstrap each of the 3 new contents (NewsContents / ChangelogContents / LoginBonusContents) against `contentDir.resolve("news"|"changelog"|"login-bonus")`, register each at p=0 against its marker port (INewsRegistry / IChangelogRegistry / ILoginBonusRegistry), bind each to the cross-plugin `IReloadableContentsRegistry` (so `/astera-reload news` / `…changelog` / `…login-bonus` works uniformly); `ILoginStreakRepository` registered at p=0 (InMemory default — Phase 2 `:plugin:persistence` overlay at p=100); `ILoginBonusService` registered at p=0 (DefaultLoginBonusService); 3 commands registered via `commandHandler.register(...)` — the restart command consumes the local `bootstrapper` val from the Wave 5w lifecycle wiring block that precedes Wave J, no forward references; `SERVICE_NAMES` gained 5 new entries documenting the published surfaces. **§I — Tests**: `AsteraRestartCommandSpecTest` × 3 — (1) `5 minute restart schedules chat + 4 title waypoints + shutdown` asserts `CaptureAnnouncer` recorded exactly 1 Chat + 4 Title scheduled at restartAt - {30,10,3,1}s; (2) `shutdown is triggered after the scheduled delay` registers a probe `IServerLifecycleListener` against `InMemoryServerBootstrapper`, advances `FakeScheduler` to N-1s = 60s × 20 ticks, asserts `shutdownPhases == ShutdownPhase.ORDER.size` (every phase fired); (3) `out of range minutes returns Failure` rejects 999 with no announcements scheduled. **Konsist**: no new rules (53 → 53); every constraint we wanted was already covered by existing rules (`application layers must not import raw JVM throwable types` for `LoginBonusError.NotImplemented`, `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files` for the 34 new keys, plus the implicit "domain stays pure" coverage for news/changelog/loginbonus packages via the existing per-plugin domain purity rules — no `core.domain.{news,changelog,loginbonus}.*`-specific rule needed because nothing imports Bukkit / kotlinx.serialization / Exposed there). **No DB migration** (content YAML + InMemory only; Phase 2 will add Flyway up-only for `login_streak` table planned V44+). **Tests**: `./gradlew check` (full Konsist + all unit tests + Testcontainers ITs) + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. ADR-0127 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Phase 2 / Wave 6**: actual login-bonus claim flow (currency ledger debit + `IItemDelivery` grant + streak repository upsert); Postgres migration for `login_streak` table (Wave 6 onwards via `:plugin:persistence` overlay at p=100); read-tracking for `/astera-news` (per-player "last-read newsId" so the command can highlight unread vs read entries); `ChangelogVersion` pre-release / build-metadata suffix support (`-rc1`, `+build42` — follow-up ADR if needed); 1-tile per-day GUI surface for the login bonus rotation (Wave 10c+ when anvil-modal `IGuiModalGateway` primitive lands); `/astera-restart` reason rendering in the 4 Title waypoints (the chat preface carries the operator intent; Titles stay terse for legibility); `/astera-restart` cancel command (operator can `stop` the server if they need to abort; explicit cancel is a future ADR); admin command `/astera-news add` / `/astera-changelog publish` for runtime authoring (current flow is edit YAML + `/astera-reload <category>` — sufficient for Phase 1 PoC); per-severity formatting in the chat adapter (Wave 6b can colour Critical news red without touching the spec). **Single commit on branch `wave-5server-content-ops`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5content-depth (2026-05-27): **Content baseline expansion + Quest / Recipe / Achievement listener wire-up** (ADR-0125 Accepted, §Round 12). Closes the two Phase 1 PoC closing gaps Round 11 left: (1) per-category sample thinness — Wave 5s/5p/5cinematic/5pve/5e each shipped a single canonical sample, leaving authors with no breadth to read schemas from; (2) silent-drop events — `QuestStageCompleted` + `RecipeCrafted` + `AchievementUnlocked` all published with no surface-side subscriber, so a stage transition / craft / unlock was invisible to the player. **§A — 30 new content YAMLs across 10 categories** (lifting each to ≥3 files): skills (+3: flame-burst / frost-shard / lightning-bolt — fire/ice/electric kit pairing with status applies), mobs (+3: dark-zombie / cave-spider / nether-ghast — covers fodder + cave-poison + ranged-boss), stages (+2: forest-arena / boss-arena — PvE forest + boss-fight rings beside Wave 4.5's example-arena), quests (+2: forest-exploration 2-stage / item-collection 1-stage — both reuse Wave 5p `AdvancementCriteriaDto.{kill_count, matches_won, item_acquired}`), recipes (+3: iron-armor-set representative iron-helmet shaped / diamond-pickaxe shaped / golden-apple shaped — workbench station, kept consistent w/ Wave 5s `RecipeIngredientsDto.Shaped`), advancements (+3: first-mob-kill `TASK` / kills-100 `GOAL` parent=first-kill / boss-defeat `CHALLENGE` parent=kills-100 with `all_of(kill_count, matches_won)` composite — `kills-100` id chosen to honor `AdvancementId` `^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$` regex), shops (+2: iron-merchant `GOLD`-tier / rare-merchant `STAR`-tier with `stock.type: limited` — Currency enum has no IRON tier, so GOLD plays the common-coin role per the existing Wave 4.5 example-shop pattern), music (+2: boss-theme `mood: high_intensity` looping / victory-fanfare `mood: victory` one-shot — placeholder resource-pack paths under `astera:music/*`), status (+3: burn = BURN `damage_per_tick: 2.0` stackable / freeze = SPEED_DOWN `multiplier: 0.3` (no FREEZE family in `StatusKind` enum, slow is the closest) / poison = BURN longer-DoT to distinguish from burn), tutorials (+2: combat-tutorial = `await_skill_cast` chain through the new fire/ice/electric trio + shopkeeper dialog / skill-tutorial = narrower cast → buff loop using existing example-blast + example-buff). **§B — 3 new listeners**: `UpdateQuestProgressOnQuestStageCompletedListener` (`:plugin:core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/quest/`) subscribes core-domain `QuestStageCompleted`, pushes `HudLayout.ActionBar` stub via `IHudGateway` w/ message key forked on `nextStageId == null` (chain-completed vs stage-completed) + INFO chain-advance log; `NotifyOnRecipeCraftedListener` (`:plugin:core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/recipe/`) subscribes `RecipeCrafted`, sends chat via `IPlayerGateway.sendMessage` + `IMessageRenderer.render` w/ `{recipe, item, count}` placeholders + pushes `HudLayout.Subtitle` stub; `NotifyOnAchievementUnlockedListener` (`:plugin:stats/.../application/eventbus/listeners/`) subscribes stats-domain `AchievementUnlocked`, sends chat + Subtitle stub — lives in `:plugin:stats` because ADR-0022 forbids `:plugin:core` from importing `stats.domain.event.*`; parallel to (not replacing) Wave 5flex's `RefreshNameplateOnAchievementUnlockedListener` (separate concern: badge-projection rebuild vs player notification). **§C — i18n keys** added in parallel to `content/languages/{en,ja}.yaml` (parity preserved, Konsist `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files` passes): `astera.advancement.{first_mob_kill, kills_100, boss_defeat}.{title, description}` × 6 / `astera.achievement.unlocked.{chat, subtitle}` × 2 (new top-level namespace, distinct from existing `astera.advancement.*` catalog + `astera.stats.achievement.*` Wave 5e milestones) / `astera.recipe.crafted.{chat, subtitle}` × 2 / `astera.quest.{forest-exploration, item-collection}.*.{title, description}` × 5 / `astera.quest.progress.{stage-completed, chain-completed}` × 2 (listener) / `astera.tutorial.{combat_tutorial.title, combat.{intro, fire_done, ice_done, complete}, skill_tutorial.title, skill.{intro, first_cast, complete}}` × 9. **§D — Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` adds 2 new `wave5completeSubs += eventBus.subscribe(...)` blocks (QuestProgress + RecipeNotify listeners) right after the Wave 5match-hud ResourcePool wiring; `AsteraStatsPlugin.onEnable` drops the `@Suppress("UNUSED_VARIABLE")` on `messages` (now consumed) + appends a second `subscriptions += eventBus.subscribe(AchievementUnlocked::class) { notifyOnUnlock.handle(it) }` next to the existing nameplate-refresh subscriber. **§E — Tests**: 3 new listener test classes (9 tests total — `UpdateQuestProgressOnQuestStageCompletedListenerTest` × 3 / `NotifyOnRecipeCraftedListenerTest` × 3 / `NotifyOnAchievementUnlockedListenerTest` × 3) using `FakeHudGateway` / `FakePlayerGateway` / `FakeMessageRenderer` from `:plugin:test-fixtures`, asserting ordered ops + per-player invocation + correct MessageKey routing. **§F — Preexisting-bug fixes** (build was already broken at HEAD `2fdc352` — these unblock my own `./gradlew check`): `AdminOpsCommands.kt` `mentionsPlayer` gained the missing `is AuditEvent.ServerErrorReported -> false` branch (left out when Wave 5server-harden added the variant); `AsteraCorePlugin.kt` `RestoreInventoryOnPlayerJoinedListener` constructor call gained `sessionRepo` (left stale when Wave 5admin-ops added the parameter for double-join idempotency). **Konsist**: no new rules (53 → 53); existing rules all pass including `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files`, `application layers must not import raw JVM throwable types`, `every plugin onEnable subscribe must be assigned into a subscription holder (ADR-0123)`. **No DB migration** (content YAML only). **Tests**: `./gradlew check` (full Konsist + all unit tests + Testcontainers ITs) + 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green.
