ADR-0125 → Accepted. **Final category file counts** (target met everywhere): skills 8 (was 5) / mobs 8 (was 5) / stages 3 (was 1) / quests 3 (was 1) / recipes 5 (was 2) / advancements 5 (was 2) / shops 3 (was 1) / music 6 (was 4) / status 6 (was 3) / tutorials 4 (was 2). **Out of scope deferred to follow-up waves**: per-file i18n display keys for status / stage / shop content (existing convention is to skip — renderer falls back gracefully; future Konsist referential-integrity rule would force the issue); GUI achievement-popup adapter beyond the Subtitle stub (Wave 6b Paper popup adapter); `RecipeCrafted` → achievement progress bridge (already lives in `CraftRecipeUseCase` via `ItemAcquired` re-publish, no double-fire needed); content for items referenced by new recipes/mobs (`iron-helmet`, `diamond-pickaxe`, `golden-apple`, `astera:dark-zombie-drops`, etc. — loaders accept dangling refs at load time by design, gap surfaces at execution time). **Single commit on branch `wave-5content-depth`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave K 5integration-wire (2026-05-30): **Cross-feature critical integration wire** (ADR-0128 Accepted, §Round 14). Closes 7 Annihilation-mode-style silent drops a cross-feature audit surfaced: (1) `ComboRouteCompleted` published with no subscriber → combo finishers visually played but the follow-up strike dealt base damage; (2) `IFocusFireTracker.bonusMultiplierFor` never called by the damage pipeline → focus windows lit the HUD chip but did not amplify; (3) `ITargetLockController.consumeBonusFor` never called → lock acquisition fired the HUD event but ranged hits stayed base; (4) `TutorialCompleted` had no subscriber → onboarding loop had no closing milestone (no achievement, no reward); (5) `QuestStageCompleted` did not feed achievement progress (`rewards` list was the only path and it's wrong vocabulary); (6) `AchievementUnlocked` did not refresh the long-lived `PublicProfileCard` (Wave 5flex's nameplate listener covered the in-match cache but not the public profile); (7) `MatchEnded` did not refresh per-player engagement snapshots (5-min scheduler tick made the freshest signal in the player's session the most stale on the dashboard). **§A/B/C — Damage-bonus integration**: new outbound port `IDamageBonusSource(attempt, nowTick): Double` (`combat.application.port.outbound/`) + `ApplyDamageUseCase` ctor `bonusSources: List<IDamageBonusSource> = emptyList()` parameter — folded *after* `damageRule.resolve(...)` returns so combo / focus / target-lock amplify post-armor damage (a bonus must never turn a fully-absorbed hit into a chip hit); product of sources applied to `ResolvedDamage.finalAmount` and the boosted resolution is what `DamageDealt` publishes. Three impls in `combat.application.service.damagebonus/`: `ComboBonusSource` subscribes to `ComboRouteCompleted`, stores `pending[playerId] = bonusDamageMultiplier`, consumes on next attempt regardless of weapon (the chain was already weapon-gated when scored); `FocusFireBonusSource` stateless delegates to `IFocusFireTracker.bonusMultiplierFor(attacker, victim, atTick)`; `TargetLockBonusSource` ranged-only (`DamageSource.Projectile`) delegates to a new `PlayerId`-keyed `ITargetLockController.consumeBonusFor(attackerId, victimId, nowTick)` overload + new `armAgainstPlayer(attacker, victim, atTick)` companion method (existing `EntityHandle` overload preserved for Wave 6b's Paper adapter). All three sources wired in `AsteraCombatPlugin.onEnable` in fixed consume order (combo → focus → target-lock); `ComboBonusSource.subscription` joined to the plugin's `subscriptions` list so cancel-on-disable cleans it. **§D — Tutorial → Achievement + Reward chain**: `GrantAchievementOnTutorialCompletedListener` in `:plugin:stats/.../application/eventbus/listeners/onboarding/` subscribes core-domain `TutorialCompleted` and calls `UnlockAchievementUseCase.unlock(playerId, AchievementId("tutorial-graduate"))`; `GrantRewardOnTutorialCompletedListener` in `:plugin:economy/.../application/eventbus/listeners/onboarding/` subscribes the same event and calls `DepositUseCase.execute(playerId, Currency.STAR, 50, CurrencyChangeReason.TutorialReward)` — both listeners are independent (either firing without the other is still a partial close); new sealed-interface variant `CurrencyChangeReason.TutorialReward` distinguishes tutorial deposits from match rewards in analytics; `content/advancements/tutorial-graduate.yaml` + matching en/ja i18n keys (`astera.advancement.tutorial_graduate.{title, description}`) land alongside. **§E — Quest → Achievement progress chain**: `QuestStage` domain gains optional `linkedAchievementId: AchievementId?` field (and `QuestStageDto` an `linked_achievement_id: String?` YAML field — pre-release breaking change tolerated per CLAUDE.md §Pre-release posture); new `UpdateAchievementProgressOnQuestStageCompletedListener` in `:plugin:stats/.../application/eventbus/listeners/quest/` resolves the stage via `IQuestChainRegistry`, reads `linkedAchievementId`, and unlocks the achievement (one-shot in Phase 1 — no multi-tick progress counter yet; field name keeps the path open for a counter-based model later); `content/quests/forest-exploration.yaml`'s `prove-prowess` stage links to the new `explorer` achievement (`content/advancements/explorer.yaml` + en/ja `astera.advancement.explorer.{title, description}` i18n keys). **§F — AchievementUnlocked → ProfileCard recompute**: `RecomputeProfileCardOnAchievementUnlockedListener` in `:plugin:stats/.../application/eventbus/listeners/flex/` subscribes stats-domain `AchievementUnlocked` and calls `RecomputeProfileCardUseCase.recompute(playerId)` — independent from Wave 5flex's nameplate listener (different cache, different invalidation cadence; both subscribe in parallel). **§G — MatchEnded → Engagement snapshot recompute**: `EngagementDashboardService` gains `recomputeForPlayer(playerId)` method — re-stamps the player's existing snapshot's `lastSeenAt = now` + recomputes the score via `EngagementScoringPolicy`; no-op if the player has no prior snapshot (`recordPlayerActivity` hasn't fired yet); new `RecomputeEngagementSnapshotOnMatchEndedListener` in `:plugin:match/.../application/eventbus/listeners/engagement/` resolves the match via `IMatchRepository`, walks every team, and calls `recomputeForPlayer` for each participant (same indirection pattern as `RecordMatchPlayedOnMatchEndedListener`; `MatchEnded` only carries `MatchId`, not the roster). **§H — Plugin wiring**: `AsteraCombatPlugin` instantiates 3 bonus sources + threads them through `ApplyDamageUseCase` ctor + subscribes `ComboBonusSource.subscription`; `AsteraStatsPlugin` wires 3 new listener subscriptions (TutorialCompleted, QuestStageCompleted, AchievementUnlocked) next to the existing milestone listeners; `AsteraEconomyPlugin` wires 1 new TutorialCompleted subscription next to the existing MissionCompleted block; `AsteraMatchPlugin` wires 1 new MatchEnded subscription next to TeleportToLobbyOnMatchEnded (resolves `EngagementDashboardService` from the registry). **§I — Tests**: 9 new test classes covering all 7 wires — `ComboTriggerScenarioTest` (combo chain → next damage × 2.0 then disarms; `:plugin:combat`), `FocusFireScenarioTest` × 2 (3 attackers amplify × 1.5; 2 attackers don't trigger; `:plugin:combat`), `TargetLockScenarioTest` × 2 (armed lock amplifies ranged hit × 1.8 then disarms; melee is ignored by the ranged-only filter; `:plugin:combat`), `TutorialCompletionScenarioTest` × 3 (unlock + dedup + NotInCatalog graceful; `:plugin:stats`), `QuestProgressionScenarioTest` × 3 (linked stage unlocks; unlinked stage no-ops; unknown chain no-ops; `:plugin:stats`), `ProfileCardRecomputeScenarioTest` × 2 (recompute persists; missing display name no-ops; `:plugin:stats`), `EngagementRecomputeScenarioTest` × 2 (per-participant recompute + lastSeenAt advance; unknown match no-ops; `:plugin:match`), `GrantRewardOnTutorialCompletedListenerTest` × 2 (50 STAR with TutorialReward reason; custom amount + currency override; `:plugin:economy`). **§J — i18n parity** (en + ja parity preserved): 4 new keys total under `astera.advancement.{tutorial_graduate.{title, description}, explorer.{title, description}}`. **§K — Konsist unchanged** (53 → 53): no new architectural rule; all new code follows existing patterns (`IDamageBonusSource` is `fun interface` in `application.port.outbound/` mirroring sibling ports, listeners follow the `<Verb>On<Event>Listener` naming convention, log levels follow the existing INFO-on-success/DEBUG-on-no-op/WARN-on-misconfig discipline). **No DB migration** (pure wire + 1 new sealed-interface variant + 1 new optional domain field). **Tests**: `./gradlew check` (full Konsist 53 + all unit tests + Testcontainers ITs across all 7 plugins + persistence) + 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. ADR-0128 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / future ADR**: multi-tick `IAchievementService.incrementProgress` counter for progressive achievement unlocks (currently quest stage = 1 achievement unlock; future supersede ADR introduces counter persistence); `IItemDelivery` invocation from `GrantRewardOnTutorialCompletedListener` (port requires `IMcPlayer` + `ItemSpec`, neither resolvable at MatchEnded scope — STAR credit alone is the Phase 1 reward); per-player engagement snapshot recompute that re-pulls raw `totalPlaytime` / `loginStreakDays` / `skillsExercisedCount` from their owning ports (Wave K only re-stamps `lastSeenAt`; pulling inputs would require new ports per source); `armAgainstPlayer` invocation from a Paper-side `tickAim` resolver (Wave 6b — current method is exposed for tests + adapter to call when `EntityHandle ↔ Player` resolves). **Single commit on branch `wave-5integration-wire`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave I 5match-wireup-fix (2026-05-30): **Match listener wire-up + Private-beta UX** (ADR-0126 Accepted, §Round 13). Closes a **CRITICAL** regression caught by the Round 13 audit: 4 listener classes shipped by Wave 5game-complete (ADR-0121 §A/§C/§D/§E — `AnnihilationWinConditionListener` / `MatchCountdownListener` / `MatchEndSummaryListener` / `ApplyPvpDeathPolicyListener`) were never subscribed in `AsteraMatchPlugin.onEnable`, so `AnnihilationMatchTracker.evaluateWinCondition` was read-only and Annihilation matches **never ended**. Plus 3 Private-beta UX gaps Round 12 deferred. **§A — 4 listener wire-up + `DefaultPvpDeathPolicyResolver` registration**: `subscriptions += eventBus.subscribe(MatchStarted::class) { matchCountdownListener.handle(it) }` + `subscriptions += eventBus.subscribe(MatchEnded::class) { matchEndSummaryListener.handle(it) }` + `subscriptions += eventBus.subscribe(PlayerKilled::class) { applyPvpDeathPolicyListener.handle(it) }` + `subscriptions += annihilationWinConditionListener.subscribe()` (listener owns its dual TeamEliminated + AnnihilationPhaseTransitioned subscribe internally); all four flow into the existing `subscriptions` list so Wave 5server-harden (ADR-0123 §4) cancel-on-disable cleans them. `DefaultPvpDeathPolicyResolver` registered at priority 0 via the `IPvpDeathPolicyResolver` port (previously never registered — `ApplyPvpDeathPolicyListener` would have NPE'd if it had been wired). **§B — `LoadoutSelectorShowIntentHandler` wireup**: wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` next to the Wave G handler block via the shared `IGuiIntentRouter` on the `loadout.selector.` prefix; joins `waveGRegistrations` so `onDisable` releases the captured handler lambda alongside the Wave G handlers (single cleanup path). Closes the silent-no-op on the lobby Customize-menu Loadout tile. **§C — `ReconnectCountdownHudListener`** (new, `plugin/match/.../application/eventbus/listeners/hud/`): subscribes `PlayerDisconnectedFromMatch` + `PlayerReconnectedToMatch`; on non-null `willTimeoutAtTick` starts a 20-tick repeating `IScheduler` task that pushes `HudLayout.ActionBar` with one of three coarse i18n keys (`astera.match.reconnect.countdown.{long,medium,short}` for ≥2 min / ≥1 min / <1 min); on expiry pushes one final `astera.match.reconnect.expired` frame and self-cancels; `PlayerReconnectedToMatch` cancels the running task. Per-player `IScheduler.TaskHandle`s tracked in a `ConcurrentHashMap`. **Minute-grain rather than `2:45`-style** because `HudComponent.Text` is i18n-key-only — second-precision deferred to Wave 6b `HudComponent.TextWithPlaceholders`. **§D — `AfkWarningHudListener`** (new, `plugin/core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/afk/`): subscribes `PlayerAfkWarned` (push `astera.match.afk.warning` to ActionBar) + `PlayerAfkKicked` (explicit `IHudGateway.clearFor(HudRegion.ACTION_BAR)`). Recovery-to-Active doesn't publish an event by `AfkSweepScheduler` design — the ActionBar fades naturally via its `ttlTicks` once the sweeper stops re-publishing. **§E — `LoadingProgressListener`** (new, `plugin/core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/loading/`): subscribes the Round 8 travel events `PlayerTraveledToPveWorld` / `PlayerArrivedAtIsland` / `PlayerReturnedToLobby`; pushes one `HudLayout.Subtitle` per arrival with destination-specific i18n key. **Static placeholder, not real 0 %→100 % progress** — deferred to Wave 6b's PaperWorldGateway integration (async-load polling is gateway-impl-shaped). **§F — i18n parity** (en + ja parity preserved): 9 new keys total under `astera.match.{reconnect.countdown.{long,medium,short}, reconnect.expired, afk.warning, loading.{pve-world,island,lobby}}`. **§G — Konsist unchanged** (53 → 53): no new architectural rule; pure wireup + small listeners follow existing patterns. **§H — Regression test**: new `AnnihilationWinConditionEndToEndScenarioTest` (`plugin/match/src/test/.../scenario/`) exercises the full chain — start Annihilation, seed tracker with both teams, advance through Phase 1 → Phase 2 → Phase 3 (opens Tier 3), `recordDamage(redTier3, pillarHp)` → tracker publishes `TeamEliminated` → listener calls `IMatchLifecycle.end(ObjectiveCompleted)` → `EndMatchUseCase` publishes `MatchEnded`. Second test asserts `AnnihilationWinConditionListener.subscribe()` returns both event subscriptions so cancel-on-disable covers both paths. If the §A wire-up regresses, the `MatchEnded.hasSize(1)` assertion fails with a guidance message pointing back to this ADR. **Tests**: `./gradlew check` (full Konsist + all unit tests + Testcontainers ITs across all 7 plugins + persistence) + `:plugin:match:shadowJar` + `:plugin:core:shadowJar` 全 green. ADR-0126 → Accepted. **No RTM rows flipped** — all 4 listener-class RTM rows were already ✅ since Wave 5game-complete (the rows tracked class existence, not wiring); the regression target was the unhappy-path runtime behavior, not the catalog. **No DB migration** (pure wireup + 3 in-memory listeners). **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6 / Wave 6b**: second-precision reconnect countdown via `HudComponent.TextWithPlaceholders` (Wave 6b); real loading-screen 0 %→100 % progress via PaperWorldGateway async-load polling (Wave 6b); GUI match summary screen (Wave 6b / Phase 4 — already noted in ADR-0121 §D); `PvpDeathRule.DropOnly` per-item probability rolling (Wave 6 PaperInventoryMutator); main-hand item drop on `DropEquipped` (Wave 6 — Paper-side held-slot query); stage-YAML `pvp_death:` block (future stage-content wave). **Single commit on branch `wave-5match-wireup-fix`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave M 5polish-quickwin (2026-05-30): **Polish quick wins** (ADR-0130 Accepted, §Round 14). Closes the five polish gaps a Round 14 audit surfaced — each individually small, but each filling a "feels half-finished" gap. **§A — Friend tile dynamic binding** (`plugin/core/.../application/gui/binding/PlayerFriendsListGuiBindingResolver.kt`): implements `IGuiBindingResolver` for `player.friends.list.0..8`; reads `IFriendshipRepository` + `IPlayerSessionRepository` lazily, returns one of 3 tile variants (`indicator-friend-online` if accepted + session active, `indicator-friend-offline` if accepted + no session, `indicator-friend-pending` for pending); `BLOCKED`/`DECLINED` omitted from the list. New `CompositeGuiBindingResolver` (same package) wraps the existing `DefaultGuiBindingResolver` so adding new resolvers does not rewrite the renderer — mutable `addDelegate`/`removeDelegate` hooks let feature plugins extend the chain at their own onEnable. `FriendsListGuiSpec` (Wave 5lobby-content) gained 9 reactive slots (0..8) bound to the new keys; old close/add/pending tiles preserved. **§B — Achievement viewer GUI** (`plugin/core/.../application/gui/lobby/AchievementViewerGuiSpec.kt` + `LobbyAchievementShowIntentHandler.kt` + `plugin/stats/.../application/service/gui/PlayerAchievementListGuiBindingResolver.kt`): `CHEST_54` viewer with 45 reactive tiles bound to `player.achievement.list.<n>`; intent prefix `lobby.achievement.` opens via the existing shared `IGuiIntentRouter`. The catalog projection lives in `:plugin:stats` (can read `IAchievementRepository`) — registered onto Core's composite via `compositeBindingResolver.addDelegate(...)` at `AsteraStatsPlugin.onEnable`. Unlocked tiles render as `indicator-achievement-unlocked`, locked as `indicator-achievement-locked`; achievement nameKey rendered through `displayOverride`. Companion YAML `content/gui/achievement-viewer.yaml` ships reload-aware. **§C — Hotbar bind UI** (`plugin/core/.../application/gui/lobby/intent/BindQuickbarSlotIntentHandler.kt`): two-step flow — `lobby.hotbar.bind.<N>` opens a `GuiModalSpec.QuickbarSelector` modal listing available skills; modal `onSelect` fires `lobby.hotbar.bind.commit.<N>` with payload `skillId` and the handler writes the entry to `PlayerSettings.quickCastSlots` via `IPlayerSettingsRepository.upsert`. Skill enumeration is a `() -> List<String>` provider — Combat plugin supplies it from `SkillContents.all()` because Core cannot import Combat (ADR-0022 sibling-isolation), and the router registration lives in `AsteraCombatPlugin.onEnable`. Phase 1 caps modal at 18 entries (`GuiModalSpec.QuickbarSelector.MAX_QUICKBAR_SLOTS`); pagination deferred. **§D — Tooltip provider register (sample 2)** (`plugin/items/.../application/service/tooltip/`): two providers registered at `AsteraItemsPlugin.onEnable` against Core's `ITooltipRegistry` — `RarityGlowTooltipProvider` under `TooltipScope.AllItems` surfaces a per-rarity tooltip line (`astera.tooltip.rarity.{common, uncommon, rare, epic, legendary}` via `HudColor` map per tier); `RefinementTierTooltipProvider` registered under `TooltipScope.ByRarity(RARE/EPIC/LEGENDARY)` looks up the viewer's owned `ItemInstance`s of the hovered item and surfaces a tier line when the highest layer count > 0. Both stay vendor-neutral; scope evaluation + per-viewer fan-out happens in the Wave 5gui-depth registry. **§E — Server population HUD listener** (`plugin/core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/hud/UpdatePopulationHudOnPlayerJoinedListener.kt`): listener pair (joined / quit) subscribes `PlayerJoinedServer` / `PlayerQuitServer`; recomputes the active-session count from `IPlayerSessionRepository.all()` (excluding `Disconnected`) and pushes a `HudLayout.Sidebar` (`astera.population.online.title` + `.line`) to every `SessionState.Lobby` player via `IHudGateway.showFor`. Decoupled from Wave 5w `LobbyWaitingRoomHud` (queue concern vs population concern); separate listener pattern keeps both surfaces independently swappable. **i18n parity preserved** (en+ja): new keys `astera.gui.binding.friend.{online, offline, pending}` × 3 + `astera.gui.achievement-viewer.{title, tile.tooltip}` × 2 + `astera.gui.friends-list.tile.tooltip` × 1 + `astera.tooltip.rarity.{common, uncommon, rare, epic, legendary}` × 5 + `astera.tooltip.refinement.tier` × 1 + `astera.population.online.{title, line}` × 2 + `astera.hotbar.bind.{no-skills, commit-ok, skill.tooltip}` × 3. Konsist `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files` passes. **Konsist unchanged** (53 → 53): no new architectural rule; pure GUI / listener / provider additions follow existing patterns. **No DB migration** (per ADR-0130 § Pre-release posture — pure additions). **Tests**: 6 new test classes (29 tests) — `PlayerFriendsListGuiBindingResolverTest` × 8 / `CompositeGuiBindingResolverTest` × 4 / `PlayerAchievementListGuiBindingResolverTest` × 6 / `BindQuickbarSlotIntentHandlerTest` × 6 / `UpdatePopulationHudOnPlayerJoinedListenerTest` × 3 / `RarityGlowTooltipProviderTest` × 5 / `RefinementTierTooltipProviderTest` × 5 (total 37 tests). `./gradlew check` (full Konsist + all unit tests + Testcontainers ITs across all 7 plugins + persistence) + 7 plugin shadowJars 全 green. ADR-0130 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Phase 2 / Wave 6**: friend-list pagination beyond 9 entries (Phase 2); achievement viewer pagination beyond 45 tiles (Phase 2); current-binding preview + clear-binding option for the quickbar modal (Phase 2 follow-up); per-instance refinement tier resolution in `RefinementTierTooltipProvider` (requires widening the Wave 5gui-depth tooltip lookup signature to carry `ItemInstanceId` — Wave 6+); Paper sidebar dedupe to avoid scoreboard flicker on every join/quit (Wave 6b PaperHudGateway). **Single commit on branch `wave-5polish-quickwin`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 5konsist-harden (2026-05-31): **Konsist architecture hardening** (ADR-0131 Accepted, §Round 15). Adds 1 new Konsist rule + fixes 1 filter bug in existing rule; **no RTM game-feature rows flipped** (infrastructure-only commit). **§A — Rule 54: Listener wiring leak prevention**: adds `tools/architecture-test/src/test/kotlin/.../ArchitectureTest.kt` new `@Test fun` rule targeting `platform/Astera*Plugin.kt` files. Scans all `val [a-z][A-Za-z0-9]*Listener` variable declarations and verifies that each listener variable is referenced at least once in the same file after its declaration (i.e., not a silent no-op). Catches the Wave I / Wave K pattern where a listener is instantiated but never subscribed, triggering no-op during plugin boot and leaving match/event handling dead until caught 50+ commits later (Annihilation matches never ended; cross-feature integration events dropped). Rule comment enumerates acceptable subsequent-reference patterns: `.subscribe(...)` / `.handle(...)` / `.onXxx(...)` / `.bind(...)` / `.start(...)` / `.registerEvents(...)` / field assignment / registry registration. Failing listeners listed by file:line with AssertJ + ADR-0131 backref for quick remediation. **§B — ADR-0123 filter bug fix (incidental)**: existing rule `every plugin onEnable subscribe must be assigned into a subscription holder (ADR-0123)` was using `.endsWith("Plugin.kt")` filter on Konsist `KoFile.name`, which returns basename **without extension** (e.g., `AsteraCorePlugin`). Filter never matched any file, so ADR-0123 rule was silently green on 0 plugin files examined. Fix: change filter to `.endsWith("Plugin")` (no `.kt`). Now rule correctly scans all 6 plugin *Plugin files and verifies the existing invariant (all `.subscribe()` calls must be `.subscribe(...).also { subscriptions += it }` or similar). Fix has zero functional impact; ADR-0123 rule remains count-invariant (28 prior); only coverage gap is closed. **Konsist rule count: 53 → 54** (Rule 54 new; ADR-0123 unchanged). **No RTM row flips** (architecture QA only; no domain / application / Paper code). **No DB migration** (pure Konsist infrastructure). **No domain impact** (codebase already green on both new rule + filter fix; no violations to remediate). **Tests**: Konsist rule itself is a test; all 53+1 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew :tools:architecture-test:test` + `./gradlew check` (full suite) BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0131 → Accepted. **Single commit on branch `wave-5konsist-harden`; not pushed (user reviews + pushes manually per Wave protocol).**

- Wave 6a-redesign-step3 5paper-rewrite (2026-06-02): **Paper adapter rewrite — Astera owns damage math + Bukkit is UI mirror only (Step 3 of 3)** (ADR-0151 Accepted, supersedes ADR-0145; §Round 19). Closes the 3-step Wave 6a-redesign per ADR-0149. **§A — PaperPlayerHealthGateway** rewritten as thin wrapper over container-backed InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway (Step 2 / ADR-0150). Constructor gains `containerStore: IPlayerAttributeContainerStore`. Every mutation delegates to in-memory gateway → on Success calls `mirrorToBukkit(playerId)` which reads container CURRENT_HP/MAX_HP and computes `mirror = ((asteraHp / asteraMax) * bukkitMax).coerceAtLeast(MIRROR_MIN_HP=1.0).coerceAtMost(bukkitMax)`. Floor of 1.0 prevents vanilla PlayerDeathEvent. ~200 → 130 LOC. **§B — PaperDamageGateway** complete rewrite (supersedes ADR-0146 implementation). Constructor `Server + IScheduler + IPlayerAttributeContainerStore`. Flow: getPlayer → isDead/noDamageTicks short-circuits → appliedThisTick.add → read container CURRENT_HP → newHp = (currentHp - resolved.finalAmount).coerceAtLeast(0.0) Kotlin arithmetic NO vanilla math → container.set(CURRENT_HP, NumberValue(newHp)) → mirror to player.health (clamped ≥ MIRROR_MIN_HP=1.0, ≤ bukkitMax) → playHurtAnimation(0f) for vanilla feedback → noDamageTicks reset → return Killed (newHp ≤ 0.0) or HpReduced. ~115 → 130 LOC. Wave K combat-bonus chain end-to-end live in Paper. **§C — PaperItemMaterializer cleanup**: 5 of 7 combat-math vanilla AttributeModifier writes REMOVED (ATTACK_DAMAGE, ATTACK_SPEED, ARMOR, ARMOR_TOUGHNESS, MAX_HEALTH) per ADR-0149 §F. KEPT: KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE (Wave 6a-1 ADR-0144 data-channel) + MOVEMENT_SPEED (Astera setMultiplier last-writer-wins Phase 1 acceptable). Vanilla MC tooltips ("+5 attack damage") lost; Astera lore renderer fills the gap. **§D — Konsist Rule 57 strengthened**: replaces "must contain appliedThisTick + isDead" with stronger version: required tokens `appliedThisTick` + `isDead` + `Attributes.CURRENT_HP`; forbidden patterns `player.damage(` AND `player.health = 0` / `player.health=0` (two literal whitespace variants). Net rule count: 58 → 58 (Rule 57 strengthened, no new rule added). **§E — KDoc anti-pattern hygiene**: KDoc references to forbidden patterns rewritten in prose form (e.g. "Bukkit's Damageable `damage(amount)` method") to avoid Konsist false-positive on documentation. Same pattern as Step 2 InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway. **§F — BukkitEventAdapter defensive cancellation DEFERRED to Wave 6a-6** per ADR-0149 §D. Vanilla intrusion paths (lava/void/fall) currently un-handled; acceptable Phase 1 PoC because demo/staff sessions don't expose players to vanilla damage sources. **AsteraCorePlugin wiring** updated to pass attributeContainerStore into PaperPlayerHealthGateway. **AsteraCombatPlugin wiring** updated to inject IPlayerAttributeContainerStore via services.getOrThrow into PaperDamageGateway. **ADR-0145 status → Superseded** (PaperPlayerHealthGateway design fully rewritten). **Konsist 58 → 58**. **No DB migration**. **Tests**: 58 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0151 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6a-6**: BukkitEventAdapter EntityDamageEvent + PlayerDeathEvent defensive cancellation (vanilla intrusion handling); 15+ Bukkit→DomainEvent translations (ADR-0134 allowlist closure). Phase 2+: shared MIRROR_MIN_HP constant centralization; Astera-side death sequence listener consuming PlayerKilled (currently RespawnUseCase exists but not wired to Astera death). **3-step Wave 6a-redesign sequence complete**: Step 1 ADR-0149 (design) + Step 2 ADR-0150 (InMemory container migration) + Step 3 ADR-0151 (Paper rewrite). Astera-internal HP / death model fully landed. **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-redesign-step3-paper-rewrite`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-redesign-step2 5hp-container-migration (2026-06-02): **InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway → IAttributeContainer migration (Step 2)** (ADR-0150 Accepted, §Round 19). Step 2 of 3 in Wave 6a-redesign per ADR-0149. Closes the Wave 5e ADR-0058 stalled-migration window for HP. **§A** Constructor gains `containerStore: IPlayerAttributeContainerStore`; internal HP/maxHP MutableMaps removed; regen map (scheduler handles) stays. **§B** All HP reads → `(container.get(Attributes.CURRENT_HP) as? NumberValue)?.value`; all writes → `container.set(key, NumberValue(v))`. Same for MAX_HP. `getCurrentHealth/getMaxHealth` preserve "null = no value recorded" semantic via container.get null-return. **§C** AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable wiring passes the already-instantiated `attributeContainerStore` (Wave 5e line ~715) into constructor; `@Suppress("UNUSED_VARIABLE") attrStoreRef` field promoted from registry-decorative to load-bearing. **§D** 2 test sites updated to inject InMemoryPlayerAttributeContainer (PlayerHealthGatewayTest, RespawnUseCaseTest); no assertion bodies changed — container get/set round-trip preserves prior semantics exactly. **§E — Konsist Rule 58** (`InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway must use IAttributeContainer (ADR-0150)`): text-scan source MUST contain `containerStore` token AND MUST NOT contain `MutableMap<PlayerId, Double>` / `mutableMapOf<PlayerId, Double>` exact patterns. Born already-green. Future refactor reintroducing internal HP map fails CI with fail message pointing at ADR-0149 §A + ADR-0150 §B. Rule is narrow: only the exact HP-shape Kotlin type signatures forbidden; MutableMap<PlayerId, RegenSlot> (scheduler-handle map that stays) not forbidden; KDoc prose references to "internal HP map" not flagged. **Konsist 57 → 58**. **No DB migration**. **Wave 6a-2 main-branch PaperPlayerHealthGateway commit still supersede-pending** — Step 3 will rewrite as UI mirror. **Tests**: 58 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0150 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Step 3**: Paper adapter rewrite (PaperPlayerHealthGateway as UI mirror, PaperDamageGateway as container-consumer); PaperItemMaterializer 5 vanilla AttributeModifier removal; Konsist Rule 57 strengthening; Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter defensive cancellation. Phase 2+: modifier-based HP writes (addModifier with sourceId) for layered armor-set / buff / curse stacks. **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-redesign-step2-container`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-redesign-step1 5astera-internal-hp-design (2026-06-02): **ADR-0149 Astera-internal HP / death model — design-only supersede record** (Accepted, §Round 19). Architectural correction in response to user directives: (1) "バニラは基本的にコントローラブルじゃない、基本独自実装が望ましい"; (2) "Astera 管理下では vanilla で言う死はない、内部 HP=0 で内部イベント発行のみ". Code change zero (design ADR only). **Supersede targets**: (i) **ADR-0146 entirely** — PaperDamageGateway will be re-implemented in Step 3 per ADR-0149 §E (no `player.damage()`, no `setHealth(0)`, container-as-source-of-truth, MIRROR_MIN_HP=1.0 floor); (ii) **ADR-0080 §HP / two-tier mutation layer framing partially superseded** — IPlayerSpeedGateway / IPlayerFoodGateway / IResourcePoolService sections remain Accepted (no vanilla-math/vanilla-death problem); only the "Bukkit runtime mutation layer" claim for HP is reversed. **§A** HP source of truth = `IAttributeContainer.CURRENT_HP / MAX_HP` (keys already in Attributes.kt:16-17 since Wave 5e). **§B** IPlayerHealthGateway refactored to thin facade over container (Step 2). **§C** Bukkit player.health is UI mirror with formula `((asteraHp / asteraMax) * bukkitMax).coerceAtLeast(MIRROR_MIN_HP)` where `MIRROR_MIN_HP = 1.0` prevents vanilla `PlayerDeathEvent` (vanilla fires at health ≤ 0). **§D** No vanilla PlayerDeathEvent under Astera management — defensive cancellation in Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter for EntityDamageEvent + PlayerDeathEvent. **§E** PaperDamageGateway redesign (Step 3): read container CURRENT_HP → subtract finalAmount → write container → update Bukkit mirror (clamped) → playHurtAnimation for feedback → noDamageTicks for i-frame; never calls player.damage() or sets player.health = 0. **§F** PaperItemMaterializer:215-235 combat-math vanilla AttributeModifier removal (5 of 7): ATTACK_DAMAGE / ATTACK_SPEED / ARMOR / ARMOR_TOUGHNESS / MAX_HEALTH; KEPT: KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE (Wave 6a-1 ADR-0144 intentional data-channel) + MOVEMENT_SPEED (Astera setMultiplier last-writer wins acceptable Phase 1). **§G** Konsist Rule 57 strengthening (Step 3): adds `Attributes.CURRENT_HP` token requirement + forbids `player.damage(` and `player.health\s*=\s*0` patterns. **3-step migration plan**: Step 1 = ADR-0149 (this, design only); Step 2 = ADR-0150 (InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway refactor + Konsist Rule §5 HP extension); Step 3 = ADR-0151 (Paper adapter re-impl + PaperItemMaterializer cleanup + Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter defensive cancellation). **Konsist unchanged** (57 → 57). **No DB migration**. **Wave 6a-2 + 6a-3 main-branch commits remain as supersede-pending code paths** until Step 2/3 ship (ADR-0149 declaration makes intent immediate; code follows). **Wave 6a-1 (knockback) + Wave 6a-4 (speed) NOT superseded** — setVelocity / walkSpeed are direct writes with no vanilla math intermediate. **Tests**: existing 57 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green (no code change). **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-redesign-step1-adr`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-4 5paper-sweep-speed (2026-06-02): **PaperPlayerSpeedGateway — Wave 6a-4** (ADR-0147 Accepted, §Round 19). Fourth Paper sweep sub-wave; mirrors InMemoryPlayerSpeedGateway shape with Bukkit writeback for 5 ADR-0080 locomotion knobs. **§A — Impl** (`plugin/core/.../adapter/paper/physiology/`, ~280 LOC): constructor Server + IScheduler + IDomainEventBus. Identical TTL-tracking maps to InMemory (walkMul/walkExpiry/flyMul/flyExpiry/flightAllowed/flightExpiry/sprinting/sprintingExpiry/noClip/noClipExpiry). 5 mutation methods follow the same shape: validate args → state update → cancel old expiry → Bukkit writeback if online (`onlinePlayer(playerId)?.let { ... }`) → schedule one-shot expiry via IScheduler.runLaterTicks if ttl != MAX → publish event. Bukkit mappings: WALK multiplier → walkSpeed = (0.2 * m).toFloat().coerceIn(-1f, 1f) (Bukkit vanilla baseline); FLY → flySpeed = (0.1 * m).toFloat().coerceIn(-1f, 1f); allowFlight → player.allowFlight; sprinting → player.isSprinting; no-clip → player.isCollidable = !noClip (Bukkit no-clip-without-GameMode-change path per port KDoc — walks through entities; world geometry collision unchanged; true no-clip = GameMode.SPECTATOR is ISpectatorGateway scope). **§B — Offline semantics**: mutations still update local state map (so currentMultiplier/isFlightAllowed returns intended setting + takes effect on next login) but skip Bukkit writeback with DEBUG log. Returns Result.success(Unit), NOT BackendUnavailable — distinguishes from IPlayerHealthGateway which DOES return BackendUnavailable because Bukkit Player.health is the source of truth. **§C — No Konsist guard**: contract surface too small (5 method bodies, each 1 Bukkit setter). IPlayerSpeedGateway interface is compile-checked + InMemory behavioural test contract catches refactor regressions. **§D — Registration**: priority 100 in AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable between InMemoryPlayerSpeedGateway priority 0 and the next gateway (InMemoryPlayerFoodGateway). **PaperPlayerFoodGateway pulled to Wave 6a-5** (introduces Bukkit @EventHandler listener for FoodLevelChangeEvent cancel-when-suppressed — new Paper adapter pattern in Astera deserves own ADR treatment without diluting this Speed-only commit). **Konsist unchanged** (57 → 57). **No DB migration**. **Tests**: no PaperPlayerSpeedGateway unit test (no MockBukkit dep); InMemory domain coverage + Bukkit setter calls trivially verifiable by review. 57 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0147 → Accepted. **Out of scope**: Wave 6a-5 PaperPlayerFoodGateway (foodLevel/saturation + FoodLevelChangeEvent listener); per-player walkSpeed baseline override for servers using non-vanilla baselines (Phase 2+ if demanded). **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-4-paper-speed`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-3 5paper-sweep-damage (2026-05-31): **PaperDamageGateway — Wave 6a-3** (ADR-0146 Accepted, §Round 19). Third Paper sweep sub-wave; closes ADR-0137 §Path 1 contract + lands Konsist Rule 57. **§A — PaperDamageGateway impl** (`plugin/combat/.../adapter/paper/`, ~115 LOC): constructor `Server` + `IScheduler`. apply(target, resolved, source) flow per ADR-0137 Path 1 code sketch: getPlayer null → TargetOffline; player.isDead → TargetAlreadyDead; noDamageTicks > 0 → Immune (Bukkit i-frame semantics); appliedThisTick.add(target) BEFORE Damageable.damage so Bukkit's synchronous PlayerDeathEvent firing inside damage() sees the marker; player.damage(resolved.finalAmount) → Bukkit vanilla particles + sound + i-frame tick reset; read post-damage player.health → Killed (≤ 0.0) or HpReduced(remainingHp). ApplyDamageUseCase publishes PlayerKilled on DamageOutcome.Killed (line 196-205, unchanged). **§B — appliedThisTick marker lifecycle**: public val MutableSet<PlayerId> = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet() so Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath can read across class boundary; cleared by 1-tick IScheduler.runRepeatingTicks(1L,1L) task started via PaperDamageGateway.start() invoked right after construction; TaskHandle held by IScheduler lifecycle (auto-cancel on Core disable / plugin unload — Combat's subscriptions list is Subscription-typed and incompatible with TaskHandle; separate list out of scope). **§C — Konsist Rule 57** (ADR-0137 deferred guard finally lands): text-scan PaperDamageGateway under *.adapter.paper.* MUST contain BOTH `appliedThisTick` (Path 1 vs Path 2 marker) AND `isDead` (dead-target short-circuit) tokens. Two separate `assertThat(file.text).contains(...)` checks within same @Test with targeted fail messages citing ADR-0137 §rationale. Born already-green. **§D — Registration**: priority 100 in `AsteraCombatPlugin.onEnable` between InMemoryDamageGateway priority 0 and existing services.get<IDamageGateway>() lookup. Wave K combat-bonus chain now runs through Player.damage end-to-end in Phase 1 Paper deployments. **Konsist 56 → 57** (Rule 57 new). **No DB migration**. **Path 2 (vanilla deaths) NOT in this wave** — Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath consumes appliedThisTick marker to distinguish Path 1 (skip) from Path 2 (synthesize DamageSource.Environment.<cause> + publish PlayerKilled killer=null). Phase 1 PoC acceptable: vanilla deaths produce visible Bukkit message + drops; only statistical attribution is the loss. **Tests**: no PaperDamageGateway unit test (no MockBukkit dep); InMemoryDamageGateway domain coverage + Konsist Rule 57 guards. 57 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0146 → Accepted. **Out of scope**: Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter Path 2 handler; EntityDamageEvent Bukkit-to-Astera translation (mob → player damage); IPlayerHealthGateway consult-first for armor-based last-stand (Phase 2+ Annihilation vision Wave 7c). **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-3-paper-damage`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-2 5paper-sweep-health (2026-05-31): **PaperPlayerHealthGateway — Wave 6a-2** (ADR-0145 Accepted, §Round 19). Second Wave 6a sub-wave; ships HP path Bukkit writeback. **§A — Impl** (`plugin/core/.../adapter/paper/physiology/`, new dir): constructor `Server` + `IScheduler` + `IDomainEventBus`. 7 IPlayerHealthGateway methods (setMaxHealth → Attribute.MAX_HEALTH.baseValue + auto-clamp player.health if exceeds new max; setHealth → player.health = clamped + PlayerHealthChanged publish only on value change; heal/damage → read player.health + setHealth; setRegenRate → runRepeatingTicks(1L,1L) calling heal(perTick, Regen) where perTick = hpPerSecond/20.0 + optional expiry runLaterTicks(ttlTicks) calling clearRegen + PlayerRegenRateChanged; clearRegen → cancel handles + publish; getCurrentHealth → player.health if online else null; getMaxHealth → attribute.value if online else null). Paper 1.21+ Attribute enum: MAX_HEALTH no GENERIC_ prefix (matches PaperItemMaterializer:215-222). **§B — Offline semantics**: every mutation checks server.getPlayer(uuid); null = `BackendUnavailable("player offline")` per ADR-0080 §HealthMutationError. Read-side returns null per contract (= vanilla default). **§C — Threading**: KDoc-load-bearing requirement that Bukkit health/attribute writes run on main thread; regen scheduler block via IScheduler dispatches on main; off-main callers MUST route through scheduler.runLater(Duration.ZERO, ...). **§D — Registration** at priority 100 in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` right after InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway priority 0; InMemory stays for unit tests / non-Paper. **§E — No Konsist guard this wave** (deferred): unlike ADR-0144 PaperKnockbackGateway which lands Konsist Rule 56 for resistance contract, HP gateway has no equivalent silent-drop pattern requiring token scan; contract surface is IPlayerHealthGateway interface (compile-checked). **Konsist unchanged** (56 → 56). **No DB migration**. **No domain change** (regen scheduling shape mirrors InMemory exactly so HUD / achievement / analytics subscribers impl-agnostic; InMemoryPlayerHealthGatewayTest regen scenarios remain authoritative domain coverage). **Tests**: no PaperPlayerHealthGateway unit test (same constraint as ADR-0144: no MockBukkit dep); 56 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0145 → Accepted. **Out of scope**: IAttributeContainer consult-first integration (Phase 2+ when armor max-health use case is driver); PlayerQuitServer subscriber for proactive clearRegen on logout (Phase 2 cleanup polish). **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-2-paper-health`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave 6a-1 5paper-sweep-knockback (2026-05-31): **PaperKnockbackGateway — first Wave 6a sub-wave Paper adapter** (ADR-0144 Accepted, §Round 19 Wave 6 Paper sweep begins). First concrete Paper adapter ship after Round 17/18 pre-Paper closure. Picked knockback as first sub-wave because: (1) smallest viable scope ~80 lines; (2) lands the ADR-0136 §Konsist guard deferred "to Wave 6a alongside actual adapter"; (3) no cross-cutting deps (independent of death-cycle ADR-0137, BukkitEventAdapter, other Paper gateways); (4) clean synchronous main-thread `Player.setVelocity` API; (5) demonstrates Wave 6 workflow (Paper adapter + priority 100 overlay + Konsist guard + ADR). **§A — PaperKnockbackGateway impl** (`plugin/combat/src/main/kotlin/.../adapter/paper/`, new dir): constructor takes `org.bukkit.Server`; `applyImpulse(target, impulse)` flow: get Bukkit Player → offline = no-op DEBUG log; zero-impulse short-circuit (avoid Bukkit setVelocity(0,0,0) "stop in place" semantics); read `player.getAttribute(Attribute.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE)?.value` (Paper 1.21+ no GENERIC_ prefix matching PaperItemMaterializer:215-222 convention) clamped 0..1; full immunity (resistance == 1.0) = no setVelocity DEBUG log; otherwise `player.velocity = Vector(impulse.x * scale, y * scale, z * scale)`. Per ADR-0136 §A contract: Bukkit aggregates equipped-item ItemAttributes.knockbackResistance (set via PaperItemMaterializer:220 AttributeModifier) into player's effective attribute, so this gateway gets authoritative Phase 1 source for free. **§B — Registration**: AsteraCombatPlugin.onEnable inserts `services.register<IKnockbackGateway>(PaperKnockbackGateway(server), priority = 100)` right before the existing `services.get<IKnockbackGateway>() ?: NoOpKnockbackGateway()` lookup. Priority overlay convention: Paper at 100 overrides NoOp at 0; NoOp remains for unit tests / non-Paper deployments (exempt from resistance contract per ADR-0136 §Exemption). **§C — Konsist Rule 56** (ADR-0136 deferred guard finally landing): text-scan `PaperKnockbackGateway` source under `*.adapter.paper.*` MUST contain `KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE` token. Born already-green; forces future refactors that remove resistance read to fail CI — exact silent-drop pattern Wave S / ADR-0136 was designed to prevent. Pre-Wave-6a-1 deployments lack file → rule trivially passes (no scope match). **Konsist 55 → 56** (Rule 56 new). **No DB migration**. **No domain change** (pure adapter ship). **Tests**: no PaperKnockbackGateway unit test (Bukkit Player hard to mock without MockBukkit dep not shipped today; Konsist Rule 56 token guard + FakeKnockbackGateway domain coverage adequate for Phase 1 PoC; integration test ships when Paper test harness lands as separate ADR). 56 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0144 → Accepted. **Out of scope** (subsequent Wave 6a sub-waves): Wave 6a-2 PaperPlayerHealthGateway; Wave 6a-3 PaperDamageGateway + Konsist Rule 57 per ADR-0137; Wave 6a-4 PaperPlayerSpeedGateway/PaperPlayerFoodGateway; Wave 6a-5 PaperStatusEffectAdapter; Wave 6a-6 BukkitEventAdapter (15 ADR-0134 allowlist events + death-event hijack + PlayerInteractEvent skill cast); continuing through Wave 6f per IMPLEMENTATION-ROADMAP. **Single commit on branch `wave-6a-1-paper-knockback`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave AF 5operator-observability (2026-05-31): **Operator observability — k8s probes via /healthz + /readyz** (ADR-0142 Accepted, §Round 18). Closes 1 of 5 audit γ claims (CRITICAL Phase 1.5 multi-server rollout blocker); documents 4 FALSE alarms. **Phase A FALSE alarms verified**: (1) "only 1 Micrometer gauge active" — AsteraCorePlugin:1541-1556 wires MicrometerMetricsRegistry + JvmMemoryMetrics + JvmGcMetrics + ProcessorMetrics + HttpMetricsServer + MatchQueueMetric all active; (2) "metrics endpoint no scrape" — /metrics served + port 9092 in manifest; (3) "structured logging absent" — Phase 2 multi-server scope, analytics covers Phase 1 correlation via event UUID; (4) "graceful shutdown drain untested" — Wave 5w IServerLifecycleListener shipped; e2e test out-of-scope (k8s fixture). **TRUE gap (§A+B+C)**: deploy/minecraft/game/game-deployment.yaml had ZERO livenessProbe/readinessProbe blocks — rolling restart silently strands players. The ironic part: IServerHealthCheck (Wave 5t / ADR-0086) explicitly documented /healthz + /readyz mapping in KDoc ("Kubernetes mapping (Wave _ops / Phase 1.5)") — domain contract complete; only HTTP endpoint binding + k8s manifest probe were missing. **§A — HttpMetricsServer extended**: optional `healthCheck: IServerHealthCheck?` constructor param; start() registers 2 new contexts /healthz (200 if `snapshot().overall == Healthy`, else 503) + /readyz (200 if `overall != Down`, Healthy ∪ Degraded); body format `<state>: <individuals>\n`; null-safe fallback "no health check registered" for unit tests / pre-Wave-5t paths. **§B — AsteraCorePlugin wiring**: line 1548 updated to pass already-instantiated healthCheck (Wave 5t / ADR-0086 line 1393) into HttpMetricsServer constructor; zero new state. **§C — k8s manifest probes**: livenessProbe (path /healthz, port metrics 9092, initialDelay 60s, period 30s, timeout 5s, failureThreshold 3 → worst-case 90s before restart) + readinessProbe (path /readyz, initialDelay 30s, period 10s, timeout 5s, failureThreshold 3 → worst-case 30s before drain). Conservative thresholds calibrated for Astera 7-plugin cold-boot (~30-60s). **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No DB migration**. **No code change outside HttpMetricsServer + 1 line in AsteraCorePlugin + manifest yaml** (smallest possible surface for the CRITICAL gap). **Tests**: existing 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0142 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred** (audit γ items not addressed): 5-10 high-value counters (Phase 2 per-feature incremental); structured logging MDC (Phase 2 multi-server); per-tick budget monitoring (Wave 6a Paper-adapter scheduler instrumentation). Per-feature HealthIndicator registration grows organically as each plugin discovers a meaningful health signal. **Single commit on branch `wave-5operator-observability`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave AG 5operator-data-integrity (2026-05-31): **Operator data integrity — slow-query audit retention** (ADR-0143 Accepted, §Round 18). Closes 1 of 4 audit γ claims (TRUE gap); documents 3 FALSE alarms. **Phase A FALSE alarms**: (1) IServerBackupGateway "fake success" — actually per-player export/import gateway (Wave 5t/ADR-0086); server-wide is intentionally deferred Wave 5w restic; (2) Reload UX "no feedback" — IReloadableContentsRegistry.reload returns Result<LoadReport(loaded:Int, failed:Int), ReloadError>; (3) IErrorReporter "7 sites missing" — 5 callers verified, audit counting drift not gap. **TRUE gap (§A+B)**: ISlowQueryAuditRepository previously had no archival method; V23 slow_query_audit table grew unbounded multi-GB over months, DBA ops would silently degrade. **§A — deleteOlderThan(cutoff: Instant): Int method** added to ISlowQueryAuditRepository interface; InMemory impl iterates ConcurrentLinkedDeque + removes records with capturedAt.isBefore(cutoff); Postgres impl single SQL DELETE WHERE capturedAt less cutoff via Exposed deleteWhere (uses existing V23 index for O(rows-to-delete) cost). **§B — SlowQueryRetentionScheduler** (`plugin/core/.../application/service/observability/`): periodic task via IScheduler.runRepeating(initialDelay, period); defaults 30-day retention / 7-day sweep / 1-hour initial delay; logs purged-count at INFO when > 0; constructor params allow operator override. Wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` right after slowQueryRepo registration. **§C — Tests**: SlowQueryRetentionSchedulerTest seeds 5 old (40+ days) + 3 fresh (1 day) records; sweep() purges 5 + preserves 3; second test verifies 365-day retention preserves everything. **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No DB migration** (V23 table unchanged; new DELETE uses existing index). **Tests**: 1 new test class (2 tests) + all 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0143 → Accepted. **Out of scope**: server-wide backup `IServerBackupOrchestrator` (Wave 5w restic separate ADR); similar retention for other audit tables (audit log, replay capture — future follow-up using this scheduler pattern). **Single commit on branch `wave-5operator-data-integrity`; merged to main per user delegation.**

(§D) Match Pause coordination = TRUE GAP — no MatchPaused/Resumed events, no ISchedulerCoordinator port, no per-mode integration. **Phase 2 deferral rationale**: Match Pause requires new MatchStateMachine state + 2 domain events + ISchedulerCoordinator port + per-mode pause-aware integration across 8 game modes + anomaly/generator/respawn schedulers + match-paused HUD overlay + admin commands + Konsist guard rule. ADR-0141 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Phase 2**: Match Pause full impl (1-2 waves, 8-15h). **Single commit on branch `wave-5match-enforcement`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave AB 5lobby-ux-complete (2026-05-31): **Lobby UX — chat display + tab list population** (ADR-0138 Accepted, §Round 18). Closes 2 verified Phase 1 PoC UX blockers. **Phase A grep verified 3 audit α claims FALSE**: (a) "Achievement unlock celebration missing" — `NotifyOnAchievementUnlockedListener` exists/wired at `AsteraStatsPlugin.kt:357-364` (chat + HudLayout.Subtitle); (b) "Settings handlers no-op" — `LobbySettingsLocale/Volume/Controls IntentHandler.kt` all implement actual side-effects (use cases + repository writes); (c) "Reconnect countdown wiring unclear" — wired at `AsteraMatchPlugin.kt:992`. **True remaining gaps**: ChatMessageSent had 2 subscribers (audit + replay) — no UX display; ITabList registered priority 0 but no listener populated entries on join/quit. **§A — ChatDisplayListener** (`plugin/core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/chat/`, new dir): subscribes `ChatMessageSent`; routes Global → `IPlayerSessionRepository.all()` recipients (sender included); Private → single recipient; Team/Party → WARN+skip (multi-plugin port dependencies; deferred to follow-up wave in plugin/match for Team via IMatchTeamLookup and a sibling wave for Party). Sender display name via `IPlayerProfileService.find(sender)?.displayName` (fallback 8-char UUID prefix). Format `<senderName> {content}`. Per-recipient sendMessage failure logs WARN + continues (best-effort broadcast). **§B — UpdateTabListOnPlayerJoinedListener** (`plugin/core/.../application/eventbus/listeners/hud/`): subscribes both `PlayerJoinedServer` and `PlayerQuitServer`; reads `sessions.all()`, resolves each playerId to displayName via profileService, calls `ITabList.setPlayerEntries(viewerId, rosterEntries)` for every online player (O(N²) per join — bounded by Phase 1 PoC ~100 cap). `TabListEntry.pingMs = 0` Phase 1 (no IPlayerPingProvider; Wave 6b PaperPingProvider TBD). **§C — Wiring**: both listeners in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` right after HintOnPlayerJoinedListener (ADR-0139) block; reuses already-instantiated playerGateway / sessionRepo / profileService / tabList / eventBus. **§D — Tests**: ChatDisplayListenerTest (4 tests: Global → all online; Private → single; Team/Party skip; missing profile UUID fallback) + UpdateTabListOnPlayerJoinedListenerTest (3 tests: PlayerJoinedServer refresh; PlayerQuitServer refresh; missing profile UUID fallback). **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No DB migration**. **No Paper dependency** — pure Core-port wiring; Wave 6b PaperPlayerGateway / PaperTabListAdapter carries payloads through automatically. **Tests**: 2 new test classes (7 tests total) + all 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0138 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred**: Team chat routing (follow-up wave in plugin/match via IMatchTeamLookup); Party chat routing (sibling wave in plugin/core via IPartyRepository); IPlayerPingProvider for tab list ping column (Wave 6b PaperPingProvider); PaperChatRenderer for MiniMessage + Bukkit display name (Wave 6b). **Single commit on branch `wave-5lobby-ux-complete`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave AC 5onboarding-flow (2026-05-31): **Onboarding rotating tip on PlayerJoinedServer** (ADR-0139 Accepted, §Round 18). Closes ongoing-player discovery gap surfaced by Round 18 audit α. **Phase A verified** audit's "first-time profile creation flow missing" claim was FALSE alarm — `ShowWelcomeDialogOnFirstTimeProfileCreatedListener` + `StartTutorialOnFirstTimeProfileCreatedListener` already exist and wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable:1794+1802`. True remaining gap: every-login rotating tip surface (3 existing `PlayerJoinedServer` listeners only cover inventory restore / first-time profile creation kickoff / population HUD). **§A** new `HintOnPlayerJoinedListener` (constructor: `IPlayerGateway` + `IMessageRenderer`, single `handle()` method): picks tip via `AtomicInteger.getAndIncrement() mod HINT_KEYS.size` (deterministic round-robin per CLAUDE.md §Coding posture forbidding Math.random in domain), renders via `IMessageRenderer.render(playerId, key)` for per-player locale, sends via `IPlayerGateway.sendMessage(playerId, rendered)`, swallows `RuntimeException` with WARN log (tip best-effort UX not gameplay-critical). 6-tip catalogue covers lobby features Round 18 audit α identified as "discoverable but unsignaled": menu navigation, hotbar bind, party invite, achievement tracking, item refinement, survival islands. Sibling pattern to existing `NotifyOnRecipeCraftedListener` (Wave H 5content-depth / ADR-0125). **§B** wired in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` after population HUD listeners (line ~2020); fires on every `PlayerJoinedServer` (every login, even veterans) — distinct from `FirstTimeProfileCreated` listeners which fire only once-per-account. **§C** 6 new i18n keys `astera.tip.1` … `astera.tip.6` (en + ja parity verified by existing Konsist rule `i18n keys must match across en and ja language files`); covers menu / hotbar / party / achievement / refinement / survival islands. **§D** unit test (`HintOnPlayerJoinedListenerTest`) verifies (a) 8 sequential `handle()` calls produce `1→2→3→4→5→6→1→2` round-robin sequence and (b) handle swallows renderer exceptions (RecordingPlayerGateway.sent empty). **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No DB migration**. **No domain change** (pure additive listener + i18n). **Tests**: 1 new test class (2 tests) + all 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0139 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred**: per-player tip counter persistence (Phase 2+); IHintRegistry + content/tips/ YAML promotion (Phase 2 if live-ops cadence demands); player opt-out preference (Phase 2). **Single commit on branch `wave-5onboarding-flow`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave T 5damage-deathcycle-adr (2026-05-31): **Damage / death-cycle contract — IDamageGateway vs Bukkit PlayerDeathEvent** (ADR-0137 Accepted, §Round 17). **Design-only wave** (no code change). Closes an unresolved design question Round 17 HP/damage Explore agent flagged: who fires `PlayerKilled` when HP→0 — Astera domain (via `ApplyDamageUseCase.execute()` reacting to `DamageOutcome.Killed` at line 196-205) or Wave 6a `BukkitEventAdapter` (intercepting Bukkit `PlayerDeathEvent`)? Risk: without explicit contract, Wave 6a `PaperDamageGateway` calling `Damageable.damage()` would trigger Bukkit's synchronous `PlayerDeathEvent`, which if also intercepted + re-routed through `ApplyDamageUseCase` causes reentrancy + double-fire of `PlayerKilled`, doubling assist attribution payouts and polluting stats (Wave K 5integration-wire / ADR-0128 `AssistAttributionUseCase` subscribes to `PlayerKilled`). **Decision — 2-path contract**: **Path 1 (Astera combat death)** = ApplyDamageUseCase publishes PlayerKilled when gateway returns `DamageOutcome.Killed`; PaperDamageGateway maintains `appliedThisTick: MutableSet<PlayerId>` cleared by 1-tick scheduler task; `BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath` MUST NOT publish from PlayerDeathEvent if target was in `appliedThisTick` (only suppress vanilla side-effects like deathMessage + drops per game-mode policy). **Path 2 (vanilla/non-combat death — suicide/void/fall/`/kill`)** = `BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath` checks `appliedThisTick`; if target absent, synthesize `DamageSource.Environment.<cause>` from `EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause`, publish `PlayerHealthChanged` via `IPlayerHealthGateway.damage()`, publish `PlayerKilled(killer = null, source = synthesized)`. ADR includes code sketches for both `PaperDamageGateway.apply()` (5-step: getPlayer / isDead → TargetAlreadyDead / noDamageTicks > 0 → Immune / appliedThisTick.add + Player.damage / read post-HP → HpReduced or Killed) and `BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath` (Path 1 vs Path 2 branching). **Konsist Rule 56 deferred to Wave 6a** (text-scan: `*.adapter.paper.*` `PaperDamageGateway` source MUST contain `appliedThisTick` + `isDead` tokens) — rule lands alongside the actual adapter so it's born already-green; today there's no class to assert against. **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No code change** (design ADR only). **Tests**: existing 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green (unchanged). `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0137 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred to Wave 6a**: PaperDamageGateway impl + appliedThisTick scheduler task + BukkitEventAdapter.onPlayerDeath handler + Konsist Rule 56 guard + 3-case integration test suite (single-fire combat / single-fire vanilla / no cross-fire). **Single commit on branch `wave-5damage-deathcycle-adr`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave S 5knockback-resistance (2026-05-31): **Knockback resistance integration — gateway-boundary contract** (ADR-0136 Accepted, §Round 17). Closes a subtle silent feature drop pre-Wave-6a: `ItemAttributes.knockbackResistance` is applied via Bukkit's vanilla path (`PaperItemMaterializer:220` sets `Attribute.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE` AttributeModifier on equipped items, Bukkit aggregates per-equipment), but Astera's **custom knockback path bypasses vanilla physics entirely** (`KnockbackModifier` computes Vec3 impulse → `IKnockbackGateway.applyImpulse()` → Wave 6a `PaperKnockbackGateway` calls `Player.setVelocity()` directly, which does NOT consult `KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE`). For Phase 1 invisible (NoOpKnockbackGateway is log-only stub), but the moment Wave 6a PaperKnockbackGateway ships without explicit resistance-consultation contract, knockback resistance becomes silently ignored for Astera-fired impulses. **§A — gateway-boundary contract**: KDoc-load-bearing on `IKnockbackGateway.applyImpulse(...)` requires implementations to consult target's effective resistance (Bukkit `Attribute.KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE` Phase 1 source / future Astera `RESIST_KNOCKBACK` container source) and scale impulse via `impulse * (1.0 - resistance.coerceIn(0.0, 1.0))` before applying. **§B — forward-compat `RESIST_KNOCKBACK` AttributeKey** added to `Attributes.kt` (parallels Wave 5e RESIST_PHYSICAL/etc. pattern; reserved for future feature waves that may sync ItemAttributes → container directly for non-Bukkit-backed deployments). **§C — `NoOpKnockbackGateway` exemption**: KDoc explicitly documents that NoOp is exempt from the resistance contract (no physical write → reading attributes is wasted work in unit-test scope). **No new domain modifier**: resistance lives at adapter (gateway) layer, NOT domain pipeline layer (rationale in ADR-0136 Alternatives §Alt A — per-target physical state aggregation belongs at I/O boundary, not in DamageRule chain). **No Konsist rule yet**: text-scan guard for "PaperKnockbackGateway source MUST contain KNOCKBACK_RESISTANCE token" deferred to Wave 6a (lands alongside the actual Paper adapter so the rule is born already-green). **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55). **No DB migration**. **No domain behavior change** (Phase 1 NoOp path identical; future Paper path receives explicit contract). **Tests**: existing 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0136 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred**: Wave 6a `PaperKnockbackGateway` resistance impl + Konsist Rule 56 guard (lands together); future Astera-native `ApplyKnockbackResistanceModifiers` equip-sync use case (only needed for non-Bukkit deployments). **Single commit on branch `wave-5knockback-resistance`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave R 5proxy-impl-wire (2026-05-31): **Proxy InMemory backends — register baseline in AsteraCorePlugin** (ADR-0135 Accepted, §Round 17). Closes critical silent-drop discovered by Round 17 audit (3 Explore agents verifying Velocity / knockback / HP / damage abstractions pre-Wave 6). **§A** `InMemoryProxyMessenger` (Wave 5r / ADR-0084) and `InMemoryServerTransferGateway` (same Wave 5r delivery) were fully implemented in `plugin/core/.../application/service/` but **never registered** in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` — `services.get<IProxyMessenger>()` and `services.get<IServerTransferGateway>()` returned null on live server, meaning the Wave O 5cross-server-wire (ADR-0132) log-only subscribers (PlayerArrivedOnServer / PlayerTransferRequested / ProxyHeartbeat) had no live publisher to fire them. Same family as Wave Q 5port-fill (ADR-0133 §A) PostgresBlockDurabilityRepository registration-forgotten bug. **§B** wiring in `AsteraCorePlugin.onEnable` after the existing `InMemoryPermissionService` registration (because `InMemoryServerTransferGateway` constructor depends on `IPermissionService`): 2 new `services.register<I>(impl, priority = 0)` blocks. `localServer = ServerName("astera-local")` Phase 1 default (Wave 6b VelocityProxyMessenger will read actual server name from Velocity proxy config). Matches existing AsteraCorePlugin pattern (call register without holding Registration return value; Core has no per-plugin lifecycle teardown). **Known limitation documented in ADR-0135**: `InMemoryServerTransferGateway.init { }` block subscribes to `PlayerJoinedServer` / `MatchEnded` without capturing Subscription — separate pre-existing bug that Konsist Rule ADR-0123 doesn't catch (scopes to `Astera*Plugin.kt` only). Out of scope for Wave R; deferred to Round 18+ or Wave 6b Velocity adapter design. **Konsist unchanged** (55 → 55): no new architectural rule (the bug class — port with InMemory impl but no registration — is on the audit β residual candidate list for Round 18+ Konsist hardening). **No DB migration**. **No domain impact** (no code change, only wiring). **Tests**: existing 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0135 → Accepted. **Out of scope**: Velocity adapter classes themselves (Wave 6b); `InMemoryServerTransferGateway` init-block subscribe leak refactor (Round 18+); Konsist rule for port-has-InMemory-but-no-registration silent-drop class (Round 18+). **Single commit on branch `wave-5proxy-impl-wire`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave P 5konsist-rule1 (2026-05-31): **Konsist Rule 55 — DomainEvent publisher-exists rule (Wave K prevention)** (ADR-0134 Accepted, §Round 16). Adds 1 new Konsist architecture rule + 29-entry allowlist baseline; **no RTM game-feature rows flipped** (infrastructure-only commit). **§A — Rule 55: publisher-exists check** (`tools/architecture-test/.../ArchitectureTest.kt` new `@Test fun`): scans every concrete class implementing `DomainEvent` in `plugin/*/src/main/kotlin/**`, verifies a constructor call `XxxEvent(` exists in at least one main-source file OUTSIDE the event's defining file. Subscriber side intentionally NOT checked (Astera's `InMemoryScheduledAnnouncementSystem` holds wildcard `subscribe(DomainEvent::class)` that polymorphically catches every subtype per `InMemoryDomainEventBus.publish` `entry.type.isInstance(event)` — per-type subscriber check would be vacuously true and require 149-entry baseline; deferred per ADR-0131 §Alt A → future ADR when wildcard restricted). Catches the Wave K-style forget-to-publish bug: declaring `data class XxxEvent : DomainEvent` and forgetting to publish from any use case is now a CI failure. **§B — 29-entry allowlist baseline** (`tools/architecture-test/src/test/resources/konsist-allowlist/deferred-events.txt`): categorized by deferral reason with section headers + per-entry inline Bukkit/Velocity-event mapping comments — **15 Wave 6a Bukkit-adapter** (PlayerJoinedServer→PlayerJoinEvent / PlayerQuitServer→PlayerQuitEvent / PlayerInteractedBlock→PlayerInteractEvent / PlayerTeleported→PlayerTeleportEvent / ChunkLoaded→ChunkLoadEvent / ChunkUnloaded→ChunkUnloadEvent / WorldUnloading→WorldUnloadEvent / WeatherChanged→WeatherChangeEvent / TimeChanged→world.fullTime polling / MobKilled→EntityDeathEvent / EntityEnteredRegion→PlayerMoveEvent custom region tracker / EntityLeftRegion / EntityPickedUpItem→EntityPickupItemEvent / EnvironmentDamageApplied→EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause); **2 Wave 6b Velocity-adapter** (PlayerArrivedOnServer→VelocityProxyMessenger inbound astera.transfer channel / ProxyHeartbeat→Velocity periodic peer roster broadcast; subscriber-side already wired by Wave O ADR-0132); **12 Phase 2+ feature waves** (3 Mining + 2 NPC + 3 Tutorial lifecycle + 2 Portal + 2 PvE reward + 1 RoleExpired). Each Wave 6a/6b/Phase 2 wave will trim entries as publishers ship — provides forward-looking "remaining work" ledger that survives release planning. **Konsist rule count: 54 → 55** (Rule 55 new; existing rules unchanged). **No RTM row flips** (architecture QA only). **No DB migration**. **No domain impact** (no code change, only rule + allowlist + ADR). **Sanity-check verified**: temporarily removing PlayerJoinedServer from allowlist surfaces it as the single violator → Rule 55 mechanically catches missing-publisher state. **Tests**: Rule 55 itself is a test; all 55 architecture tests + all 7 plugin unit tests + Testcontainers ITs 全 green. `./gradlew :tools:architecture-test:test` + `./gradlew check` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. ADR-0134 → Accepted. **Out of scope deferred**: subscriber-side check (Round 17+ once wildcard analytics consumer restricted); audit β remaining rules (DTO↔Domain field sync / Port↔impl pairing / YAML schema drift — Round 17+ or Wave 6 close per Konsist hardening expansion proposal). **Single commit on branch `wave-5konsist-rule1`; merged to main per user delegation.**

- Wave Q 5port-fill (2026-05-31): **Pre-Paper port fill — IDamageGateway InMemory + PostgresBlockDurabilityRepository registration** (ADR-0133 Accepted, §Round 16). Closes 2 pre-Paper-sweep gaps audit γ Round 16 verified. **§A — PostgresBlockDurabilityRepository registration**: class shipped at `plugin/persistence/.../repository/PostgresBlockDurabilityRepository.kt` (Wave 5x / ADR-0090 / V20 migration), KDoc explicitly states "Registered by AsteraPersistencePlugin at priority=100" but `AsteraPersistencePlugin.onEnable` imported the class without ever calling `services.register<IBlockDurabilityRepository>(...)` — block-durability state ran as in-memory-only despite Postgres plugin being loaded, pod restart silently lost every block durability tick, V20 migration permanently unused. With this stub the use case wires, the bonus chain runs end-to-end through `ApplyDamageUseCase` in Phase 1, every damage attempt returns `TargetOffline` (semantically correct: no game-world hit box). Wave 6a `PaperDamageGateway` will overlay at priority 100 (standard Astera priority-overlay convention; consistent with `InMemoryPlayerHealthGateway` / `InMemoryPlayerSpeedGateway` pattern). **Konsist unchanged** (54 → 54): no new architectural rule. **No DB migration** (V20 already applied; just wiring it). **No domain impact** (gateway stub returns documented failure variant; persistence fix uses existing repo class). **Tests**: 1 new `InMemoryDamageGatewayTest` (verifies `apply` returns `Failure(TargetOffline)` for representative call); existing damage pipeline tests use FakeDamageGateway directly so are unaffected. `./gradlew check` (full suite) BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
